# M2a — Editor Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Turn the read-only page view into an editor: one Markdown model (unified/remark + Obsidian extensions) driving CodeMirror 6 Live Preview / Source / Split, a sanitized rendered pane with embeds, compare-and-swap autosave with 3-way merge and conflict copies, the page header with breadcrumb, the M1b polish items, and a zero-network end-to-end test.

**Architecture:** The frontend owns the Markdown model (`src/markdown/`): positions are UTF-16 offsets shared by CodeMirror decorations, the rendered pane (`data-source-*` ranges) and the Rust indexer (parity fixtures). Pages are edited as `PageDoc`s (`src/features/editor/pageDoc.ts`) that save through new async Tauri commands (`page_read`/`page_write` with a content hash) and merge external changes; vault files reach `<img>/<audio>` through a custom `liber-asset:` protocol that resolves through the open session only.

**Tech Stack:** Tauri 2 (Rust), React 19, TypeScript 6, zustand 5, CodeMirror 6, unified/remark/mdast/hast, node-diff3, Vitest 5 + jsdom + Testing Library + axe-core, Playwright (Chromium), pnpm 12 workspace, cargo.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-m2a-editor-design.md` (read it with this plan; section numbers below refer to it). Hand-off of the previous milestone: `docs/handoff/2026-10-12-m1b-app-integration-handoff.md` (§6 recipes for new commands, §10 the polish items).

**How this plan was produced:** every task was executed in a clean clone of `main` at `6d05dd5`, in order, with each RED observed and each GREEN passing the full check set. Every code block below is the exact content of that replay. Diffs are unified diffs against the state left by the previous task (`git apply` accepts them; applying by hand works too).

## Global Constraints

- Work from repo root `notes-in-my-way/` on a branch `feat/m2a-editor` cut from `main` (`6d05dd5`).
- Every dependency GPL-3.0-compatible (MIT / Apache-2.0 / MPL-2.0 / ISC / BSD); no network code (NFR-04, P1). `cargo deny check` must stay clean.
- Exact versions (resolved through pnpm 12.11.2's release-age gate, verified 2026-10-12): `unified` 11.0.5, `remark-parse` 11.0.0, `remark-gfm` 4.0.1, `remark-frontmatter` 5.0.0, `mdast-util-directive` 3.1.1, `micromark-extension-directive` 4.0.0, `micromark-util-decode-string` 2.0.1, `yaml` 2.9.1, `mdast-util-to-hast` 13.2.1, `hast-util-raw` 9.1.0, `hast-util-sanitize` 5.0.2, `github-slugger` 2.0.0, `node-diff3` 3.2.1, `@codemirror/state` 6.7.6, `@codemirror/view` 6.43.14, `@codemirror/commands` 6.11.1, `@codemirror/language` 6.13.1, `@codemirror/lang-markdown` 6.5.2, `@lezer/highlight` 1.2.5; dev: `@types/unist` 3.0.3, `@types/mdast` 4.0.4, `@types/hast` 3.0.5, `@playwright/test` 1.64.0 (Chromium headless shell 156); Rust: `tauri-plugin-opener` 2.7.1, `percent-encoding` 2.3.2 (already a workspace dependency).
- The UI reaches the backend only through `apps/desktop/src/ipc/` (eslint enforces it); tests use `src/ipc/testing.ts`.
- Every new Tauri command is `async` and runs its work through `vault_commands::blocking` (the guard test `nfr_ui_thread_never_runs_disk_or_index_commands` reads `editor_commands.rs` too), is listed in `build.rs`, allowed in `capabilities/main.json`, and has an ACL test in `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/commands.rs`: allowed from `main`, "not allowed" from another window. The same holds for every plugin/core command the UI newly calls (`plugin:window|destroy`, `plugin:event|listen` on `tauri://close-requested`).
- The webview gets no `opener:*` permission; external opening only through `url_open` (http/https/mailto) and `file_open_external` (vault files).
- Production CSP (in `tauri.conf.json`) after this milestone: `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' liber-asset: http://liber-asset.localhost data: blob:; media-src 'self' liber-asset: http://liber-asset.localhost blob:; font-src 'self'; connect-src ipc: http://ipc.localhost; frame-src 'none'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'`.
- Build target includes Safari 15 (WKWebView on macOS 12): no regex lookbehind (a lint rule enforces it from Task 8).
- All positions are UTF-16 offsets (JS string indices = CodeMirror positions).
- Every user-visible string goes through i18next with `en` and `es` in parity (`pnpm check:i18n`).
- Autosave 500 ms; retry backoff 1 s → 30 s; rendered pane debounce 150 ms; Live Preview re-parse debounce 150 ms; large-note threshold 200 KB (204,800 chars); quit flush cap 2 s.
- Never set `LIBER_TRASH_TESTS=1` locally (it uses the real OS Trash). Never send synthetic keystrokes to the user's Mac; agents may launch the app for a screenshot only.
- Every GREEN step must leave these clean (run from repo root): `pnpm lint` (eslint + prettier), `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm check:i18n`, and for Rust changes `pnpm build:ui && cargo fmt --all --check && cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings && cargo test --workspace && cargo deny check`. Run every verification with `set -o pipefail` and never mask an exit code with `| grep … &&`.
- Commits: conventional commits; every message ends with a `Co-Authored-By:` trailer naming the model that wrote the code, e.g. `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (use your own model's name).

## Review Focus

Five inputs the spec implies but does not spell out, most likely to bite first; each is pinned by a test in the owning task:

1. **Switching pages while a save is in flight** — the old page's write must land on the old page and its result must never touch the new page's hash/text. Pinned in Task 5: `editorStore.test.tsx` › "review focus: switching pages mid-save saves the old page and leaves the new one alone".
2. **Undo after an external merge** — ⌘Z must revert only the user's own edit, never the text that arrived from disk. Pinned in Task 7: `Editor.test.tsx` › "review focus: undo after an external merge undoes only my edit".
3. **CRLF files (Windows-made notes)** — the editor works in LF, but a save must write CRLF back so the file isn't rewritten line by line. Pinned in Task 5: `pageDoc.test.ts` › "keeps CRLF files CRLF: the editor sees LF, the disk gets CRLF back".
4. **Astral characters and emoji before markup** (😀, 👩‍👩‍👧, CJK) — hidden markup must stay aligned (UTF-16 offsets everywhere). Pinned in Task 7: `livePreview.test.ts` › "review focus: positions are UTF-16 offsets, so astral characters keep markup aligned".
5. **Typing in a very long note** — a keystroke in a 5,000-line note must re-parse in < 50 ms (a full remark parse takes ~110 ms). Pinned in Task 7: `incremental.test.ts` › "re-parses one typed character in a 5,000-line note in under 50 ms", plus seeded fuzz tests proving the incremental model equals the full parse.

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/parse.test.ts`:

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Embed, Tag, WikiLink } from './types';
import { parsePage } from './parse';
import { findAll } from './walk';

const slice = (
  src: string,
  n: { position?: { start: { offset?: number }; end: { offset?: number } } },
) => src.slice(n.position!.start.offset!, n.position!.end.offset!);

describe('parsePage — wikilinks and embeds (M2a §2.1)', () => {
  it('parses every wikilink form with exact source positions', () => {
    const src = 'See [[Sparda]], [[Game 1#Plot]], [[rex-draft|Rex]] and [[A#B|C]].\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    const links = findAll<WikiLink>(p.tree, 'wikiLink');
    expect(links.map((l) => [l.target, l.heading, l.alias])).toEqual([
      ['Sparda', null, null],
      ['Game 1', 'Plot', null],
      ['rex-draft', null, 'Rex'],
      ['A', 'B', 'C'],
    ]);
    expect(links.map((l) => slice(src, l))).toEqual([
      '[[Sparda]]',
      '[[Game 1#Plot]]',
      '[[rex-draft|Rex]]',
      '[[A#B|C]]',
    ]);
  });

  it('parses embeds with Obsidian size syntax', () => {
    const src = '![[rex.png]] ![[rex.png|300]] ![[rex.png|300x200]] ![[Sparda#Palette]]\n';
    const embeds = findAll<Embed>(parsePage(src).tree, 'embed');
    expect(embeds.map((e) => [e.target, e.heading, e.width, e.height])).toEqual([
      ['rex.png', null, null, null],
      ['rex.png', null, 300, null],
      ['rex.png', null, 300, 200],
      ['Sparda', 'Palette', null, null],
    ]);
    expect(slice(src, embeds[1])).toBe('![[rex.png|300]]');
  });

  it('PG-05 table alias form [[target\\|alias]] works inside a table cell', () => {
    const src = '| a | b |\n|---|---|\n| x | [[A\\|shown]] |\n';
    const [l] = findAll<WikiLink>(parsePage(src).tree, 'wikiLink');
    expect([l.target, l.alias]).toEqual(['A', 'shown']);
  });

  it('ignores escaped \\[[ and anything inside code', () => {
    const src = 'Not \\[[a link]]. `[[code]]`\n\n```\n[[fenced]]\n```\n\n    [[indented]]\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    expect(findAll(p.tree, 'wikiLink')).toEqual([]);
    expect(p.links).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('keeps the surrounding text, decoded, around links', () => {
    const p = parsePage('a &amp; b \\* [[X]] c\n');
    const para = p.tree.children[0] as { children: { type: string; value?: string }[] };
    expect(para.children.map((c) => c.type)).toEqual(['text', 'wikiLink', 'text']);
    expect(para.children[0].value).toBe('a & b * ');
    expect(para.children[2].value).toBe(' c');
  });
});

describe('parsePage — links list (spec §2.2, parity with liber-markdown)', () => {
  it('lists wiki, embed, markdown and markdown_image links with decoded targets', () => {
    const src =
      '[[Sparda]] ![[rex.png|300]] [p](DMC/Sparda.md#Palette) [g](</Game Design/Game 1.md>) ' +
      '[e](../Game%20Design/Game%201.md) ![i](assets/a.png) [x](https://example.com) [h](#top)\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    expect(p.links.map((l) => [l.kind, l.target, l.heading, l.alias])).toEqual([
      ['wiki', 'Sparda', null, null],
      ['embed', 'rex.png', null, '300'],
      ['markdown', 'DMC/Sparda.md', 'Palette', null],
      ['markdown', '/Game Design/Game 1.md', null, null],
      ['markdown', '../Game Design/Game 1.md', null, null],
      ['markdown_image', 'assets/a.png', null, null],
    ]);
    for (const l of p.links) expect(l.range[0]).toBeLessThan(l.range[1]);
  });
});

describe('parsePage — frontmatter', () => {
  it('parses YAML data and lists related and cover links', () => {
    const src =
      '---\nsubtitle: Hero\ncover: assets/c.png\nrelated:\n  - "[[Sparda]]"\n  - { link: "[[Game 1]]", label: x, directed: true }\n---\n# T\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    expect(p.frontmatter?.data?.subtitle).toBe('Hero');
    expect(src.slice(...p.frontmatter!.range)).toMatch(/^---\n[\s\S]*\n---$/);
    expect(p.links.map((l) => [l.kind, l.target])).toEqual([
      ['cover', 'assets/c.png'],
      ['related', 'Sparda'],
      ['related', 'Game 1'],
    ]);
  });

  it('reports malformed YAML without failing the body', () => {
    const p = parsePage('---\ntags: [unclosed\n---\n[[target]]\n');
    expect(p.frontmatter?.data).toBeNull();
    expect(p.frontmatter?.error).toBeTruthy();
    expect(p.links.map((l) => l.target)).toEqual(['target']);
  });
});

describe('review focus: offsets', () => {
  it('positions are UTF-16 offsets that slice the source exactly, after emoji and accents', () => {
    const src = '🎨 Café ñandú → [[Ideas]] and #tag\n';
    const [l] = findAll<WikiLink>(parsePage(src).tree, 'wikiLink');
    expect(slice(src, l)).toBe('[[Ideas]]');
    const [t] = findAll<Tag>(parsePage(src).tree, 'tag');
    expect(slice(src, t)).toBe('#tag');
  });

  it('CRLF files keep exact positions', () => {
    const src = '# Title\r\n\r\nText [[A]]\r\nmore [[B|b]]\r\n';
    const links = findAll<WikiLink>(parsePage(src).tree, 'wikiLink');
    expect(links.map((l) => slice(src, l))).toEqual(['[[A]]', '[[B|b]]']);
  });
});
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the model**

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/frontmatter.ts`:

```ts
import type { Root, Yaml } from 'mdast';
import { parse as parseYaml } from 'yaml';
import type { Frontmatter, LinkRef } from './types';
import { parseWikiInner } from './wikilink';

/** Reads the YAML frontmatter node (remark-frontmatter) if the page has one. */
export function readFrontmatter(tree: Root): Frontmatter | null {
  const node = tree.children[0];
  if (!node || node.type !== 'yaml') return null;
  const yaml = node as Yaml;
  const range: [number, number] = [yaml.position!.start.offset!, yaml.position!.end.offset!];
  try {
    const data = parseYaml(yaml.value);
    if (data === null || data === undefined) return { range, data: {} };
    if (typeof data !== 'object' || Array.isArray(data)) {
      return { range, data: null, error: 'frontmatter is not a mapping' };
    }
    return { range, data: data as Record<string, unknown> };
  } catch (e) {
    return { range, data: null, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) };
  }
}

/** `cover:` and `related:` links (same keys `liber-markdown` reads). */
export function frontmatterLinks(fm: Frontmatter | null): LinkRef[] {
  const data = fm?.data;
  if (!fm || !data) return [];
  const out: LinkRef[] = [];
  if (typeof data.cover === 'string' && data.cover.trim()) {
    out.push({
      kind: 'cover',
      target: data.cover.trim(),
      heading: null,
      alias: null,
      range: fm.range,
    });
  }
  const related = Array.isArray(data.related) ? data.related : [];
  for (const item of related) {
    const raw =
      typeof item === 'string'
        ? item
        : item && typeof item === 'object' && typeof (item as { link?: unknown }).link === 'string'
          ? (item as { link: string }).link
          : null;
    const m = raw ? /^\s*\[\[([^\]]+)\]\]\s*$/.exec(raw) : null;
    const parts = m ? parseWikiInner(m[1]) : null;
    if (parts) out.push({ kind: 'related', ...parts, range: fm.range });
  }
  return out;
}

/** Frontmatter `tags:` as a list or a comma/space separated string; `#` stripped. */
export function frontmatterTags(fm: Frontmatter | null): string[] {
  const tags = fm?.data?.tags;
  const list = Array.isArray(tags)
    ? tags.filter((t): t is string => typeof t === 'string')
    : typeof tags === 'string'
      ? tags.split(/[,\s]+/)
      : [];
  return list.map((t) => t.trim().replace(/^#/, '')).filter(Boolean);
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/inline.ts`:

```ts
// Inline extensions found inside mdast text nodes (M2a spec §2.1): wikilinks,
// embeds, ==highlights== and #tags. The scan runs on the *source* slice of a
// text node — so escapes (`\[[`, the table form `\|`) are still visible — and
// the plain text between matches is decoded like mdast text values.
import type { PhrasingContent, Text } from 'mdast';
import { decodeString } from 'micromark-util-decode-string';
import type { locator } from './locate';
import type { Embed, Highlight, Tag, WikiLink } from './types';
import { parseSize, parseWikiInner } from './wikilink';

type Locator = ReturnType<typeof locator>;

const TAG_CHAR = /[\p{L}\p{N}_\-/]/u;

/** Obsidian tag at `i` (which holds `#`): returns the tag name or null. */
function tagAt(s: string, i: number, before: string): string | null {
  if (before !== '' && !/\s/.test(before)) return null;
  let j = i + 1;
  while (j < s.length) {
    const ch = String.fromCodePoint(s.codePointAt(j)!);
    if (!TAG_CHAR.test(ch)) break;
    j += ch.length;
  }
  const name = s.slice(i + 1, j).replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!name || name.startsWith('/') || /^\d+$/.test(name)) return null;
  return name;
}

/** Plain text value as mdast would have it: escapes and character references
 * decoded, continuation-line indentation dropped. */
function plainValue(raw: string): string {
  return decodeString(raw).replace(/\n[ \t]+/g, '\n');
}

/**
 * Splits one text node into text + extension nodes. `src` is the whole
 * document; `before` is the source character just before the node (for the
 * tag rule). Returns `null` when the node contains no extension.
 */
export function scanText(src: string, node: Text, loc: Locator): PhrasingContent[] | null {
  const start = node.position?.start.offset;
  const end = node.position?.end.offset;
  if (start === undefined || end === undefined) return null;
  const s = src.slice(start, end);
  if (!/\[\[|==|#/.test(s)) return null;
  const out = scan(s, start, src[start - 1] ?? '', loc);
  return out.length === 1 && out[0].type === 'text' ? null : out;
}

function scan(s: string, base: number, prevChar: string, loc: Locator): PhrasingContent[] {
  const out: PhrasingContent[] = [];
  let plainStart = 0;
  const flush = (to: number) => {
    if (to > plainStart) {
      out.push({
        type: 'text',
        value: plainValue(s.slice(plainStart, to)),
        position: loc.position(base + plainStart, base + to),
      });
    }
  };
  let i = 0;
  while (i < s.length) {
    const ch = s[i];
    const escaped = i > 0 && s[i - 1] === '\\';
    // [[wikilink]] / ![[embed]]
    if (ch === '[' && s[i + 1] === '[' && !escaped) {
      const close = s.indexOf(']]', i + 2);
      const inner = close >= 0 ? s.slice(i + 2, close) : '';
      const parts =
        close >= 0 && !inner.includes('\n') && !inner.includes('[[') ? parseWikiInner(inner) : null;
      if (parts) {
        const embed = i > 0 && s[i - 1] === '!' && !(i > 1 && s[i - 2] === '\\');
        const from = embed ? i - 1 : i;
        flush(from);
        const position = loc.position(base + from, base + close + 2);
        if (embed) {
          const size = parseSize(parts.alias);
          out.push({
            type: 'embed',
            target: parts.target,
            heading: parts.heading,
            alias: parts.alias,
            ...size,
            position,
          } satisfies Embed);
        } else {
          out.push({ type: 'wikiLink', ...parts, position } satisfies WikiLink);
        }
        i = close + 2;
        plainStart = i;
        continue;
      }
    }
    // ==highlight==
    if (ch === '=' && s[i + 1] === '=' && !escaped) {
      const close = s.indexOf('==', i + 2);
      if (close > i + 2 && !/^\s|\s$/.test(s.slice(i + 2, close))) {
        flush(i);
        const children = scan(s.slice(i + 2, close), base + i + 2, '=', loc);
        out.push({
          type: 'highlight',
          children,
          position: loc.position(base + i, base + close + 2),
        } satisfies Highlight);
        i = close + 2;
        plainStart = i;
        continue;
      }
    }
    // #tag
    if (ch === '#' && !escaped) {
      const name = tagAt(s, i, i === 0 ? prevChar : s[i - 1]);
      if (name) {
        flush(i);
        out.push({
          type: 'tag',
          name,
          position: loc.position(base + i, base + i + 1 + name.length),
        } satisfies Tag);
        i += 1 + name.length;
        plainStart = i;
        continue;
      }
    }
    i++;
  }
  flush(s.length);
  return out;
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/locate.ts`:

```ts
import type { Point, Position } from 'unist';

/** Maps UTF-16 offsets to unist points (1-based line/column). */
export function locator(src: string) {
  const starts = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) if (src.charCodeAt(i) === 10) starts.push(i + 1);
  const point = (offset: number): Point => {
    let lo = 0;
    let hi = starts.length - 1;
    while (lo < hi) {
      const mid = (lo + hi + 1) >> 1;
      if (starts[mid] <= offset) lo = mid;
      else hi = mid - 1;
    }
    return { line: lo + 1, column: offset - starts[lo] + 1, offset };
  };
  const position = (start: number, end: number): Position => ({
    start: point(start),
    end: point(end),
  });
  return { point, position };
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/parse.ts`:

```ts
// The single Markdown model (M2a spec §2): unified + remark + our extensions.
// Drives Live Preview decorations, the rendered pane and (M4) block dragging.
import type { Image, Link, Parent, Root, RootContent, Text } from 'mdast';
import remarkFrontmatter from 'remark-frontmatter';
import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
import remarkParse from 'remark-parse';
import { unified } from 'unified';
import { frontmatterLinks, frontmatterTags, readFrontmatter } from './frontmatter';
import { scanText } from './inline';
import { locator } from './locate';
import type { Embed, LinkRef, ParsedPage, Tag, WikiLink } from './types';
import { findAll, rangeOf } from './walk';

const processor = unified().use(remarkParse).use(remarkGfm).use(remarkFrontmatter, ['yaml']);

/** Parents whose text is never scanned for extensions. */
const OPAQUE = new Set(['link', 'linkReference', 'image', 'imageReference']);

function transformInline(tree: Root, src: string, loc: ReturnType<typeof locator>) {
  const walk = (parent: Parent) => {
    if (OPAQUE.has(parent.type)) return;
    const next: RootContent[] = [];
    let changed = false;
    for (const child of parent.children as RootContent[]) {
      if (child.type === 'text') {
        const parts = scanText(src, child as Text, loc);
        if (parts) {
          next.push(...parts);
          changed = true;
          continue;
        }
      } else if ('children' in child) {
        walk(child as Parent);
      }
      next.push(child);
    }
    if (changed) (parent as { children: RootContent[] }).children = next;
  };
  walk(tree);
}

const SCHEME = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i;

/** Decoded target of a Markdown link/image destination, or null if external. */
function markdownTarget(url: string): { target: string; heading: string | null } | null {
  if (!url || url.startsWith('#') || SCHEME.test(url)) return null;
  const hash = url.indexOf('#');
  const path = hash >= 0 ? url.slice(0, hash) : url;
  if (!path) return null;
  let target = path;
  try {
    target = decodeURIComponent(path);
  } catch {
    // keep the raw path when it isn't valid percent-encoding
  }
  const heading = hash >= 0 ? url.slice(hash + 1) || null : null;
  return { target, heading };
}

function collectLinks(tree: Root, fmLinks: LinkRef[]): LinkRef[] {
  const out: LinkRef[] = [...fmLinks];
  const walk = (node: RootContent | Root) => {
    switch (node.type) {
      case 'wikiLink': {
        const n = node as WikiLink;
        out.push({
          kind: 'wiki',
          target: n.target,
          heading: n.heading,
          alias: n.alias,
          range: rangeOf(n),
        });
        return;
      }
      case 'embed': {
        const n = node as Embed;
        out.push({
          kind: 'embed',
          target: n.target,
          heading: n.heading,
          alias: n.alias,
          range: rangeOf(n),
        });
        return;
      }
      case 'link':
      case 'image': {
        const t = markdownTarget((node as Link | Image).url);
        if (t) {
          out.push({
            kind: node.type === 'link' ? 'markdown' : 'markdown_image',
            ...t,
            alias: null,
            range: rangeOf(node),
          });
        }
        break;
      }
    }
    if ('children' in node) for (const c of (node as Parent).children) walk(c as RootContent);
  };
  walk(tree);
  return out;
}

function collectTags(tree: Root, fmTags: string[]): string[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const t of [...fmTags, ...findAll<Tag>(tree, 'tag').map((n) => n.name)]) {
    const key = t.toLowerCase();
    if (!seen.has(key)) {
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(t);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Parses one page. Pure and synchronous; positions are UTF-16 offsets into `text`. */
export function parsePage(text: string): ParsedPage {
  const loc = locator(text);
  const tree = processor.parse(text) as Root;
  transformInline(tree, text, loc);
  const frontmatter = readFrontmatter(tree);
  return {
    tree,
    frontmatter,
    links: collectLinks(tree, frontmatterLinks(frontmatter)),
    tags: collectTags(tree, frontmatterTags(frontmatter)),
    comments: [],
  };
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/types.ts`:

```ts
// Node types the Liber Linteum Markdown model adds to mdast (M2a spec §2.1).
import type { BlockContent, Data, Literal, Node, Parent, PhrasingContent, Root } from 'mdast';

/** `[[target#heading|alias]]` (also the table form `[[target\|alias]]`). */
export interface WikiLink extends Node {
  type: 'wikiLink';
  target: string;
  heading: string | null;
  alias: string | null;
  data?: Data;
}

/** `![[target#heading|300x200]]`; `alias` keeps the raw text after `|`. */
export interface Embed extends Node {
  type: 'embed';
  target: string;
  heading: string | null;
  alias: string | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  data?: Data;
}

/** `==text==` */
export interface Highlight extends Parent {
  type: 'highlight';
  children: PhrasingContent[];
}

/** `#tag` / `#nested/tag` (Obsidian rules). */
export interface Tag extends Node {
  type: 'tag';
  name: string;
}

/** `> [!type]- Title` */
export interface Callout extends Parent {
  type: 'callout';
  calloutType: string;
  title: string;
  foldable: boolean;
  folded: boolean;
  children: BlockContent[];
}

/** `::::columns` container holding `:::column` children. */
export interface Columns extends Parent {
  type: 'columns';
  children: Column[];
}

export interface Column extends Parent {
  type: 'column';
  children: BlockContent[];
}

/** `%% … %%` (kept only as a range; not rendered). */
export interface Comment extends Literal {
  type: 'comment';
}

declare module 'mdast' {
  interface PhrasingContentMap {
    wikiLink: WikiLink;
    embed: Embed;
    highlight: Highlight;
    tag: Tag;
  }
  interface BlockContentMap {
    callout: Callout;
    columns: Columns;
  }
  interface RootContentMap {
    wikiLink: WikiLink;
    embed: Embed;
    highlight: Highlight;
    tag: Tag;
    callout: Callout;
    columns: Columns;
    column: Column;
  }
}

/** Same names as `liber_markdown::LinkKind::as_str()`. */
export type LinkKind = 'wiki' | 'embed' | 'markdown' | 'markdown_image' | 'related' | 'cover';

export interface LinkRef {
  kind: LinkKind;
  /** Decoded target without `#heading` (as `liber-markdown` stores it). */
  target: string;
  heading: string | null;
  alias: string | null;
  /** UTF-16 offsets of the whole link in the source. */
  range: [number, number];
}

export interface Frontmatter {
  /** Offsets of the whole `---` … `---` block. */
  range: [number, number];
  data: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  error?: string;
}

export interface ParsedPage {
  tree: Root;
  frontmatter: Frontmatter | null;
  links: LinkRef[];
  /** Tags as written, frontmatter first, de-duplicated case-insensitively. */
  tags: string[];
  /** Offsets of `%% … %%` comments (not part of the tree). */
  comments: [number, number][];
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/walk.ts`:

```ts
import type { Node, Parent } from 'mdast';

/** Depth-first list of every node of `type` (document order). */
export function findAll<T extends Node>(tree: Node, type: string): T[] {
  const out: T[] = [];
  const walk = (node: Node) => {
    if (node.type === type) out.push(node as T);
    const children = (node as Parent).children;
    if (children) for (const child of children) walk(child);
  };
  walk(tree);
  return out;
}

/** Start/end offsets of a positioned node. */
export function rangeOf(node: Node): [number, number] {
  return [node.position?.start.offset ?? 0, node.position?.end.offset ?? 0];
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/wikilink.ts`:

```ts
/** Parts of the inside of `[[…]]`, mirroring `liber_markdown::wikilink::parse_inner`. */
export interface WikiParts {
  target: string;
  heading: string | null;
  alias: string | null;
}

/** Splits `target#heading|alias` (the table form `target\|alias` too).
 * Returns `null` for an empty target (e.g. `[[#Heading]]`). */
export function parseWikiInner(inner: string): WikiParts | null {
  let targetPart = inner;
  let alias: string | null = null;
  const bar = inner.indexOf('|');
  if (bar >= 0) {
    const escaped = bar > 0 && inner[bar - 1] === '\\';
    targetPart = inner.slice(0, escaped ? bar - 1 : bar);
    alias = inner.slice(bar + 1);
  }
  let heading: string | null = null;
  const hash = targetPart.indexOf('#');
  if (hash >= 0) {
    heading = targetPart.slice(hash + 1);
    targetPart = targetPart.slice(0, hash);
  }
  const target = targetPart.trim();
  if (!target) return null;
  return { target, heading: heading || null, alias: alias || null };
}

/** Obsidian embed size: `300` or `300x200`. */
export function parseSize(alias: string | null): { width: number | null; height: number | null } {
  const m = alias ? /^\s*(\d+)(?:\s*x\s*(\d+))?\s*$/.exec(alias) : null;
  if (!m) return { width: null, height: null };
  return { width: Number(m[1]), height: m[2] ? Number(m[2]) : null };
}
```

### Task 2: Block extensions (callouts, columns, `%%` comments) and TS ↔ Rust parity fixtures

Spec §2.1, §2.6. Directives are **container directives only** (`:::name`); the text form would turn `3:2` or `note:hello` into directives. Comments are found outside code, masked to the same length and the page re-parsed, so offsets still index the original text.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Callout, Columns, WikiLink } from './types';
import { parsePage } from './parse';
import { findAll } from './walk';

describe('callouts (M2a §2.1, Tier B toggles)', () => {
  it('turns > [!type] Title into a callout with the remaining lines as content', () => {
    const p = parsePage('> [!note] Remember\n> Body with [[Link]]\n');
    const [c] = findAll<Callout>(p.tree, 'callout');
    expect([c.calloutType, c.title, c.foldable, c.folded]).toEqual([
      'note',
      'Remember',
      false,
      false,
    ]);
    expect(findAll<WikiLink>(c, 'wikiLink').map((l) => l.target)).toEqual(['Link']);
    expect(JSON.stringify(c.children)).not.toContain('[!note]');
  });

  it('reads foldable markers and defaults the title to the type', () => {
    const p = parsePage('> [!TIP]- \n> hidden\n\n> [!warning]+ Open\n> shown\n\n> plain quote\n');
    const callouts = findAll<Callout>(p.tree, 'callout');
    expect(callouts.map((c) => [c.calloutType, c.title, c.foldable, c.folded])).toEqual([
      ['tip', 'Tip', true, true],
      ['warning', 'Open', true, false],
    ]);
    expect(findAll(p.tree, 'blockquote')).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('columns (Tier B)', () => {
  it('maps ::::columns / :::column containers to columns and column nodes', () => {
    const src = '::::columns\n:::column\nLeft [[A]]\n:::\n:::column\nRight\n:::\n::::\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    const [cols] = findAll<Columns>(p.tree, 'columns');
    expect(cols.children.map((c) => c.type)).toEqual(['column', 'column']);
    expect(src.slice(cols.position!.start.offset!, cols.position!.end.offset!)).toBe(src.trimEnd());
    expect(p.links.map((l) => l.target)).toEqual(['A']);
  });

  it('never turns ordinary colons into directives', () => {
    const p = parsePage('Ratio 3:2, note:hello, time 10:30, a :b c\n\n::leaf[x]\n');
    const text = JSON.stringify(p.tree);
    expect(text).not.toContain('Directive');
    expect(text).toContain('note:hello');
    expect(text).toContain('::leaf[x]');
  });
});

describe('comments %% … %%', () => {
  it('records inline and block comments and leaves them out of the tree and links', () => {
    const src = 'a %%hidden [[X]]%% b\n\n%%\nblock [[Y]]\n%%\n\nafter [[Z]]\n';
    const p = parsePage(src);
    expect(p.comments.map(([s, e]) => src.slice(s, e))).toEqual([
      '%%hidden [[X]]%%',
      '%%\nblock [[Y]]\n%%',
    ]);
    expect(p.links.map((l) => l.target)).toEqual(['Z']);
    expect(JSON.stringify(p.tree)).not.toContain('hidden');
  });

  it('ignores %% inside code and unclosed %%', () => {
    const p = parsePage('`%%code%%` and %%open [[A]]\n');
    expect(p.comments).toEqual([]);
    expect(p.links.map((l) => l.target)).toEqual(['A']);
  });
});
```

// @vitest-environment node
// Parity with the Rust indexer (M2a spec §2.6): the same fixtures are asserted
// by crates/liber-markdown/tests/parity.rs.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parsePage } from './parse';

const dir = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../../fixtures/markdown/', import.meta.url));
const pages = readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.md'));

describe('parity fixtures (TS model ↔ liber-markdown)', () => {
  it('has fixtures', () => expect(pages.length).toBeGreaterThan(0));
  it.each(pages)('%s', (file) => {
    const src = readFileSync(dir + file, 'utf8');
    const expected = JSON.parse(
      readFileSync(dir + file.replace(/\.md$/, '.expected.json'), 'utf8'),
    );
    const p = parsePage(src);
    expect(
      p.links.map(({ kind, target, heading, alias }) => ({ kind, target, heading, alias })),
    ).toEqual(expected.links);
    expect(p.tags).toEqual(expected.tags);
  });
});

Create `crates/liber-markdown/tests/parity.rs`:

```rust
//! Parity with the TypeScript model (M2a spec §2.6): the same fixtures are
//! asserted by apps/desktop/src/markdown/parity.test.ts.

use liber_markdown::parse_page;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::path::PathBuf;

fn fixtures() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../fixtures/markdown")
}

#[test]
fn pg_05_parity_fixtures_match_the_typescript_model() {
    let mut seen = 0;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixtures()).unwrap() {
        let path = entry.unwrap().path();
        if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some("md") {
            continue;
        }
        seen += 1;
        let src = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        let expected: Value = serde_json::from_str(
            &std::fs::read_to_string(path.with_extension("expected.json")).unwrap(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let stem = path.file_stem().unwrap().to_str().unwrap();
        let meta = parse_page(&src, stem);
        let links: Vec<Value> = meta
            .links
            .iter()
            .map(|l| {
                json!({
                    "kind": l.kind.as_str(),
                    "target": l.target,
                    "heading": l.heading,
                    "alias": l.alias,
                })
            })
            .collect();
        let tags: Vec<&str> = meta.tags.iter().map(|t| t.text.as_str()).collect();
        assert_eq!(Value::Array(links), expected["links"], "{}", path.display());
        assert_eq!(json!(tags), expected["tags"], "{}", path.display());
    }
    assert!(seen > 0, "no fixtures found");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the block extensions**

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/blocks.ts`:

```ts
// Block-level extensions (M2a spec §2.1): callouts, columns, comments.
import type { Blockquote, Paragraph, Parent, Root, RootContent, Text } from 'mdast';
import type { ContainerDirective, LeafDirective } from 'mdast-util-directive';
import { directiveFromMarkdown } from 'mdast-util-directive';
import { directive } from 'micromark-extension-directive';
import type { Processor } from 'unified';
import type { locator } from './locate';
import type { Callout, Column, Columns } from './types';

/** Container (and leaf) directives only: the text form `:name` would turn
 * ordinary text such as `3:2` or `note:hello` into directives. */
export function remarkContainerDirectives(this: Processor) {
  const data = this.data() as {
    micromarkExtensions?: unknown[];
    fromMarkdownExtensions?: unknown[];
  };
  (data.micromarkExtensions ??= []).push({ flow: directive().flow });
  (data.fromMarkdownExtensions ??= []).push(directiveFromMarkdown());
}

const CALLOUT = /^\[!([A-Za-z][\w-]*)\]([+-]?)[ \t]*([^\n]*)/;

function toCallout(quote: Blockquote, src: string): Callout | null {
  const first = quote.children[0];
  if (!first || first.type !== 'paragraph') return null;
  const head = first.children[0];
  if (!head || head.type !== 'text') return null;
  const start = head.position!.start.offset!;
  const raw = src.slice(start, head.position!.end.offset!);
  const m = CALLOUT.exec(raw);
  if (!m) return null;
  const type = m[1].toLowerCase();
  const title = m[3].trim() || type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1);
  // Drop the marker line from the first paragraph.
  const nl = head.value.indexOf('\n');
  const children = [...quote.children];
  const rest = nl >= 0 ? head.value.slice(nl + 1) : '';
  const firstChildren = [...first.children.slice(1)];
  if (rest) {
    const restStart = start + raw.indexOf('\n') + 1;
    firstChildren.unshift({
      type: 'text',
      value: rest,
      position: { start: { ...head.position!.end, offset: restStart }, end: head.position!.end },
    } as Text);
  } else if (firstChildren[0]?.type === 'break') {
    firstChildren.shift();
  }
  if (firstChildren.length) children[0] = { ...first, children: firstChildren } as Paragraph;
  else children.shift();
  return {
    type: 'callout',
    calloutType: type,
    title,
    foldable: m[2] !== '',
    folded: m[2] === '-',
    children: children as Callout['children'],
    position: quote.position,
  };
}

/** Source-text paragraph for a directive we don't support (shown as written). */
function asText(node: RootContent, src: string, loc: ReturnType<typeof locator>): Paragraph {
  const s = node.position!.start.offset!;
  const e = node.position!.end.offset!;
  return {
    type: 'paragraph',
    children: [{ type: 'text', value: src.slice(s, e), position: loc.position(s, e) }],
    position: loc.position(s, e),
  };
}

function toColumns(node: ContainerDirective): Columns {
  const columns: Column[] = [];
  for (const child of node.children) {
    if (child.type === 'containerDirective' && child.name === 'column') {
      columns.push({
        type: 'column',
        children: child.children as Column['children'],
        position: child.position,
      });
    } else if (child.type !== 'paragraph' || child.data?.directiveLabel !== true) {
      // Content outside any :::column goes into its own column.
      columns.push({
        type: 'column',
        children: [child as Column['children'][number]],
        position: child.position,
      });
    }
  }
  return { type: 'columns', children: columns, position: node.position };
}

/** Callouts, columns, and unsupported directives back to text. */
export function transformBlocks(tree: Root, src: string, loc: ReturnType<typeof locator>) {
  const walk = (parent: Parent) => {
    parent.children = parent.children.map((child) => {
      const c = child as RootContent;
      if (c.type === 'blockquote') {
        const callout = toCallout(c, src);
        if (callout) {
          walk(callout);
          return callout;
        }
      }
      if (c.type === 'containerDirective') {
        if (c.name === 'columns') {
          const cols = toColumns(c);
          for (const col of cols.children) walk(col);
          return cols;
        }
        return asText(c, src, loc);
      }
      if (c.type === 'leafDirective') return asText(c as LeafDirective, src, loc);
      if ('children' in c) walk(c as Parent);
      return c;
    }) as typeof parent.children;
  };
  walk(tree);
}

/** Offsets of code (fenced, indented, inline) in a tree, to keep `%%` there literal. */
function codeRanges(tree: Root): [number, number][] {
  const out: [number, number][] = [];
  const walk = (n: RootContent | Root) => {
    if (n.type === 'code' || n.type === 'inlineCode' || n.type === 'yaml' || n.type === 'html') {
      out.push([n.position!.start.offset!, n.position!.end.offset!]);
      return;
    }
    if ('children' in n) for (const c of (n as Parent).children) walk(c as RootContent);
  };
  walk(tree);
  return out;
}

/** `%% … %%` ranges outside code; an unclosed `%%` is not a comment. */
export function findComments(src: string, tree: Root): [number, number][] {
  if (!src.includes('%%')) return [];
  const code = codeRanges(tree);
  const inCode = (i: number) => code.some(([s, e]) => i >= s && i < e);
  const out: [number, number][] = [];
  let i = src.indexOf('%%');
  while (i >= 0) {
    if (inCode(i)) {
      i = src.indexOf('%%', i + 2);
      continue;
    }
    let close = src.indexOf('%%', i + 2);
    while (close >= 0 && inCode(close)) close = src.indexOf('%%', close + 2);
    if (close < 0) break;
    out.push([i, close + 2]);
    i = src.indexOf('%%', close + 2);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Same length as `src`, with comments blanked (newlines kept) so every
 * position in the parsed tree still indexes the original text. */
export function maskComments(src: string, comments: [number, number][]): string {
  if (!comments.length) return src;
  let out = '';
  let last = 0;
  for (const [s, e] of comments) {
    out += src.slice(last, s) + src.slice(s, e).replace(/[^\r\n]/g, ' ');
    last = e;
  }
  return out + src.slice(last);
}
```

### Task 3: Sanitizer and URL policy

Spec §2.4, P4. Uses `hast-util-sanitize` (tree based, keeps source positions for Split scroll sync and M4) instead of DOMPurify.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/package.json`
- Test (create): `apps/desktop/src/markdown/sanitize.test.ts`
- Create: `apps/desktop/src/markdown/sanitize.ts`
- Generated (commit as produced): `pnpm-lock.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1–2 (`ParsedPage`, extension nodes).
- Produces: `toSafeHast(page: ParsedPage, ctx: RenderContext): Root` (hast) with: block elements carrying `dataSourceStart/dataSourceEnd`; remote media replaced by `span[dataLiber=remote-blocked, dataUrl]`; vault media carrying `dataAssetPath` (vault path); paths escaping the vault `dataLiber=missing`; links classified `dataLiber = wikilink | link | external | anchor` with `dataTarget`/`dataHeading` (Markdown link targets are vault paths); heading `id`s (github-slugger). `RenderContext { sourcePath: string }`, `vaultJoin(sourcePath, target): string | null`, `PALETTE = ['red','orange','yellow','green','blue','purple','gray']`.

```ts
import type { Element, Root } from 'hast';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parsePage } from './parse';
import { PALETTE, toSafeHast, vaultJoin } from './sanitize';

function elements(tree: Root): Element[] {
  const out: Element[] = [];
  const walk = (n: Root | Element) => {
    for (const c of n.children) {
      if (c.type === 'element') {
        out.push(c);
        walk(c);
      }
    }
  };
  walk(tree);
  return out;
}

const render = (src: string, sourcePath = 'Notes/page.md') =>
  toSafeHast(parsePage(src), { sourcePath });
const html = (src: string) => JSON.stringify(render(src));

describe('sanitizer (M2a §2.4, P4)', () => {
  it('strips scripts, handlers, javascript: URLs, style, iframes and forms', () => {
    const out = html(
      '<script>alert(1)</script>\n\n<img src="a.png" onerror="alert(1)">\n\n' +
        '<a href="javascript:alert(1)">x</a> <span style="color:red">s</span>\n\n' +
        '<iframe src="https://e.com"></iframe>\n\n<form action="/x"><input name="q"></form>\n',
    );
    for (const bad of ['alert', 'onerror', 'javascript', 'style', 'iframe', 'form', '"q"']) {
      expect(out).not.toContain(bad);
    }
  });

  it('keeps allowed formatting HTML and palette colors only', () => {
    const tree = render(
      '<span data-color="red">r</span> <span data-color="url(x)">u</span> <mark>m</mark> <kbd>k</kbd>\n',
    );
    const spans = elements(tree).filter((e) => e.tagName === 'span');
    expect(spans.map((s) => s.properties.dataColor ?? null)).toEqual(['red', null]);
    expect(elements(tree).map((e) => e.tagName)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['mark', 'kbd']));
    expect(PALETTE).toContain('purple');
  });
});

describe('URL policy (M2a §2.4, PG acceptance: no remote request)', () => {
  it('replaces every remote media source with a blocked placeholder', () => {
    const tree = render(
      '![a](https://x.com/p.png)\n\n<img src="https://x.com/q.png">\n\n' +
        '<audio src="http://x.com/a.mp3" controls></audio>\n\n' +
        '<video controls><source src="https://x.com/v.mp4"></video>\n\n' +
        '<img src="local.png" srcset="https://x.com/2x.png 2x">\n',
    );
    const out = JSON.stringify(tree);
    expect(out).not.toMatch(/"src":"https?:/);
    expect(out).not.toContain('srcset');
    const blocked = elements(tree).filter((e) => e.properties.dataLiber === 'remote-blocked');
    expect(blocked.map((b) => b.properties.dataUrl)).toEqual([
      'https://x.com/p.png',
      'https://x.com/q.png',
      'http://x.com/a.mp3',
      'https://x.com/v.mp4',
    ]);
  });

  it('maps vault paths to data-asset-path relative to the page, refusing paths outside the vault', () => {
    const tree = render(
      '![a](img/a.png) <img src="../b.png"> ![c](../../c.png)\n',
      'Notes/page.md',
    );
    const imgs = elements(tree).filter((e) => e.tagName === 'img');
    expect(imgs.map((i) => [i.properties.src ?? null, i.properties.dataAssetPath ?? null])).toEqual(
      [
        [null, 'Notes/img/a.png'],
        [null, 'b.png'],
        [null, null],
      ],
    );
    expect(imgs[2].properties.dataLiber).toBe('missing');
  });

  it('marks external links and internal Markdown links', () => {
    const tree = render('[e](https://e.com) [m](mailto:a@b.c) [p](Other.md#H)\n');
    const links = elements(tree).filter((e) => e.tagName === 'a');
    expect(links.map((a) => [a.properties.dataLiber, a.properties.href ?? null])).toEqual([
      ['external', 'https://e.com'],
      ['external', 'mailto:a@b.c'],
      ['link', null],
    ]);
    expect(links[2].properties.dataTarget).toBe('Notes/Other.md');
    expect(links[2].properties.dataHeading).toBe('H');
  });

  it('vaultJoin normalises relative paths and refuses escapes', () => {
    expect(vaultJoin('A/B/page.md', '../c.png')).toBe('A/c.png');
    expect(vaultJoin('A/page.md', '/root.png')).toBe('root.png');
    expect(vaultJoin('page.md', '../x.png')).toBeNull();
    expect(vaultJoin('A/page.md', 'sub%20dir/x.png')).toBe('A/sub dir/x.png');
  });
});

describe('extension nodes become marked elements', () => {
  it('wikilinks, embeds, tags, highlights, callouts and columns', () => {
    const tree = render(
      '[[Game 1#Plot|play]] ![[a.png|300]] #tag ==hi==\n\n> [!tip]- Fold\n> body\n\n' +
        '::::columns\n:::column\nL\n:::\n::::\n',
    );
    const marks = elements(tree)
      .map((e) => e.properties.dataLiber)
      .filter(Boolean);
    expect(marks).toEqual(['wikilink', 'embed', 'tag', 'callout', 'columns', 'column']);
    const wl = elements(tree).find((e) => e.properties.dataLiber === 'wikilink')!;
    expect([wl.properties.dataTarget, wl.properties.dataHeading]).toEqual(['Game 1', 'Plot']);
    expect(elements(tree).some((e) => e.tagName === 'mark')).toBe(true);
    const callout = elements(tree).find((e) => e.properties.dataLiber === 'callout')!;
    expect([callout.properties.dataCallout, callout.properties.dataFolded]).toEqual([
      'tip',
      'true',
    ]);
  });

  it('block elements carry their source range for scroll sync and (M4) dragging', () => {
    const src = '# Title\n\nPara one.\n';
    const tree = render(src);
    const p = elements(tree).find((e) => e.tagName === 'p')!;
    expect(
      src.slice(Number(p.properties.dataSourceStart), Number(p.properties.dataSourceEnd)),
    ).toBe('Para one.');
    const h = elements(tree).find((e) => e.tagName === 'h1')!;
    expect(h.properties.id).toBe('title');
  });

  it('frontmatter is not rendered', () => {
    expect(html('---\nsubtitle: S\n---\nBody\n')).not.toContain('subtitle');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/sanitize.ts`:

```ts
// Safe render tree (M2a spec §2.4): mdast → hast with our extension nodes,
// raw HTML parsed in place, then sanitized with an allowlist and the URL
// policy (vault paths → asset paths, remote media → placeholders). Uses
// hast-util-sanitize rather than DOMPurify so source positions survive
// (scroll sync in Split, block dragging in M4).
import GithubSlugger from 'github-slugger';
import type { Element, ElementContent, Nodes, Properties, Root } from 'hast';
import { raw } from 'hast-util-raw';
import { sanitize, type Schema } from 'hast-util-sanitize';
import type { Nodes as MdastNodes } from 'mdast';
import { toHast, type Handler, type State } from 'mdast-util-to-hast';
import type {
  Callout,
  Column,
  Columns,
  Embed,
  Highlight,
  ParsedPage,
  Tag,
  WikiLink,
} from './types';

/** Colors allowed in `data-color` / `data-bg` (theme-aware tokens). */
export const PALETTE = ['red', 'orange', 'yellow', 'green', 'blue', 'purple', 'gray'] as const;

export interface RenderContext {
  /** Vault path of the page being rendered (relative links resolve from its folder). */
  sourcePath: string;
}

/** Vault-relative join of a Markdown/HTML path from a page; `null` if it would
 * leave the vault or is empty. A leading `/` means the vault root. */
export function vaultJoin(sourcePath: string, target: string): string | null {
  let decoded = target;
  try {
    decoded = decodeURIComponent(target);
  } catch {
    // keep as written
  }
  const fromRoot = decoded.startsWith('/');
  const base = fromRoot ? [] : sourcePath.split('/').slice(0, -1);
  const parts = [...base];
  for (const seg of decoded.split('/')) {
    if (!seg || seg === '.') continue;
    if (seg === '..') {
      if (!parts.length) return null;
      parts.pop();
    } else {
      parts.push(seg);
    }
  }
  return parts.length ? parts.join('/') : null;
}

const el = (tagName: string, properties: Properties, children: ElementContent[] = []): Element => ({
  type: 'element',
  tagName,
  properties,
  children,
});

const handlers: Record<string, Handler> = {
  wikiLink(state: State, node: WikiLink) {
    const label = node.alias ?? (node.heading ? `${node.target} › ${node.heading}` : node.target);
    const out = el(
      'a',
      { dataLiber: 'wikilink', dataTarget: node.target, dataHeading: node.heading ?? undefined },
      [{ type: 'text', value: label }],
    );
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  embed(state: State, node: Embed) {
    const out = el('span', {
      dataLiber: 'embed',
      dataTarget: node.target,
      dataHeading: node.heading ?? undefined,
      dataWidth: node.width ?? undefined,
      dataHeight: node.height ?? undefined,
    });
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  highlight(state: State, node: Highlight) {
    const out = el('mark', {}, state.all(node as unknown as MdastNodes));
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  tag(state: State, node: Tag) {
    const out = el('span', { dataLiber: 'tag', dataTag: node.name }, [
      { type: 'text', value: `#${node.name}` },
    ]);
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  callout(state: State, node: Callout) {
    const out = el(
      'div',
      {
        dataLiber: 'callout',
        dataCallout: node.calloutType,
        dataTitle: node.title,
        dataFoldable: String(node.foldable),
        dataFolded: String(node.folded),
      },
      state.wrap(state.all(node as unknown as MdastNodes), true),
    );
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  columns(state: State, node: Columns) {
    const out = el(
      'div',
      { dataLiber: 'columns' },
      state.wrap(state.all(node as unknown as MdastNodes), true),
    );
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  column(state: State, node: Column) {
    const out = el(
      'div',
      { dataLiber: 'column' },
      state.wrap(state.all(node as unknown as MdastNodes), true),
    );
    state.patch(node, out);
    return out;
  },
  yaml() {
    return undefined;
  },
} as unknown as Record<string, Handler>;

const BLOCK = new Set([
  'p',
  'h1',
  'h2',
  'h3',
  'h4',
  'h5',
  'h6',
  'ul',
  'ol',
  'li',
  'blockquote',
  'pre',
  'table',
  'tr',
  'hr',
  'div',
]);

/** `data-source-start/end` on block elements that come from Markdown. */
function markSourceRanges(node: Nodes) {
  if (node.type === 'element' && BLOCK.has(node.tagName) && node.position) {
    node.properties.dataSourceStart = node.position.start.offset;
    node.properties.dataSourceEnd = node.position.end.offset;
  }
  if ('children' in node) for (const c of node.children) markSourceRanges(c as Nodes);
}

const DATA = ['dataLiber', 'dataSourceStart', 'dataSourceEnd'];
const schema: Schema = {
  strip: ['script', 'style'],
  clobber: [],
  clobberPrefix: '',
  ancestors: {},
  protocols: { href: ['http', 'https', 'mailto'], src: ['http', 'https', 'data'] },
  tagNames: [
    'a',
    'b',
    'strong',
    'i',
    'em',
    'u',
    's',
    'del',
    'ins',
    'mark',
    'sub',
    'sup',
    'kbd',
    'code',
    'pre',
    'br',
    'hr',
    'p',
    'span',
    'div',
    'blockquote',
    'ul',
    'ol',
    'li',
    'table',
    'thead',
    'tbody',
    'tr',
    'th',
    'td',
    'details',
    'summary',
    'img',
    'audio',
    'video',
    'source',
    'h1',
    'h2',
    'h3',
    'h4',
    'h5',
    'h6',
    'input',
    'section',
  ],
  attributes: {
    '*': [...DATA, 'title'],
    a: ['href', 'dataTarget', 'dataHeading'],
    span: [
      'dataColor',
      'dataBg',
      'dataTarget',
      'dataHeading',
      'dataWidth',
      'dataHeight',
      'dataTag',
    ],
    div: ['dataColor', 'dataBg', 'dataCallout', 'dataTitle', 'dataFoldable', 'dataFolded'],
    img: ['src', 'alt', 'width', 'height'],
    audio: ['src', 'controls'],
    video: ['src', 'controls', 'width', 'height'],
    source: ['src', 'type'],
    details: ['open'],
    ol: ['start'],
    li: ['className'],
    ul: ['className'],
    code: ['className'],
    td: ['align'],
    th: ['align'],
    input: [['type', 'checkbox'], 'checked', 'disabled'],
  },
  required: { input: { type: 'checkbox', disabled: true } },
};

const REMOTE = /^https?:/i;
const EXTERNAL = /^(https?|mailto):/i;
const MEDIA = new Set(['img', 'audio', 'video', 'source']);

function applyUrlPolicy(parent: Root | Element, ctx: RenderContext) {
  parent.children = parent.children.map((child) => {
    if (child.type !== 'element') return child;
    const p = child.properties;
    for (const key of ['dataColor', 'dataBg'] as const) {
      if (p[key] !== undefined && !(PALETTE as readonly string[]).includes(String(p[key]))) {
        delete p[key];
      }
    }
    if (child.tagName === 'a' && p.dataLiber !== 'wikilink' && typeof p.href === 'string') {
      const href = p.href;
      if (EXTERNAL.test(href)) {
        p.dataLiber = 'external';
      } else if (href.startsWith('#')) {
        p.dataLiber = 'anchor';
      } else {
        const hash = href.indexOf('#');
        const path = hash >= 0 ? href.slice(0, hash) : href;
        const target = path ? vaultJoin(ctx.sourcePath, path) : null;
        p.dataLiber = 'link';
        if (target) p.dataTarget = target;
        if (hash >= 0 && href.slice(hash + 1))
          p.dataHeading = decodeURIComponent(href.slice(hash + 1));
        delete p.href;
      }
    }
    if (MEDIA.has(child.tagName) && typeof p.src === 'string') {
      const src = p.src;
      if (REMOTE.test(src)) {
        return {
          type: 'element',
          tagName: 'span',
          properties: { dataLiber: 'remote-blocked', dataUrl: src },
          children: [{ type: 'text', value: typeof p.alt === 'string' && p.alt ? p.alt : src }],
          position: child.position,
        } satisfies Element;
      }
      if (/^data:image\//i.test(src) && child.tagName === 'img') {
        // inline image data: allowed by the CSP, no request
      } else if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(src)) {
        delete p.src;
      } else {
        const path = vaultJoin(ctx.sourcePath, src);
        delete p.src;
        if (path) p.dataAssetPath = path;
        else p.dataLiber = 'missing';
      }
    }
    applyUrlPolicy(child, ctx);
    return child;
  });
}

function textOf(n: ElementContent): string {
  if (n.type === 'text') return n.value;
  if (n.type === 'element') return n.children.map(textOf).join('');
  return '';
}

function addHeadingIds(tree: Root) {
  const slugger = new GithubSlugger();
  const walk = (n: Root | Element) => {
    for (const c of n.children) {
      if (c.type !== 'element') continue;
      if (/^h[1-6]$/.test(c.tagName)) c.properties.id = slugger.slug(textOf(c));
      walk(c);
    }
  };
  walk(tree);
}

/** The page as a sanitized hast tree, ready for React rendering. */
export function toSafeHast(page: ParsedPage, ctx: RenderContext): Root {
  const hast = toHast(page.tree, { allowDangerousHtml: true, handlers }) as Root;
  markSourceRanges(hast);
  const parsed = raw(hast) as Root;
  const clean = sanitize(parsed, schema) as Root;
  applyUrlPolicy(clean, ctx);
  addHeadingIds(clean);
  return clean;
}
```

### Task 4: Backend — page read/write, conflict copies, link resolution, `liber-asset:`, external opening, `editorMode`

Spec §3.1, §2.3, §2.5, §3.4. Two deliberate deviations (see the hand-off §8): a custom `liber-asset:` protocol instead of Tauri's asset protocol + scope (a scope can't be revoked, so a previous vault would stay readable), and opening URLs/files through our own scheme-checked commands instead of giving the webview `opener:*`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `VaultSession` (`vault()`, `create_page`, `index()`, `reindex_written`), `liber_markdown::{LinkKind, resolve}`, `liber_vault::{Hash, VaultPath, VaultError, is_hidden_name}`.
- Produces (Rust): `VaultSession::write_page(&self, path: &VaultPath, text: &str, expected: Hash) -> Result<Hash>`, `create_conflict_copy(&self, path, text) -> Result<VaultPath>` (`<stem> (conflict).md`, then `(conflict 2)`…), `resolve_links(&self, source, targets: &[(LinkKind, String)]) -> Result<Vec<Option<String>>>`; `liber_desktop::asset::{SCHEME, serve(&AppState, uri_path: &str, range: Option<&str>) -> AssetResponse {status, mime, body, headers}}`.
- Produces (IPC, camelCase args): `page_read {path} → {text, hash}` (non-UTF-8 → kind `binary`); `page_write {path, text, expectedHash} → {hash}`, on mismatch error kind `conflict` with `currentText`, `currentHash`; `page_create_conflict_copy {path, text} → string`; `links_resolve {source, targets: [{kind, target}]} → (string | null)[]` (unknown kind → `invalidUrl`); `url_open {url}` (http/https/mailto only, else `invalidUrl`); `file_open_external {path}` (vault file; missing → `notFound`, escaping → `outsideVault`); settings field `editorMode: "livePreview" | "source" | "split"` (default `livePreview`).

Create `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/asset.rs`:

```rust
//! The `liber-asset:` protocol (M2a spec §2.5): serves files of the open vault
//! only, through `VaultPath` (no traversal, no hidden files), with ranges.

use liber_desktop::AppDirs;
use liber_desktop::asset::serve;
use liber_desktop::state::AppState;
use liber_testkit::copy_fixture;
use serde_json::json;
use tauri::Manager;
use tauri::test::{INVOKE_KEY, get_ipc_response, mock_builder};
use tauri::{WebviewWindowBuilder, ipc::CallbackFn, ipc::InvokeBody, webview::InvokeRequest};

/// One `generate_context!()` per test binary (it embeds an Info.plist symbol).
fn app() -> (
    tauri::App<tauri::test::MockRuntime>,
    tempfile::TempDir,
    tempfile::TempDir,
) {
    let config = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let app = liber_desktop::configure(
        mock_builder(),
        Some(AppDirs {
            config: config.path().into(),
            cache: cache.path().into(),
        }),
    )
    .build(tauri::generate_context!())
    .unwrap();
    (app, config, cache)
}

fn open(
    vault: &std::path::Path,
) -> (
    tauri::App<tauri::test::MockRuntime>,
    tempfile::TempDir,
    tempfile::TempDir,
) {
    let (app, config, cache) = app();
    let main = WebviewWindowBuilder::new(&app, "main", Default::default())
        .build()
        .unwrap();
    let request = InvokeRequest {
        cmd: "vault_open".into(),
        callback: CallbackFn(0),
        error: CallbackFn(1),
        url: if cfg!(windows) {
            "http://tauri.localhost"
        } else {
            "tauri://localhost"
        }
        .parse()
        .unwrap(),
        body: InvokeBody::Json(json!({ "path": vault })),
        headers: Default::default(),
        invoke_key: INVOKE_KEY.to_string(),
    };
    get_ipc_response(&main, request).unwrap();
    (app, config, cache)
}

#[test]
fn serves_vault_files_with_their_media_type() {
    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
    let (app, _c, _k) = open(vault.path());
    let state = app.state::<AppState>();
    let r = serve(&state, "/Character%20Design/assets/rex-cover.png", None);
    assert_eq!(r.status, 200);
    assert_eq!(r.mime, "image/png");
    assert_eq!(
        r.body,
        std::fs::read(vault.path().join("Character Design/assets/rex-cover.png")).unwrap()
    );
    let mp3 = serve(
        &state,
        "/Character%20Design/assets/voice-reference.mp3",
        None,
    );
    assert_eq!(mp3.mime, "audio/mpeg");
}

#[test]
fn refuses_traversal_hidden_files_and_missing_files() {
    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
    let (app, _c, _k) = open(vault.path());
    let state = app.state::<AppState>();
    assert_eq!(serve(&state, "/../secret.png", None).status, 403);
    assert_eq!(serve(&state, "/%2E%2E/secret.png", None).status, 403);
    assert_eq!(serve(&state, "/.obsidian/app.json", None).status, 403);
    assert_eq!(serve(&state, "/nope.png", None).status, 404);
    assert_eq!(serve(&state, "/", None).status, 403);
}

#[test]
fn nothing_is_served_without_an_open_vault() {
    let (app, _c, _k) = app();
    assert_eq!(
        serve(&app.state::<AppState>(), "/Welcome.md", None).status,
        404
    );
}

#[test]
fn byte_ranges_for_audio_seeking() {
    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
    let (app, _c, _k) = open(vault.path());
    let state = app.state::<AppState>();
    let full = std::fs::read(
        vault
            .path()
            .join("Character Design/assets/voice-reference.mp3"),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let r = serve(
        &state,
        "/Character%20Design/assets/voice-reference.mp3",
        Some("bytes=2-5"),
    );
    assert_eq!(r.status, 206);
    assert_eq!(r.body, full[2..=5]);
    assert!(
        r.headers
            .iter()
            .any(|(k, v)| k == "Content-Range" && v == &format!("bytes 2-5/{}", full.len()))
    );
    let open_end = serve(
        &state,
        "/Character%20Design/assets/voice-reference.mp3",
        Some("bytes=3-"),
    );
    assert_eq!(open_end.body, full[3..]);
    let bad = serve(
        &state,
        "/Character%20Design/assets/voice-reference.mp3",
        Some("bytes=999999-"),
    );
    assert_eq!(bad.status, 416);
}

/// Spec §7 "revoked on close": once the vault is closed nothing of it is served.
#[test]
fn closing_the_vault_stops_serving_it() {
    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
    let (app, _c, _k) = open(vault.path());
    let path = "/Character%20Design/assets/rex-cover.png";
    assert_eq!(serve(&app.state::<AppState>(), path, None).status, 200);
    let main = app.get_webview_window("main").unwrap();
    let request = InvokeRequest {
        cmd: "vault_close".into(),
        callback: CallbackFn(0),
        error: CallbackFn(1),
        url: if cfg!(windows) {
            "http://tauri.localhost"
        } else {
            "tauri://localhost"
        }
        .parse()
        .unwrap(),
        body: InvokeBody::Json(json!({})),
        headers: Default::default(),
        invoke_key: INVOKE_KEY.to_string(),
    };
    get_ipc_response(&main, request).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(serve(&app.state::<AppState>(), path, None).status, 404);
}
```

+/// M2a §3.1: full-text read and compare-and-swap write; a stale hash returns
+/// a `conflict` error carrying the current text and hash, and writes nothing.
+#[test]
+fn ed_08_page_read_and_write_with_conflict_payload() {
+    let h = harness();
+    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
+    call(&h.main, "vault_open", json!({ "path": vault.path() })).unwrap();
+    let read = call(&h.main, "page_read", json!({ "path": "Welcome.md" })).unwrap();
+    let text = read["text"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
+    let hash = read["hash"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();
+    let new_text = format!("{text}\nEdited [[Sparda]]\n");
+    let written = call(
+        &h.main,
+        "page_write",
+        json!({ "path": "Welcome.md", "text": new_text, "expectedHash": hash }),
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    assert_ne!(written["hash"], read["hash"]);
+    assert_eq!(
+        std::fs::read_to_string(vault.path().join("Welcome.md")).unwrap(),
+        new_text
+    );
+    let err = call(
+        &h.main,
+        "page_write",
+        json!({ "path": "Welcome.md", "text": "lost", "expectedHash": hash }),
+    )
+    .unwrap_err();
+    assert_eq!(kind(err.clone()), "conflict");
+    assert_eq!(err["currentText"], new_text.as_str());
+    assert_eq!(err["currentHash"], written["hash"]);
+    assert_eq!(
+        std::fs::read_to_string(vault.path().join("Welcome.md")).unwrap(),
+        new_text
+    );
+    let bad = call(
+        &h.main,
+        "page_write",
+        json!({ "path": "Welcome.md", "text": "x", "expectedHash": "zz" }),
+    )
+    .unwrap_err();
+    assert_eq!(kind(bad), "io");
+}

+}

+    .unwrap_err();

+}

+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    let (s, warning) = store(dir.path()).get();
+    assert_eq!(s.editor_mode, EditorMode::LivePreview);
+    assert_eq!(s.theme, Theme::Dark);
+    assert!(warning.unwrap().contains("editorMode"));

Create `crates/liber-session/tests/editing.rs`:

```rust
//! Editing support for M2a (spec §3.1): CAS page writes, conflict copies and
//! link resolution against the live file set.

use liber_markdown::LinkKind;
use liber_session::{SessionError, SessionEvent, VaultSession};
use liber_testkit::copy_fixture;
use liber_vault::{Hash, VaultError, VaultPath};

fn vp(s: &str) -> VaultPath {
    VaultPath::new(s).unwrap()
}

fn session(fixture: &str) -> (tempfile::TempDir, tempfile::TempDir, VaultSession) {
    let vault = copy_fixture(fixture);
    let cache = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let (s, _) = VaultSession::open(vault.path(), cache.path(), |_: SessionEvent| {}).unwrap();
    (vault, cache, s)
}

#[test]
fn ed_08_write_page_is_compare_and_swap_and_reindexes() {
    let (dir, _c, s) = session("obsidian-basic");
    let path = vp("Welcome.md");
    let (_, hash) = s.vault().read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    let new_hash = s
        .write_page(&path, "# Hello again\n\n[[Sparda]]\n", hash)
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(new_hash, Hash::of(b"# Hello again\n\n[[Sparda]]\n"));
    assert_eq!(
        std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("Welcome.md")).unwrap(),
        "# Hello again\n\n[[Sparda]]\n"
    );
    let page = s.page(&path).unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(page.title, "Hello again");
    assert_eq!(
        page.links[0].target_path.as_deref(),
        Some("Character Design/DMC/Sparda.md")
    );
    // A stale expected hash is refused and nothing is written.
    let err = s.write_page(&path, "lost", hash).unwrap_err();
    assert!(
        matches!(err, SessionError::Vault(VaultError::Conflict { current, .. }) if current == new_hash),
        "{err:?}"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("Welcome.md")).unwrap(),
        "# Hello again\n\n[[Sparda]]\n"
    );
}

#[test]
fn ed_08_conflict_copies_get_free_names_next_to_the_page() {
    let (dir, _c, s) = session("obsidian-basic");
    let first = s
        .create_conflict_copy(&vp("Game Design/Game 1.md"), "theirs 1")
        .unwrap();
    let second = s
        .create_conflict_copy(&vp("Game Design/Game 1.md"), "theirs 2")
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(first.as_str(), "Game Design/Game 1 (conflict).md");
    assert_eq!(second.as_str(), "Game Design/Game 1 (conflict 2).md");
    assert_eq!(
        std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("Game Design/Game 1 (conflict 2).md")).unwrap(),
        "theirs 2"
    );
    assert!(
        s.page(&second).unwrap().is_some(),
        "conflict copy is indexed"
    );
}

#[test]
fn pg_05_resolve_links_uses_the_live_file_set() {
    let (_d, _c, s) = session("ambiguous-names");
    let targets = vec![
        (LinkKind::Wiki, "Ideas".to_string()),
        (LinkKind::Wiki, "Deep/Ideas".to_string()),
        (LinkKind::Markdown, "Ideas.md".to_string()),
        (LinkKind::Wiki, "Nowhere".to_string()),
    ];
    let got = s.resolve_links(&vp("Projects/Other.md"), &targets).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        got,
        vec![
            Some("Projects/Ideas.md".to_string()),
            Some("Projects/Deep/Ideas.md".to_string()),
            Some("Projects/Ideas.md".to_string()),
            None,
        ]
    );
    s.create_page(&VaultPath::root(), "Nowhere").unwrap();
    let again = s
        .resolve_links(
            &vp("Projects/Other.md"),
            &[(LinkKind::Wiki, "Nowhere".to_string())],
        )
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(again, vec![Some("Nowhere.md".to_string())]);
}
```
