Create `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/asset.rs`:

```rust
//! `liber-asset:` — serves the open vault's files to the webview (M2a spec §2.5).
//!
//! Tauri's built-in asset protocol can only *add* allowed directories, so a
//! vault that was open earlier would stay readable after switching vaults.
//! This protocol instead resolves every request through the current session:
//! `VaultPath` validation (no `..`, no absolute paths, no symlink escapes),
//! hidden files refused, nothing served when no vault is open.

use crate::state::AppState;
use liber_vault::{VaultPath, is_hidden_name};
use percent_encoding::percent_decode_str;

pub const SCHEME: &str = "liber-asset";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct AssetResponse {
    pub status: u16,
    pub mime: &'static str,
    pub body: Vec<u8>,
    pub headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
}

impl AssetResponse {
    fn status(status: u16) -> AssetResponse {
        AssetResponse {
            status,
            mime: "text/plain",
            body: Vec::new(),
            headers: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn into_response(self) -> tauri::http::Response<Vec<u8>> {
        let mut builder = tauri::http::Response::builder()
            .status(self.status)
            .header("Content-Type", self.mime)
            .header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            .header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        for (k, v) in &self.headers {
            builder = builder.header(k.as_str(), v.as_str());
        }
        builder.body(self.body).expect("valid response")
    }
}

pub fn mime_for(path: &str) -> &'static str {
    let ext = path
        .rsplit_once('.')
        .map(|(_, e)| e.to_ascii_lowercase())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    match ext.as_str() {
        "png" => "image/png",
        "jpg" | "jpeg" => "image/jpeg",
        "gif" => "image/gif",
        "webp" => "image/webp",
        "svg" => "image/svg+xml",
        "avif" => "image/avif",
        "bmp" => "image/bmp",
        "wav" => "audio/wav",
        "mp3" => "audio/mpeg",
        "m4a" => "audio/mp4",
        "ogg" => "audio/ogg",
        "mp4" => "video/mp4",
        "webm" => "video/webm",
        "mov" => "video/quicktime",
        "pdf" => "application/pdf",
        _ => "application/octet-stream",
    }
}

/// `bytes=a-b`, `bytes=a-` or `bytes=-n` → inclusive range, or `Err(())` when unsatisfiable.
fn parse_range(header: &str, len: usize) -> Option<Result<(usize, usize), ()>> {
    let spec = header.trim().strip_prefix("bytes=")?;
    let (a, b) = spec.split_once('-')?;
    if len == 0 {
        return Some(Err(()));
    }
    let range = match (a.trim(), b.trim()) {
        ("", n) => {
            let n: usize = n.parse().ok()?;
            (len.saturating_sub(n), len - 1)
        }
        (a, "") => (a.parse().ok()?, len - 1),
        (a, b) => (a.parse().ok()?, b.parse::<usize>().ok()?.min(len - 1)),
    };
    Some(if range.0 >= len || range.0 > range.1 {
        Err(())
    } else {
        Ok(range)
    })
}

/// Serves `uri_path` (the request path, percent-encoded, starting with `/`).
pub fn serve(state: &AppState, uri_path: &str, range: Option<&str>) -> AssetResponse {
    let Ok(session) = state.session() else {
        return AssetResponse::status(404);
    };
    let decoded = percent_decode_str(uri_path.trim_start_matches('/')).decode_utf8_lossy();
    let Ok(path) = VaultPath::new(&decoded) else {
        return AssetResponse::status(403);
    };
    if path.is_root() || path.as_str().split('/').any(is_hidden_name) {
        return AssetResponse::status(403);
    }
    let bytes = match session.vault().read(&path) {
        Ok((bytes, _)) => bytes,
        Err(liber_vault::VaultError::OutsideVault(_)) => return AssetResponse::status(403),
        Err(_) => return AssetResponse::status(404),
    };
    let mime = mime_for(path.as_str());
    let len = bytes.len();
    match range.and_then(|r| parse_range(r, len)) {
        Some(Ok((start, end))) => AssetResponse {
            status: 206,
            mime,
            body: bytes[start..=end].to_vec(),
            headers: vec![
                ("Accept-Ranges".into(), "bytes".into()),
                ("Content-Range".into(), format!("bytes {start}-{end}/{len}")),
            ],
        },
        Some(Err(())) => AssetResponse {
            headers: vec![("Content-Range".into(), format!("bytes */{len}"))],
            ..AssetResponse::status(416)
        },
        None => AssetResponse {
            status: 200,
            mime,
            body: bytes,
            headers: vec![("Accept-Ranges".into(), "bytes".into())],
        },
    }
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/editor_commands.rs`:

```rust
//! Editor commands (M2a spec §3.1): full-text read, compare-and-swap write,
//! conflict copies, link resolution, and opening things outside the app.
//! Every command runs off the UI thread (see `vault_commands::blocking`).

use crate::error::{CmdResult, CommandError, ConflictInfo, ErrorKind};
use crate::state::AppState;
use crate::vault_commands::blocking;
use liber_markdown::LinkKind;
use liber_session::SessionError;
use liber_vault::{Hash, VaultError, VaultPath};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tauri::{AppHandle, Manager, Runtime};
use tauri_plugin_opener::OpenerExt;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct PageText {
    pub text: String,
    pub hash: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct WriteResult {
    pub hash: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct LinkTarget {
    pub kind: String,
    pub target: String,
}

fn text_of(bytes: Vec<u8>, path: &VaultPath) -> CmdResult<String> {
    String::from_utf8(bytes)
        .map_err(|_| CommandError::new(ErrorKind::Binary, format!("not UTF-8 text: {path}")))
}

pub(crate) fn page_read_impl(state: &AppState, path: String) -> CmdResult<PageText> {
    let session = state.session()?;
    let vp = VaultPath::new(&path)?;
    let (bytes, hash) = session.vault().read(&vp)?;
    Ok(PageText {
        text: text_of(bytes, &vp)?,
        hash: hash.to_hex(),
    })
}

pub(crate) fn page_write_impl(
    state: &AppState,
    path: String,
    text: String,
    expected_hash: String,
) -> CmdResult<WriteResult> {
    let session = state.session()?;
    let vp = VaultPath::new(&path)?;
    let expected = Hash::from_hex(&expected_hash)
        .ok_or_else(|| CommandError::new(ErrorKind::Io, "invalid content hash"))?;
    match session.write_page(&vp, &text, expected) {
        Ok(hash) => Ok(WriteResult {
            hash: hash.to_hex(),
        }),
        Err(SessionError::Vault(VaultError::Conflict { .. })) => {
            let (bytes, current) = session.vault().read(&vp)?;
            let mut err = CommandError::new(
                ErrorKind::Conflict,
                format!("{path} changed on disk since it was opened"),
            );
            err.conflict = Some(ConflictInfo {
                current_text: String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).into_owned(),
                current_hash: current.to_hex(),
            });
            Err(err)
        }
        Err(e) => Err(e.into()),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn page_create_conflict_copy_impl(
    state: &AppState,
    path: String,
    text: String,
) -> CmdResult<String> {
    let session = state.session()?;
    Ok(session
        .create_conflict_copy(&VaultPath::new(&path)?, &text)?
        .to_string())
}

pub(crate) fn links_resolve_impl(
    state: &AppState,
    source: String,
    targets: Vec<LinkTarget>,
) -> CmdResult<Vec<Option<String>>> {
    let session = state.session()?;
    let parsed = targets
        .into_iter()
        .map(|t| {
            LinkKind::parse(&t.kind)
                .map(|k| (k, t.target))
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    CommandError::new(
                        ErrorKind::InvalidUrl,
                        format!("unknown link kind: {}", t.kind),
                    )
                })
        })
        .collect::<CmdResult<Vec<_>>>()?;
    Ok(session.resolve_links(&VaultPath::new(&source)?, &parsed)?)
}

/// Only web and mail links leave the app (M2a §3.4, P1: the OS browser, never the webview).
fn checked_url(url: &str) -> CmdResult<&str> {
    let lower = url.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.starts_with("https://") || lower.starts_with("http://") || lower.starts_with("mailto:")
    {
        Ok(url.trim())
    } else {
        Err(CommandError::new(
            ErrorKind::InvalidUrl,
            format!("refusing to open {url}"),
        ))
    }
}

pub(crate) fn url_open_impl<R: Runtime>(app: &AppHandle<R>, url: String) -> CmdResult<()> {
    let url = checked_url(&url)?;
    app.opener()
        .open_url(url, None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| CommandError::new(ErrorKind::Io, e.to_string()))
}

pub(crate) fn file_open_external_impl<R: Runtime>(
    app: &AppHandle<R>,
    path: String,
) -> CmdResult<()> {
    let state = app.state::<AppState>();
    let session = state.session()?;
    let vp = VaultPath::new(&path)?;
    if session.vault().stat(&vp)?.is_none() {
        return Err(CommandError::new(
            ErrorKind::NotFound,
            format!("not found: {path}"),
        ));
    }
    let full = session.vault().fs_path(&vp)?;
    app.opener()
        .open_path(full.to_string_lossy(), None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| CommandError::new(ErrorKind::Io, e.to_string()))
}

// ---- command wrappers: run off the UI thread ----

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn page_read<R: Runtime>(app: AppHandle<R>, path: String) -> CmdResult<PageText> {
    blocking(app, move |app| {
        page_read_impl(&app.state::<AppState>(), path)
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn page_write<R: Runtime>(
    app: AppHandle<R>,
    path: String,
    text: String,
    expected_hash: String,
) -> CmdResult<WriteResult> {
    blocking(app, move |app| {
        page_write_impl(&app.state::<AppState>(), path, text, expected_hash)
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn page_create_conflict_copy<R: Runtime>(
    app: AppHandle<R>,
    path: String,
    text: String,
) -> CmdResult<String> {
    blocking(app, move |app| {
        page_create_conflict_copy_impl(&app.state::<AppState>(), path, text)
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn links_resolve<R: Runtime>(
    app: AppHandle<R>,
    source: String,
    targets: Vec<LinkTarget>,
) -> CmdResult<Vec<Option<String>>> {
    blocking(app, move |app| {
        links_resolve_impl(&app.state::<AppState>(), source, targets)
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn url_open<R: Runtime>(app: AppHandle<R>, url: String) -> CmdResult<()> {
    blocking(app, move |app| url_open_impl(&app, url)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn file_open_external<R: Runtime>(app: AppHandle<R>, path: String) -> CmdResult<()> {
    blocking(app, move |app| file_open_external_impl(&app, path)).await
}
```

Create `crates/liber-session/src/editing.rs`:

```rust
//! Editing support for the M2a editor (spec §3.1): compare-and-swap page
//! writes, conflict copies and link resolution against the live file set.

use crate::error::{Result, SessionError};
use crate::session::VaultSession;
use liber_markdown::{LinkKind, resolve};
use liber_vault::{Hash, VaultError, VaultPath};

impl VaultSession {
    /// Writes a page's full text if the file still has `expected` as its hash
    /// (ED-08), then re-indexes it. A changed file returns
    /// `SessionError::Vault(VaultError::Conflict { current, .. })` and nothing
    /// is written.
    pub fn write_page(&self, path: &VaultPath, text: &str, expected: Hash) -> Result<Hash> {
        let hash = self
            .vault
            .write_cas(path, text.as_bytes(), Some(expected))?;
        self.reindex_written(path, text, hash)?;
        let mut index = self.index();
        let files = index.file_set()?;
        index.resolve_from(&files, path.as_str())?;
        Ok(hash)
    }

    /// Saves `text` next to `path` as `<stem> (conflict).md`, then
    /// `(conflict 2)`, … and returns the new page's path.
    pub fn create_conflict_copy(&self, path: &VaultPath, text: &str) -> Result<VaultPath> {
        let dir = path.parent().unwrap_or_else(VaultPath::root);
        let stem = path.stem();
        for n in 1.. {
            let name = if n == 1 {
                format!("{stem} (conflict)")
            } else {
                format!("{stem} (conflict {n})")
            };
            match self.create_page(&dir, &name) {
                Ok(copy) => {
                    self.write_page(&copy, text, Hash::of(b""))?;
                    return Ok(copy);
                }
                Err(SessionError::Vault(VaultError::AlreadyExists(_))) => continue,
                Err(e) => return Err(e),
            }
        }
        unreachable!("the loop only ends by returning")
    }

    /// Resolves link targets as seen from `source` (PG-05), using the current
    /// file set — new and renamed pages are taken into account immediately.
    pub fn resolve_links(
        &self,
        source: &VaultPath,
        targets: &[(LinkKind, String)],
    ) -> Result<Vec<Option<String>>> {
        let files = self.index().file_set()?;
        Ok(targets
            .iter()
            .map(|(kind, target)| resolve(*kind, target, source.as_str(), &files))
            .collect())
    }
}
```

### Task 5: Frontend IPC, page documents (autosave, retry, merge, conflicts) and the editor store

Spec §3.3. A `PageDoc` is one open page: editor text (LF), merge base (last text known on disk), hash, and a serialized save/reload queue. Text changed from outside the editor goes to `doc.sink` synchronously, so no keystroke can land between a merge and the editor seeing it. Pages switched away from keep retrying in the background until saved.

- Produces (editor): `class PageDoc` — `static load(path, hooks): Promise<IpcResult<PageDoc>>`, fields `path, text, base, hash, eol, orphaned, revision, sink`, getters `dirty`, `status: 'saved' | 'saving' | 'retrying'`, methods `edit(text)`, `flush()`, `sync()`, `moveTo(path)`, `markDeleted(): boolean`, `saveAsNew(): Promise<IpcResult<string>>`, `dispose()`; constants `AUTOSAVE_MS = 500`, `RETRY_MIN_MS = 1000`, `RETRY_MAX_MS = 30000`; `merge3(base, mine, theirs): {clean: true; text} | {clean: false}`; `detectEol`, `toLf`, `fromLf`.
- Produces (stores): `useEditorStore` with `{doc, path, status, dirty, orphaned, hash, error, open(path), close(), flushAll(timeoutMs?), onExternalChange(c, exists?), follow(from, to), syncPaths(paths)}`, `resetEditorStore()`, `installQuitGuard()`, `installQuitGuardIfTauri()`, `QUIT_FLUSH_MS = 2000`; `openPage(path)` in `stores/navigate.ts`; toasts accept `action?: {label, run}`; test helper `fakeDisk(files)` in `src/test/fakeDisk.ts`.

```diff
diff --git a/apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/commands.rs b/apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/commands.rs
--- a/apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/commands.rs
+++ b/apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/commands.rs
@@ -548,3 +548,31 @@ fn external_open_commands_refuse_unsafe_targets() {
     .unwrap_err();
     assert!(err.to_string().contains("not allowed"), "{err}");
 }
+
+/// M2a §3.3: closing the window first flushes unsaved pages. The UI listens for
+/// `tauri://close-requested` and then destroys the window itself, so `main`
+/// needs `core:window:allow-destroy` — and another window must not get it.
+#[test]
+fn ed_08_main_window_may_flush_then_close_itself() {
+    let h = harness();
+    let other = WebviewWindowBuilder::new(&h.app, "other", Default::default())
+        .build()
+        .unwrap();
+    let listen = json!({
+        "event": "tauri://close-requested",
+        "target": { "kind": "Window", "label": "main" },
+        "handler": 9
+    });
+    if let Err(e) = call(&h.main, "plugin:event|listen", listen) {
+        panic!("main must be allowed to listen for close requests: {e}");
+    }
+    let destroy = json!({ "label": "other" });
+    let err = call(&other, "plugin:window|destroy", destroy.clone()).expect_err("other");
+    assert!(err.to_string().contains("not allowed"), "{err}");
+    if let Err(e) = call(&h.main, "plugin:window|destroy", json!({ "label": "main" })) {
+        assert!(
+            !e.to_string().contains("not allowed"),
+            "main must be allowed to destroy itself: {e}"
+        );
+    }
+}

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/merge.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { detectEol, fromLf, toLf } from './eol';
import { merge3 } from './merge';

describe('merge3 (ED-08: 3-way merge of external changes)', () => {
  const base = 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\nfive\n';

  it('merges edits on separate lines', () => {
    const mine = 'one\nTWO\nthree\nfour\nfive\n';
    const theirs = 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\nFIVE\n';
    expect(merge3(base, mine, theirs)).toEqual({
      clean: true,
      text: 'one\nTWO\nthree\nfour\nFIVE\n',
    });
  });

  it('takes the other side when only one side changed', () => {
    expect(merge3(base, base, 'x\n')).toEqual({ clean: true, text: 'x\n' });
    expect(merge3(base, 'y\n', base)).toEqual({ clean: true, text: 'y\n' });
    expect(merge3(base, 'same\n', 'same\n')).toEqual({ clean: true, text: 'same\n' });
  });

  it('reports overlapping edits as a conflict', () => {
    const mine = 'one\ntwo\nMINE\nfour\nfive\n';
    const theirs = 'one\ntwo\nTHEIRS\nfour\nfive\n';
    expect(merge3(base, mine, theirs)).toEqual({ clean: false });
  });

  it('merges an identical change made on both sides (false conflict)', () => {
    const mine = 'one\nTWO\nthree\nfour\nFIVE\n';
    const theirs = 'one\nTWO\nthree\nfour\nfive\n';
    expect(merge3(base, mine, theirs)).toEqual({ clean: true, text: mine });
  });
});

describe('line endings (CRLF files stay CRLF)', () => {
  it('detects the first line ending and converts both ways', () => {
    expect(detectEol('a\r\nb\r\n')).toBe('\r\n');
    expect(detectEol('a\nb\r\n')).toBe('\n');
    expect(detectEol('no newline')).toBe('\n');
    expect(toLf('a\r\nb\rc\n')).toBe('a\nb\nc\n');
    expect(fromLf('a\nb\n', '\r\n')).toBe('a\r\nb\r\n');
    expect(fromLf('a\nb\n', '\n')).toBe('a\nb\n');
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/pageDoc.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { clearMocks } from '../../ipc/testing';
import { fakeDisk, hashOf } from '../../test/fakeDisk';
import { AUTOSAVE_MS, PageDoc, type PageDocHooks } from './pageDoc';

const hooks = (): PageDocHooks & { copies: string[] } => {
  const copies: string[] = [];
  return { copies, onChange: () => undefined, onConflictCopy: (_d, copy) => copies.push(copy) };
};

async function load(path: string, h = hooks()) {
  const res = await PageDoc.load(path, h);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(res.error.message);
  return res.value;
}

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  clearMocks();
});

describe('PageDoc autosave (ED-08, M2a §3.3)', () => {
  it('saves 500 ms after the last change, once, with the latest text', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    doc.edit('one\nt');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(300);
    doc.edit('one\ntwo');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(AUTOSAVE_MS - 1);
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([]);
    expect(doc.status).toBe('saving');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([{ path: 'A.md', text: 'one\ntwo' }]);
    expect(doc.status).toBe('saved');
    expect(doc.hash).toBe(hashOf('one\ntwo'));
  });

  it('flush saves immediately and does nothing when clean', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([]);
    doc.edit('two\n');
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('two\n');
  });

  it('retries failed saves with backoff 1 s → 2 s → 4 s and never drops the text', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    disk.failWrites(3);
    doc.edit('two\n');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(AUTOSAVE_MS);
    expect(doc.status).toBe('retrying');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(999);
    expect(disk.writes()).toHaveLength(1);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
    expect(disk.writes()).toHaveLength(2);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2000);
    expect(disk.writes()).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(doc.text).toBe('two\n');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(4000);
    expect(disk.writes()).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(doc.status).toBe('saved');
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('two\n');
  });

  it('keeps CRLF files CRLF: the editor sees LF, the disk gets CRLF back', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'W.md': 'one\r\ntwo\r\n' });
    const doc = await load('W.md');
    expect(doc.text).toBe('one\ntwo\n');
    doc.edit('one\nTWO\n');
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('W.md')).toBe('one\r\nTWO\r\n');
  });
});

describe('PageDoc external changes (M2a §3.3)', () => {
  it('reloads a clean page and hands the new text to the editor', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    const seen: string[] = [];
    doc.sink = (t) => seen.push(t);
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\nfrom elsewhere\n');
    await doc.sync();
    expect(seen).toEqual(['one\nfrom elsewhere\n']);
    expect(doc.dirty).toBe(false);
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('ignores the echo of its own save', async () => {
    fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    const seen: string[] = [];
    doc.sink = (t) => seen.push(t);
    doc.edit('two\n');
    await doc.flush();
    await doc.sync();
    expect(seen).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('merges non-overlapping edits into the editor and saves the result', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    const seen: string[] = [];
    doc.sink = (t) => seen.push(t);
    doc.edit('ONE\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n');
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
    await doc.sync();
    expect(seen).toEqual(['ONE\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n']);
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('ONE\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
  });

  it('overlapping edits: keeps mine, saves theirs as a conflict copy, says so', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\n' });
    const h = hooks();
    const doc = await load('A.md', h);
    doc.edit('one\nMINE\n');
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\nTHEIRS\n');
    await doc.sync();
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('A (conflict).md')).toBe('one\nTHEIRS\n');
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('one\nMINE\n');
    expect(h.copies).toEqual(['A (conflict).md']);
  });

  it('a write that hits a changed file takes the same merge path', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    doc.edit('ONE\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n');
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n'); // no watcher event yet
    await doc.flush();
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('ONE\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
    expect(disk.calls.filter(([c]) => c === 'page_read')).toHaveLength(1); // only the load
  });

  it('deleted while dirty: keeps the text and can save it as a new page', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'notes/A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('notes/A.md');
    doc.edit('unsaved\n');
    disk.files.delete('notes/A.md');
    expect(doc.markDeleted()).toBe(true);
    expect(doc.orphaned).toBe(true);
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([]);
    const saved = await doc.saveAsNew();
    expect(saved).toEqual({ ok: true, value: 'notes/A.md' });
    expect(disk.files.get('notes/A.md')).toBe('unsaved\n');
    expect(doc.orphaned).toBe(false);
  });

  it('deleted while clean: nothing to keep', async () => {
    fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    expect(doc.markDeleted()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('follows a rename and saves to the new path', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    doc.edit('two\n');
    disk.files.set('B.md', disk.files.get('A.md')!);
    disk.files.delete('A.md');
    doc.moveTo('B.md');
    await doc.flush();
    expect(disk.files.get('B.md')).toBe('two\n');
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/ipc/editor.test.ts`:

```ts
import { clearMocks, mockIPC, mockWindows } from '@tauri-apps/api/mocks';
import { emit } from '@tauri-apps/api/event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  fileOpenExternal,
  linksResolve,
  onCloseRequested,
  pageCreateConflictCopy,
  pageRead,
  pageWrite,
  toCommandError,
  urlOpen,
} from './index';

afterEach(() => clearMocks());

describe('M2a ipc commands', () => {
  it('passes camelCase arguments for the editor commands', async () => {
    const calls: [string, unknown][] = [];
    mockIPC((cmd, args) => {
      calls.push([cmd, args]);
      if (cmd === 'page_read') return { text: '# A\n', hash: 'h1' };
      if (cmd === 'page_write') return { hash: 'h2' };
      if (cmd === 'page_create_conflict_copy') return 'A (conflict).md';
      if (cmd === 'links_resolve') return ['B.md', null];
    });
    expect(await pageRead('A.md')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: { text: '# A\n', hash: 'h1' } });
    expect(await pageWrite('A.md', '# A!\n', 'h1')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: { hash: 'h2' } });
    expect(await pageCreateConflictCopy('A.md', 'theirs')).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: 'A (conflict).md',
    });
    const resolved = await linksResolve('A.md', [
      { kind: 'wiki', target: 'B' },
      { kind: 'wiki', target: 'Nope' },
    ]);
    expect(resolved).toEqual({ ok: true, value: ['B.md', null] });
    await urlOpen('https://example.org');
    await fileOpenExternal('assets/brief.pdf');
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      ['page_read', { path: 'A.md' }],
      ['page_write', { path: 'A.md', text: '# A!\n', expectedHash: 'h1' }],
      ['page_create_conflict_copy', { path: 'A.md', text: 'theirs' }],
      [
        'links_resolve',
        {
          source: 'A.md',
          targets: [
            { kind: 'wiki', target: 'B' },
            { kind: 'wiki', target: 'Nope' },
          ],
        },
      ],
      ['url_open', { url: 'https://example.org' }],
      ['file_open_external', { path: 'assets/brief.pdf' }],
    ]);
  });

  it('keeps the conflict payload of a rejected write (ED-08)', async () => {
    mockIPC(() => {
      throw { kind: 'conflict', message: 'changed', currentText: 'disk', currentHash: 'h9' };
    });
    expect(await pageWrite('A.md', 'mine', 'h1')).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { kind: 'conflict', message: 'changed', currentText: 'disk', currentHash: 'h9' },
    });
    expect(toCommandError({ kind: 'binary', message: 'not UTF-8' })).toEqual({
      kind: 'binary',
      message: 'not UTF-8',
    });
  });

  it('runs the close handler before the window is destroyed', async () => {
    mockWindows('main');
    const calls: string[] = [];
    mockIPC((cmd) => void calls.push(cmd), { shouldMockEvents: true });
    let flushed = false;
    await onCloseRequested(async () => {
      await Promise.resolve();
      flushed = true;
    });
    await emit('tauri://close-requested');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    expect(flushed).toBe(true);
    expect(calls).toContain('plugin:window|destroy');
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/stores/editorStore.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Toaster } from '../features/toasts/Toaster';
import { clearMocks, emit, mockIPC, mockWindows } from '../ipc/testing';
import { fakeDisk } from '../test/fakeDisk';
import { installQuitGuard, resetEditorStore, useEditorStore } from './editorStore';
import { usePageStore } from './pageStore';
import { useToastStore } from './toastStore';

const editor = () => useEditorStore.getState();

beforeEach(() => useToastStore.getState().clear());
afterEach(() => {
  resetEditorStore();
  vi.useRealTimers();
  clearMocks();
});

describe('editorStore (M2a §3.3)', () => {
  it('review focus: switching pages mid-save saves the old page and leaves the new one alone', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'a\n', 'B.md': 'b\n' });
    await editor().open('A.md');
    editor().doc!.edit('a edited\n');
    const release = disk.holdWrites();
    const saving = editor().doc!.flush(); // write of A is in flight
    await editor().open('B.md');
    expect(editor().path).toBe('B.md');
    release();
    await saving;
    await editor().flushAll();
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('a edited\n');
    expect(disk.files.get('B.md')).toBe('b\n');
    expect(editor().doc!.text).toBe('b\n');
    expect(editor().status).toBe('saved');
    expect(disk.writes()).toEqual([{ path: 'A.md', text: 'a edited\n' }]);
  });

  it('a page whose save keeps failing stays open in the background until it saves', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'a\n', 'B.md': 'b\n' });
    await editor().open('A.md');
    editor().doc!.edit('a2\n');
    disk.failWrites(1);
    await editor().open('B.md');
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('a\n');
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000); // retry
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('a2\n');
    // Coming back finds the saved text.
    await editor().open('A.md');
    expect(editor().doc!.text).toBe('a2\n');
  });

  it('follows renames (own or external) keeping unsaved text', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A/x.md': 'x\n' });
    await editor().open('A/x.md');
    editor().doc!.edit('x2\n');
    disk.files.set('B/x.md', disk.files.get('A/x.md')!);
    disk.files.delete('A/x.md');
    await editor().onExternalChange({
      created: [],
      modified: [],
      removed: [],
      renamed: [['A', 'B']],
    });
    expect(editor().path).toBe('B/x.md');
    await editor().flushAll();
    expect(disk.files.get('B/x.md')).toBe('x2\n');
  });

  it('deleted while dirty: orphaned, text kept; deleted while clean: closed', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'a\n', 'B.md': 'b\n' });
    await editor().open('A.md');
    editor().doc!.edit('keep me\n');
    disk.files.delete('A.md');
    await editor().onExternalChange({ created: [], modified: [], removed: ['A.md'], renamed: [] });
    expect(editor().orphaned).toBe(true);
    expect(editor().doc!.text).toBe('keep me\n');
    await editor().open('B.md');
    disk.files.delete('B.md');
    await editor().onExternalChange({ created: [], modified: [], removed: ['B.md'], renamed: [] });
    expect(editor().doc).toBeNull();
  });

  it('modified on disk reloads; a delete + recreate (atomic save) is a modify', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'a\n' });
    await editor().open('A.md');
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'new\n');
    await editor().onExternalChange(
      { created: ['A.md'], modified: [], removed: ['A.md'], renamed: [] },
      () => true,
    );
    expect(editor().doc!.text).toBe('new\n');
  });

  it('a conflict copy shows a toast whose "Open" button opens the copy', async () => {
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\n' });
    render(<Toaster />);
    await act(() => editor().open('A.md'));
    editor().doc!.edit('one\nMINE\n');
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\nTHEIRS\n');
    await act(() =>
      editor().onExternalChange({ created: [], modified: ['A.md'], removed: [], renamed: [] }),
    );
    const toast = screen.getByRole('status');
    expect(toast).toHaveTextContent(
      'Someone else changed this page — their version was saved as “A (conflict)”',
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open' }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(usePageStore.getState().path).toBe('A (conflict).md'));
  });

  it('binary pages cannot be edited', async () => {
    mockIPC(() => {
      throw { kind: 'binary', message: 'not UTF-8 text: latin1.md' };
    });
    await editor().open('latin1.md');
    expect(editor().doc).toBeNull();
    expect(editor().error?.kind).toBe('binary');
  });

  it('flushes unsaved pages before the window closes (max 2 s)', async () => {
    mockWindows('main');
    const disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'a\n' });
    const calls = () => disk.calls.map(([c]) => c);
    await editor().open('A.md');
    editor().doc!.edit('typed just before quitting\n');
    await installQuitGuard();
    await emit('tauri://close-requested');
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(calls()).toContain('plugin:window|destroy'));
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('typed just before quitting\n');
    expect(calls().indexOf('page_write')).toBeLessThan(calls().indexOf('plugin:window|destroy'));
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/test/fakeDisk.ts`:

```ts
// A fake vault behind mockIPC for editor tests: content hashes, compare-and-swap
// writes with the M2a conflict payload, conflict copies and page creation.
import { mockIPC } from '../ipc/testing';

export const hashOf = (text: string) => `h:${text}`;

export interface FakeDisk {
  files: Map<string, string>;
  calls: [string, Record<string, unknown>][];
  writes: () => { path: string; text: string }[];
  /** Makes the next `n` writes fail with an io error. */
  failWrites: (n: number) => void;
  /** Holds writes until the returned function is called. */
  holdWrites: () => () => void;
}

const err = (kind: string, message: string, extra: object = {}) => ({ kind, message, ...extra });

export function fakeDisk(initial: Record<string, string>): FakeDisk {
  const files = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
  const calls: [string, Record<string, unknown>][] = [];
  let failing = 0;
  let gate: Promise<void> | null = null;
  mockIPC(
    async (cmd, raw) => {
      const args = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, string>;
      calls.push([cmd, args]);
      switch (cmd) {
        case 'page_read': {
          const text = files.get(args.path);
          if (text === undefined) throw err('notFound', args.path);
          return { text, hash: hashOf(text) };
        }
        case 'page_write': {
          if (gate) await gate;
          if (failing > 0) {
            failing--;
            throw err('io', 'disk full');
          }
          const current = files.get(args.path);
          if (current === undefined) throw err('notFound', args.path);
          if (hashOf(current) !== args.expectedHash) {
            throw err('conflict', 'changed', {
              currentText: current,
              currentHash: hashOf(current),
            });
          }
          files.set(args.path, args.text);
          return { hash: hashOf(args.text) };
        }
        case 'page_create_conflict_copy': {
          const stem = args.path.replace(/\.md$/, '');
          let copy = `${stem} (conflict).md`;
          for (let n = 2; files.has(copy); n++) copy = `${stem} (conflict ${n}).md`;
          files.set(copy, args.text);
          return copy;
        }
        case 'entry_create_page': {
          const name = args.name.endsWith('.md') ? args.name : `${args.name}.md`;
          const path = args.dir ? `${args.dir}/${name}` : name;
          if (files.has(path)) throw err('alreadyExists', path);
          files.set(path, '');
          return path;
        }
        default:
          return null;
      }
    },
    { shouldMockEvents: true },
  );
  return {
    files,
    calls,
    writes: () =>
      calls
        .filter(([c]) => c === 'page_write')
        .map(([, a]) => ({ path: a.path as string, text: a.text as string })),
    failWrites: (n) => {
      failing = n;
    },
    holdWrites: () => {
      let release!: () => void;
      gate = new Promise((r) => (release = r));
      return () => {
        gate = null;
        release();
      };
    },
  };
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/eol.ts`:

```ts
// The editor works on LF text; a CRLF file is written back as CRLF so a save
// never rewrites every line of it (Review Focus: CRLF).

export type Eol = '\n' | '\r\n';

/** The file's line ending, taken from its first line break. */
export function detectEol(text: string): Eol {
  const i = text.indexOf('\n');
  return i > 0 && text[i - 1] === '\r' ? '\r\n' : '\n';
}

export const toLf = (text: string): string => text.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n');

export const fromLf = (text: string, eol: Eol): string =>
  eol === '\n' ? text : text.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n');
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/merge.ts`:

```ts
import { diff3Merge } from 'node-diff3';

export type MergeOutcome = { clean: true; text: string } | { clean: false };

const lines = (text: string): string[] => text.split(/(?<=\n)/);

/** Line-based 3-way merge (M2a §3.3): `base` is the last text both sides
 *  agreed on, `mine` the editor, `theirs` the file on disk. */
export function merge3(base: string, mine: string, theirs: string): MergeOutcome {
  if (mine === base || mine === theirs) return { clean: true, text: theirs };
  if (theirs === base) return { clean: true, text: mine };
  let text = '';
  for (const region of diff3Merge(lines(mine), lines(base), lines(theirs), {
    excludeFalseConflicts: true,
  })) {
    if (region.conflict) return { clean: false };
    text += (region.ok ?? []).join('');
  }
  return { clean: true, text };
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/pageDoc.ts`:

```ts
import {
  entryCreatePage,
  pageCreateConflictCopy,
  pageRead,
  pageWrite,
  type IpcResult,
} from '../../ipc';
import { nameOf, parentOf } from '../../stores/tree';
import { detectEol, fromLf, toLf, type Eol } from './eol';
import { merge3 } from './merge';

/** Autosave delay after the last change (M2a §3.3). */
export const AUTOSAVE_MS = 500;
export const RETRY_MIN_MS = 1000;
export const RETRY_MAX_MS = 30_000;

export type SaveStatus = 'saved' | 'saving' | 'retrying';

export interface PageDocHooks {
  /** Status, path or text changed. */
  onChange: (doc: PageDoc) => void;
  /** Overlapping edits: their version was saved as `copy`. */
  onConflictCopy: (doc: PageDoc, copy: string) => void;
}

/** One open page: the editor text, the last text known to be on disk (the
 *  merge base) and its hash, plus the save loop. Saves, reloads and merges run
 *  one at a time; typing never waits for them. */
export class PageDoc {
  /** Editor text, LF line endings. */
  text: string;
  /** Last text known to be on disk, LF. */
  base: string;
  hash: string;
  eol: Eol;
  /** Deleted on disk while it had unsaved edits: kept, never written back. */
  orphaned = false;
  /** Bumped whenever the text changes from outside the editor. */
  revision = 0;
  /** Receives text that changed from outside the editor, synchronously, so a
   *  keystroke can't land between the merge and the editor seeing it. */
  sink: ((text: string) => void) | null = null;

  private writing = false;
  private failing = false;
  private retryDelay = 0;
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private queue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

  private constructor(
    public path: string,
    raw: string,
    hash: string,
    private readonly hooks: PageDocHooks,
  ) {
    this.text = this.base = toLf(raw);
    this.hash = hash;
    this.eol = detectEol(raw);
  }

  static async load(path: string, hooks: PageDocHooks): Promise<IpcResult<PageDoc>> {
    const res = await pageRead(path);
    if (!res.ok) return res;
    if (typeof res.value?.text !== 'string' || typeof res.value.hash !== 'string') {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: { kind: 'io', message: `unexpected page_read reply for ${path}` },
      };
    }
    return { ok: true, value: new PageDoc(path, res.value.text, res.value.hash, hooks) };
  }

  get dirty(): boolean {
    return this.text !== this.base;
  }

  get status(): SaveStatus {
    if (this.failing) return 'retrying';
    return this.writing || this.dirty ? 'saving' : 'saved';
  }

  /** The editor's text changed. */
  edit(text: string): void {
    if (text === this.text) return;
    const wasDirty = this.dirty;
    this.text = text;
    if (!this.orphaned) this.schedule(AUTOSAVE_MS);
    if (wasDirty !== this.dirty) this.hooks.onChange(this);
  }

  /** Saves now (if there is anything to save); resolves when done. */
  flush(): Promise<void> {
    this.cancelTimer();
    return this.enqueue(() => this.save());
  }

  /** The file changed on disk: reload or merge (M2a §3.3). */
  sync(): Promise<void> {
    return this.enqueue(async () => {
      const res = await pageRead(this.path);
      if (res.ok) await this.reconcile(res.value.text, res.value.hash);
      else if (res.error.kind === 'notFound') this.markDeleted();
    });
  }

  /** The file was renamed or moved; keeps the text and history. */
  moveTo(path: string): void {
    this.path = path;
    this.hooks.onChange(this);
  }

  /** The file is gone. Returns whether there are unsaved edits worth keeping. */
  markDeleted(): boolean {
    if (!this.dirty) return false;
    this.cancelTimer();
    this.orphaned = true;
    this.failing = false;
    this.hooks.onChange(this);
    return true;
  }

  /** Writes an orphaned page's text to a new page next to its old path. */
  async saveAsNew(): Promise<IpcResult<string>> {
    const dir = parentOf(this.path);
    const stem = nameOf(this.path).replace(/\.md$/i, '');
    let created = await entryCreatePage(dir, stem);
    if (!created.ok && created.error.kind === 'alreadyExists') {
      created = await pageCreateConflictCopy(this.path, '');
    }
    if (!created.ok) return created;
    const fresh = await pageRead(created.value);
    if (!fresh.ok) return fresh;
    this.path = created.value;
    this.base = toLf(fresh.value.text);
    this.hash = fresh.value.hash;
    this.orphaned = false;
    await this.flush();
    return created;
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.cancelTimer();
    this.sink = null;
  }

  private enqueue(job: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
    this.queue = this.queue.then(job, job);
    return this.queue;
  }

  private cancelTimer(): void {
    if (this.timer) clearTimeout(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
  }

  private schedule(ms: number): void {
    this.cancelTimer();
    this.timer = setTimeout(() => void this.flush(), ms);
  }

  private async save(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.dirty || this.orphaned) return;
    const text = this.text;
    this.writing = true;
    this.hooks.onChange(this);
    const res = await pageWrite(this.path, fromLf(text, this.eol), this.hash);
    this.writing = false;
    if (res.ok) {
      this.base = text;
      this.hash = res.value.hash;
      this.failing = false;
      this.retryDelay = 0;
      if (this.dirty) this.schedule(AUTOSAVE_MS); // typed while saving
    } else if (res.error.kind === 'conflict' && res.error.currentText !== undefined) {
      await this.reconcile(res.error.currentText, res.error.currentHash ?? '');
    } else if (res.error.kind === 'notFound') {
      this.markDeleted();
    } else {
      this.retry();
    }
    this.hooks.onChange(this);
  }

  private retry(): void {
    this.failing = true;
    this.retryDelay = this.retryDelay ? Math.min(this.retryDelay * 2, RETRY_MAX_MS) : RETRY_MIN_MS;
    this.schedule(this.retryDelay);
  }

  /** Brings the disk version (`raw`, `hash`) in: replace when clean, merge
   *  when dirty, conflict copy when the merge fails. */
  private async reconcile(raw: string, hash: string): Promise<void> {
    if (hash === this.hash) return; // our own save, or nothing new
    const theirs = toLf(raw);
    if (raw.includes('\n')) this.eol = detectEol(raw);
    if (!this.dirty) {
      this.base = theirs;
      this.hash = hash;
      this.replace(theirs);
      return;
    }
    const merged = merge3(this.base, this.text, theirs);
    if (merged.clean) {
      this.base = theirs;
      this.hash = hash;
      this.replace(merged.text);
    } else {
      const copy = await pageCreateConflictCopy(this.path, raw);
      if (!copy.ok) {
        this.retry(); // never overwrite their version before it is saved
        return;
      }
      this.base = theirs;
      this.hash = hash;
      this.hooks.onConflictCopy(this, copy.value);
    }
    if (this.dirty) this.schedule(0);
    this.hooks.onChange(this);
  }

  private replace(text: string): void {
    if (text === this.text) return;
    this.text = text;
    this.revision++;
    this.sink?.(text);
  }
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/stores/editorStore.ts`:

```ts
import i18next from 'i18next';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { PageDoc, type PageDocHooks, type SaveStatus } from '../features/editor/pageDoc';
import { isTauri, onCloseRequested, type ChangedPayload, type CommandError } from '../ipc';
import { useToastStore } from './toastStore';
import { nameOf, remap } from './tree';

/** How long closing the window waits for pending saves (M2a §3.3). */
export const QUIT_FLUSH_MS = 2000;

interface EditorState {
  /** The page being edited, or `null`. */
  doc: PageDoc | null;
  path: string | null;
  status: SaveStatus;
  dirty: boolean;
  orphaned: boolean;
  hash: string | null;
  /** Why the requested page can't be edited (e.g. `binary`). */
  error: CommandError | null;
  open: (path: string) => Promise<void>;
  /** Saves and closes the current page. */
  close: () => Promise<void>;
  /** Saves every open page (current and background); capped at `timeoutMs`. */
  flushAll: (timeoutMs?: number) => Promise<void>;
  onExternalChange: (c: ChangedPayload, exists?: (path: string) => boolean) => Promise<void>;
  follow: (from: string, to: string) => void;
  /** Re-reads pages rewritten by our own link updates (rename/move). */
  syncPaths: (paths: string[]) => Promise<void>;
}

/** Every page with a live document: the current one plus pages switched away
 *  from whose last save hasn't succeeded yet (they keep retrying). */
const docs = new Map<string, PageDoc>();
let seq = 0;

const IDLE = {
  doc: null,
  path: null,
  status: 'saved' as SaveStatus,
  dirty: false,
  orphaned: false,
  hash: null,
  error: null,
};

const snapshot = (doc: PageDoc) => ({
  doc,
  path: doc.path,
  status: doc.status,
  dirty: doc.dirty,
  orphaned: doc.orphaned,
  hash: doc.hash,
  error: null,
});

const hooks: PageDocHooks = {
  onChange: (doc) => {
    const current = useEditorStore.getState().doc;
    if (doc === current) useEditorStore.setState(snapshot(doc));
    else dropIfDone(doc);
  },
  onConflictCopy: (_doc, copy) => {
    useToastStore.getState().push({
      message: i18next.t('editor.conflictCopy', { name: nameOf(copy).replace(/\.md$/i, '') }),
      // Lazy: navigate → vaultStore → pageStore → editorStore would be a load cycle.
      action: {
        label: i18next.t('editor.openCopy'),
        run: () => void import('./navigate').then((m) => m.openPage(copy)),
      },
    });
  },
};

/** A background page is dropped once it's saved (or orphaned: nothing to retry). */
function dropIfDone(doc: PageDoc) {
  if (doc === useEditorStore.getState().doc) return;
  if (doc.status === 'saved' || doc.orphaned) {
    doc.dispose();
    if (docs.get(doc.path) === doc) docs.delete(doc.path);
  }
}

const delay = (ms: number) => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

/** Test helper. */
export function resetEditorStore() {
  seq++;
  docs.forEach((d) => d.dispose());
  docs.clear();
  useEditorStore.setState({ ...IDLE });
}

export const useEditorStore = create<EditorState>((set, get) => ({
  ...IDLE,
  open: async (path) => {
    const prev = get().doc;
    if (prev?.path === path) return;
    const mine = ++seq;
    if (prev) void prev.flush().then(() => dropIfDone(prev));
    set({ ...IDLE, path });
    let doc = docs.get(path);
    if (!doc) {
      const res = await PageDoc.load(path, hooks);
      if (mine !== seq) return; // a newer open won
      if (!res.ok) {
        set({ ...IDLE, path, error: res.error });
        return;
      }
      doc = res.value;
      docs.set(path, doc);
    }
    set(snapshot(doc));
  },
  close: async () => {
    seq++;
    const doc = get().doc;
    set({ ...IDLE });
    if (doc) {
      await doc.flush();
      dropIfDone(doc);
    }
  },
  flushAll: async (timeoutMs) => {
    const all = Promise.all([...docs.values()].map((d) => d.flush()));
    await (timeoutMs === undefined ? all : Promise.race([all, delay(timeoutMs)]));
    docs.forEach((d) => dropIfDone(d));
  },
  onExternalChange: async (c, exists) => {
    for (const [from, to] of c.renamed) get().follow(from, to);
    for (const doc of [...docs.values()]) {
      const removed = c.removed.some((r) => remap(doc.path, r, r) !== null);
      const touched = removed || c.modified.includes(doc.path) || c.created.includes(doc.path);
      if (removed && !(exists?.(doc.path) ?? false)) {
        if (!doc.markDeleted()) {
          doc.dispose();
          docs.delete(doc.path);
          if (get().doc === doc) set({ ...IDLE });
        }
      } else if (touched) {
        await doc.sync();
      }
    }
  },
  follow: (from, to) => {
    for (const doc of [...docs.values()]) {
      const next = remap(doc.path, from, to);
      if (!next) continue;
      docs.delete(doc.path);
      docs.set(next, doc);
      doc.moveTo(next);
    }
  },
  syncPaths: async (paths) => {
    await Promise.all(paths.flatMap((p) => docs.get(p)?.sync() ?? []));
  },
}));

/** Closing the window first saves every unsaved page (max 2 s). */
export async function installQuitGuard(): Promise<void> {
  await onCloseRequested(() => useEditorStore.getState().flushAll(QUIT_FLUSH_MS));
}

/** Installs the quit guard when running inside Tauri. */
export function installQuitGuardIfTauri(): void {
  if (isTauri()) void installQuitGuard().catch(() => undefined);
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/stores/navigate.ts`:

```ts
import { usePageStore } from './pageStore';
import { parentOf } from './tree';
import { useVaultStore } from './vaultStore';

/** Opens a page from anywhere (links, toasts): reveals and selects it in the
 *  explorer and shows it. */
export async function openPage(path: string): Promise<void> {
  const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
  const dir = parentOf(path);
  if (dir) vault.expand(dir);
  vault.select(path);
  await usePageStore.getState().load(path);
}
```

### Task 6: Rendered pane — links, embeds, callouts, columns, privacy placeholders

Spec §4, §3.4. The pane renders the sanitized hast with our own small hast → React renderer (no extra dependency); links resolve through `links_resolve`; note embeds transclude lazily; nested embeds become link cards; links never navigate the webview (all `href="#"` + handlers).

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/Preview.test.tsx`:

````tsx
import { act, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import axe from 'axe-core';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { clearMocks, mockIPC } from '../../ipc/testing';
import { resetEditorStore } from '../../stores/editorStore';
import { usePageStore } from '../../stores/pageStore';
import { useUiStore } from '../../stores/uiStore';
import { Preview, RENDER_DEBOUNCE_MS } from './Preview';

/** Vault seen by links_resolve / page_read in these tests. */
const FILES: Record<string, string> = {
  'Notes/Page.md': '',
  'Notes/Sparda.md':
    '# Sparda\n\nA demon knight.\n\n## Weapons\n\nThe sword.\n\n## Family\n\nTwins.\n',
  'Notes/Loop.md': '# Loop\n\n![[Page]]\n',
  'Notes/Nested.md': '# Nested\n\n![[Sparda]]\n',
  'assets/rex cover.png': '',
  'assets/voice.mp3': '',
  'assets/brief.pdf': '',
};

function vault() {
  const calls: [string, Record<string, unknown>][] = [];
  mockIPC((cmd, raw) => {
    const args = (raw ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
    calls.push([cmd, args]);
    if (cmd === 'links_resolve') {
      return (args.targets as { kind: string; target: string }[]).map(({ kind, target }) => {
        const t = target.replace(/^\//, '');
        if (kind === 'markdown') return t in FILES ? t : null;
        const hit = Object.keys(FILES).find(
          (p) => p === t || p.endsWith(`/${t}`) || p.replace(/\.md$/, '').endsWith(`/${t}`),
        );
        return hit ?? null;
      });
    }
    if (cmd === 'page_read') {
      const text = FILES[args.path as string];
      if (text === undefined) throw { kind: 'notFound', message: String(args.path) };
      return { text, hash: 'h' };
    }
    if (cmd === 'page_get') throw { kind: 'notFound', message: 'x' };
    return null;
  });
  return calls;
}

const show = (text: string, path = 'Notes/Page.md') => render(<Preview text={text} path={path} />);

beforeEach(() => {
  vault();
  useUiStore.setState({ dialog: null });
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  usePageStore.getState().clear();
  resetEditorStore();
  clearMocks();
});

describe('Preview: every node type (M2a §4)', () => {
  it('renders Markdown, GFM and the Obsidian extensions; frontmatter stays hidden', async () => {
    show(
      [
        '---',
        'subtitle: hidden',
        '---',
        '# Title',
        '',
        'Some *em* **strong** ~~gone~~ ==marked== `code` #idea <span data-color="red">red</span>',
        '',
        '- [x] done',
        '- [ ] todo',
        '',
        '1. one',
        '',
        '> quoted',
        '',
        '> [!warning]- Careful',
        '> folded body',
        '',
        '```js',
        'let x = 1;',
        '```',
        '',
        '| a | b |',
        '|---|---|',
        '| 1 | 2 |',
        '',
        '::::columns',
        ':::column',
        'left',
        ':::',
        ':::column',
        'right',
        ':::',
        '::::',
        '',
        '---',
        '',
        '<script>alert(1)</script>',
      ].join('\n'),
    );
    expect(screen.queryByText(/subtitle/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Title' })).toHaveAttribute('id', 'title');
    expect(screen.getByText('em').tagName).toBe('EM');
    expect(screen.getByText('strong').tagName).toBe('STRONG');
    expect(screen.getByText('gone').tagName).toBe('DEL');
    expect(screen.getByText('marked').tagName).toBe('MARK');
    expect(screen.getByText('code').tagName).toBe('CODE');
    expect(screen.getByText('#idea')).toHaveAttribute('data-liber', 'tag');
    expect(screen.getByText('red')).toHaveAttribute('data-color', 'red');
    const done = screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'done' });
    expect(done).toBeChecked();
    expect(done).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'todo' })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByText('quoted').closest('blockquote')).not.toBeNull();
    const callout = screen.getByText('Careful').closest('details')!;
    expect(callout).toHaveAttribute('data-callout', 'warning');
    expect(callout).not.toHaveAttribute('open');
    expect(screen.getByText('let x = 1;').closest('pre')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getByRole('table')).toHaveTextContent('12');
    const columns = screen.getByText('left').closest('[data-liber="columns"]')!;
    expect(within(columns as HTMLElement).getByText('right')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(document.querySelector('hr')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(document.body.innerHTML).not.toContain('alert');
  });

  it('marks block elements with their source range (Split scroll sync)', () => {
    show('# A\n\npara\n');
    const p = screen.getByText('para');
    expect(p).toHaveAttribute('data-source-start', '5');
    expect(p).toHaveAttribute('data-source-end', '9');
  });

  it('re-renders on a 150 ms debounce', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const { rerender } = show('first');
    rerender(<Preview text="second" path="Notes/Page.md" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('first')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(RENDER_DEBOUNCE_MS));
    expect(screen.getByText('second')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('has no axe violations', async () => {
    const { container } = show(
      '# Title\n\n- [x] done\n\n[[Sparda]] [[Nope]] [site](https://example.org)\n\n![](https://e.com/x.png)\n',
    );
    await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'Sparda' });
    const results = await axe.run(container);
    expect(results.violations).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('Preview: links (M2a §3.4)', () => {
  it('shows resolved, unresolved and external links differently', async () => {
    show('[[Sparda]] [[Nope]] [md](Sparda.md) [site](https://example.org)\n');
    const sparda = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'Sparda' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(sparda).toHaveAttribute('data-state', 'resolved'));
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Nope' })).toHaveAttribute('data-state', 'unresolved');
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'md' })).toHaveAttribute('data-state', 'resolved');
    const site = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /site/ });
    expect(site).toHaveAttribute('data-liber', 'external');
    expect(site).not.toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://example.org');
  });

  it('opens resolved pages, offers to create missing ones and confirms external links', async () => {
    show('[[Sparda#Weapons]] [[Nope]] [site](https://example.org)\n');
    const sparda = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'Sparda › Weapons' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(sparda).toHaveAttribute('data-state', 'resolved'));
    await userEvent.click(sparda);
    await waitFor(() => expect(usePageStore.getState().path).toBe('Notes/Sparda.md'));
    expect(usePageStore.getState().anchor?.heading).toBe('Weapons');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Nope' }));
    expect(useUiStore.getState().dialog).toEqual({
      type: 'createPage',
      source: 'Notes/Page.md',
      target: 'Nope',
    });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: /site/ }));
    expect(useUiStore.getState().dialog).toEqual({
      type: 'openExternal',
      url: 'https://example.org',
    });
  });
});

describe('Preview: embeds (M2a §4, §1.1)', () => {
  it('images with the size syntax, through liber-asset:, lazy', async () => {
    show('![[rex cover.png|300]]\n');
    const img = await screen.findByRole('img', { name: 'rex cover.png' });
    expect(img).toHaveAttribute('src', 'liber-asset://localhost/assets/rex%20cover.png');
    expect(img).toHaveAttribute('width', '300');
    expect(img).toHaveAttribute('loading', 'lazy');
  });

  it('audio gets native controls', async () => {
    show('![[voice.mp3]]\n');
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(document.querySelector('audio')).toHaveAttribute(
        'src',
        'liber-asset://localhost/assets/voice.mp3',
      ),
    );
    expect(document.querySelector('audio')).toHaveAttribute('controls');
  });

  it('notes are transcluded with a title and "Open"; a heading embeds its section only', async () => {
    show('![[Sparda#Weapons]]\n');
    const embed = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Sparda › Weapons' });
    expect(await within(embed).findByText('The sword.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(embed).queryByText('Twins.')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(embed).getByRole('button', { name: 'Open' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(usePageStore.getState().path).toBe('Notes/Sparda.md'));
  });

  it('nested embeds become link cards; a page embedding itself says so', async () => {
    show('![[Nested]]\n\n![[Page]]\n');
    const nested = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Nested' });
    expect(await within(nested).findByRole('link', { name: 'Sparda' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(nested).queryByText('A demon knight.')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Embedded note refers back to this page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('missing targets get a "Not found" card with "Create page"; other files a file card', async () => {
    show('![[Ghost]]\n\n![[brief.pdf]]\n');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Not found: Ghost')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create page' }));
    expect(useUiStore.getState().dialog).toMatchObject({ type: 'createPage', target: 'Ghost' });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Open in default app' }));
    expect(useUiStore.getState().dialog).toEqual({ type: 'openFile', path: 'assets/brief.pdf' });
  });

  it('remote media never load: a privacy placeholder instead (P1)', () => {
    show('![cat](https://e.com/cat.png)\n\n<img src="http://e.com/a.png">\n');
    expect(screen.getAllByText('Remote image blocked (privacy)')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(document.querySelector('img')).toBeNull();
  });
});

Create `apps/desktop/src/theme/palette.test.ts`:

```ts
// @vitest-environment node
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PALETTE } from '../markdown/sanitize';

const css = readFileSync(new URL('./tokens.css', import.meta.url), 'utf8');

/** `--name: #hex` pairs of one rule block (light: first `:root {`, dark: `[data-theme='dark']`). */
function tokens(block: string): Map<string, string> {
  return new Map([...block.matchAll(/(--[\w-]+):\s*(#[0-9a-f]{6})/gi)].map((m) => [m[1], m[2]]));
}

const light = tokens(css.slice(0, css.indexOf('@media')));
const dark = tokens(css.slice(css.indexOf(":root[data-theme='dark']")));

function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = [1, 3, 5].map((i) => {
    const c = parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255;
    return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  });
  return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
}

const contrast = (a: string, b: string) => {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x);
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
};

describe('palette tokens (M2a §4: readable in light and dark)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['light', light],
    ['dark', dark],
  ] as const)('%s: every color is ≥ 4.5:1 on its tint and on the page surface', (_, t) => {
    for (const color of PALETTE) {
      const fg = t.get(`--palette-${color}`)!;
      const bg = t.get(`--palette-${color}-bg`)!;
      expect(fg, color).toBeDefined();
      expect(contrast(fg, bg), `${color} on its tint`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
      expect(contrast(fg, t.get('--color-surface')!), `${color} on surface`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(
        4.5,
      );
      expect(contrast(t.get('--color-text')!, bg), `text on ${color}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
    }
  });
});
```
