Create `apps/desktop/src/features/dialogs/LinkDialogs.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { entryCreatePage, fileOpenExternal, urlOpen, type IpcResult } from '../../ipc';
import { errorMessage } from '../../stores/messages';
import { openPage } from '../../stores/navigate';
import { useToastStore } from '../../stores/toastStore';
import { nameOf, parentOf } from '../../stores/tree';
import { useVaultStore } from '../../stores/vaultStore';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import styles from './Dialogs.module.css';

function report(res: IpcResult<unknown>) {
  if (!res.ok) useToastStore.getState().push({ message: errorMessage(res.error), tone: 'error' });
}

function Confirm({
  title,
  text,
  confirm,
  onConfirm,
  onClose,
}: {
  title: string;
  text?: string;
  confirm: string;
  onConfirm: () => Promise<void>;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Dialog title={title} onClose={onClose}>
      {text && <p className={`${styles.text} ${styles.wrap}`}>{text}</p>}
      <div className={styles.buttons}>
        <button type="button" className={styles.button} onClick={onClose}>
          {t('dialogs.cancel')}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`${styles.button} ${styles.primary}`}
          onClick={() => {
            onClose();
            void onConfirm();
          }}
        >
          {confirm}
        </button>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/** External links leave the app only after a confirmation, to the OS browser (P1). */
export function OpenExternalDialog({ url, onClose }: { url: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Confirm
      title={t('dialogs.external.title')}
      text={url}
      confirm={t('dialogs.external.confirm')}
      onConfirm={async () => report(await urlOpen(url))}
      onClose={onClose}
    />
  );
}

export function OpenFileDialog({ path, onClose }: { path: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Confirm
      title={t('dialogs.openFile.title', { name: nameOf(path) })}
      confirm={t('dialogs.openFile.confirm')}
      onConfirm={async () => report(await fileOpenExternal(path))}
      onClose={onClose}
    />
  );
}

/** "Create page 'X'?" for an unresolved link: created next to the linking page. */
export function CreatePageDialog({
  source,
  target,
  onClose,
}: {
  source: string;
  target: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const name = nameOf(target).replace(/\.md$/i, '');
  const dir = parentOf(source);
  const folder = dir || (useVaultStore.getState().info?.name ?? '');
  return (
    <Confirm
      title={t('dialogs.createPage.title', { name })}
      text={t('dialogs.createPage.where', { folder })}
      confirm={t('dialogs.createPage.confirm')}
      onConfirm={async () => {
        const res = await entryCreatePage(dir, name);
        report(res);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        await useVaultStore.getState().refreshTree();
        await openPage(res.value);
      }}
      onClose={onClose}
    />
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/Callout.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Element } from 'hast';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import styles from './Preview.module.css';

/** Callout type → palette color (Obsidian's types and aliases). */
const TONE: Record<string, string> = {
  note: 'blue',
  info: 'blue',
  todo: 'blue',
  abstract: 'blue',
  summary: 'blue',
  tldr: 'blue',
  tip: 'green',
  hint: 'green',
  success: 'green',
  check: 'green',
  done: 'green',
  important: 'purple',
  example: 'purple',
  question: 'yellow',
  help: 'yellow',
  faq: 'yellow',
  warning: 'orange',
  caution: 'orange',
  attention: 'orange',
  failure: 'red',
  fail: 'red',
  missing: 'red',
  danger: 'red',
  error: 'red',
  bug: 'red',
  quote: 'gray',
  cite: 'gray',
};

export function Callout({
  e,
  props,
  children,
}: {
  e: Element;
  props: Record<string, unknown>;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const type = String(e.properties.dataCallout ?? 'note');
  const title =
    String(e.properties.dataTitle ?? '') || type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1);
  const tone = TONE[type] ?? 'gray';
  if (e.properties.dataFoldable === 'true') {
    return (
      <details
        {...props}
        className={styles.callout}
        data-tone={tone}
        open={e.properties.dataFolded !== 'true'}
      >
        <summary className={styles.calloutTitle}>{title}</summary>
        {children}
      </details>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div {...props} className={styles.callout} data-tone={tone} role="note">
      <p className={styles.calloutTitle}>{title}</p>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/Embed.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Element } from 'hast';
import { assetUrl } from '../../ipc';
import { followLink } from '../../stores/links';
import { nameOf } from '../../stores/tree';
import styles from './Preview.module.css';
import type { RenderCtx } from './renderHast';

const IMAGE = new Set(['png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'gif', 'webp', 'svg', 'avif', 'bmp']);
const AUDIO = new Set(['wav', 'mp3', 'm4a']);

const extOf = (path: string) => {
  const name = nameOf(path);
  return name.includes('.') ? name.slice(name.lastIndexOf('.') + 1).toLowerCase() : '';
};

/** `![[…]]` (M2a §4): image, audio, transcluded note, link card, file card or
 *  "Not found". */
export function Embed({ e, ctx, block }: { e: Element; ctx: RenderCtx; block: boolean }) {
  const { t } = ctx;
  const p = e.properties;
  const target = String(p.dataTarget ?? '');
  const heading = typeof p.dataHeading === 'string' ? p.dataHeading : null;
  const resolved = ctx.resolve('embed', target);
  if (resolved === undefined) {
    return <span className={styles.pending}>{t('preview.loading')}</span>;
  }
  if (resolved === null) {
    const ext = extOf(target);
    return (
      <span className={styles.card}>
        <span>{t('preview.notFound', { target })}</span>
        {(ext === '' || ext === 'md') && (
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.cardButton}
            onClick={() =>
              followLink({
                type: 'page',
                kind: 'embed',
                target,
                heading,
                resolved: null,
                source: ctx.path,
              })
            }
          >
            {t('preview.createPage')}
          </button>
        )}
      </span>
    );
  }
  const ext = extOf(resolved);
  if (IMAGE.has(ext)) {
    return (
      <img
        className={styles.embedImage}
        src={assetUrl(resolved)}
        alt={target}
        width={p.dataWidth === undefined ? undefined : Number(p.dataWidth)}
        height={p.dataHeight === undefined ? undefined : Number(p.dataHeight)}
        loading="lazy"
      />
    );
  }
  if (AUDIO.has(ext)) {
    return (
      <audio
        className={styles.embedAudio}
        controls
        src={assetUrl(resolved)}
        aria-label={nameOf(resolved)}
      />
    );
  }
  if (ext === 'md') {
    if (ctx.chain.includes(resolved)) {
      return <span className={styles.card}>{t('preview.cycle')}</span>;
    }
    if (!block || ctx.depth > 0) {
      const label = heading ? `${target} › ${heading}` : target;
      return (
        <a
          href="#"
          className={styles.linkCard}
          data-state="resolved"
          onClick={(ev) => {
            ev.preventDefault();
            followLink({
              type: 'page',
              kind: 'embed',
              target,
              heading,
              resolved,
              source: ctx.path,
            });
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </a>
      );
    }
    return ctx.renderNote(resolved, heading);
  }
  return (
    <span className={styles.card}>
      <span className={styles.fileName}>{nameOf(resolved)}</span>
      <button
        type="button"
        className={styles.cardButton}
        onClick={() => followLink({ type: 'file', path: resolved })}
      >
        {t('preview.openInApp')}
      </button>
    </span>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/Preview.module.css`:

```css
/* Rendered pane (M2a §4). */
.content,
.nested {
  font: 400 15px/1.65 var(--font-body);
  color: var(--color-text-body);
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.content > :first-child,
.nested > :first-child {
  margin-top: 0;
}

.content h1,
.content h2,
.content h3,
.content h4,
.nested h1,
.nested h2,
.nested h3,
.nested h4 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  color: var(--color-text-strong);
  line-height: 1.2;
  margin: 1.4em 0 0.5em;
}

.content pre,
.nested pre {
  font: 400 13px/1.6 var(--font-mono);
  background: var(--color-surface-subtle);
  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 12px 14px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  white-space: pre;
}

.content code,
.nested code {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 0.9em;
}

.content :not(pre) > code,
.nested :not(pre) > code {
  background: var(--color-surface-subtle);
  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  padding: 0 4px;
}

.content blockquote,
.nested blockquote {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 2px 14px;
  border-left: 3px solid var(--color-border-strong);
  color: var(--color-text-muted);
}

.content table,
.nested table {
  border-collapse: collapse;
}

.content th,
.content td,
.nested th,
.nested td {
  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
  padding: 4px 10px;
}

.content hr,
.nested hr {
  border: none;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border-strong);
  margin: 20px 0;
}

.content mark,
.nested mark {
  background: var(--palette-yellow-bg);
  color: var(--color-text);
  border-radius: 3px;
  padding: 0 2px;
}

.content :global(.contains-task-list),
.nested :global(.contains-task-list) {
  list-style: none;
  padding-left: 4px;
}

/* Colored spans: palette tokens only (sanitizer allowlist). */
.content [data-color],
.nested [data-color] {
  color: var(--tone);
}

.content [data-bg],
.nested [data-bg] {
  background: var(--tone-bg);
  border-radius: 3px;
  padding: 0 2px;
}

[data-color='red'],
[data-bg='red'],
[data-tone='red'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-red);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-red-bg);
}
[data-color='orange'],
[data-bg='orange'],
[data-tone='orange'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-orange);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-orange-bg);
}
[data-color='yellow'],
[data-bg='yellow'],
[data-tone='yellow'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-yellow);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-yellow-bg);
}
[data-color='green'],
[data-bg='green'],
[data-tone='green'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-green);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-green-bg);
}
[data-color='blue'],
[data-bg='blue'],
[data-tone='blue'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-blue);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-blue-bg);
}
[data-color='purple'],
[data-bg='purple'],
[data-tone='purple'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-purple);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-purple-bg);
}
[data-color='gray'],
[data-bg='gray'],
[data-tone='gray'] {
  --tone: var(--palette-gray);
  --tone-bg: var(--palette-gray-bg);
}

.link {
  color: var(--color-accent);
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 2px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.unresolved {
  color: var(--color-text-muted);
  text-decoration: underline dashed;
  text-underline-offset: 2px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.externalIcon {
  font-size: 0.8em;
  margin-left: 2px;
}

.tag {
  font: 600 12px var(--font-body);
  color: var(--color-accent);
  background: var(--color-accent-soft);
  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
  padding: 2px 8px;
}

.callout {
  margin: 12px 0;
  padding: 10px 14px;
  border-left: 4px solid var(--tone);
  background: var(--tone-bg);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  color: var(--color-text);
}

.calloutTitle {
  margin: 0 0 4px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--tone);
}

summary.calloutTitle {
  cursor: pointer;
}

/* Columns: a grid that stacks when its container is narrower than 600 px. */
.columns {
  container-type: inline-size;
  margin: 12px 0;
}

.columnsGrid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--columns, 2), minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 20px;
}

@container (max-width: 600px) {
  .columnsGrid {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  }
}

.column > :first-child {
  margin-top: 0;
}

.block {
  margin: 12px 0;
}

.embedImage {
  max-width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
}

.embedAudio {
  width: min(100%, 420px);
}

.noteEmbed {
  border: 1px solid var(--color-border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 10px 14px 12px;
  background: var(--color-surface-subtle);
}

.noteHeader {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}

.noteTitle {
  font: 700 13px var(--font-body);
  color: var(--color-text-muted);
}

.card,
.linkCard,
.remote {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  border: 1px dashed var(--color-border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 6px 10px;
  color: var(--color-text-muted);
  font-size: 13.5px;
}

.linkCard {
  border-style: solid;
  color: var(--color-accent);
  text-decoration: none;
}

.remote::before {
  content: '⊘';
}

.fileName {
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
}

.cardButton {
  font: 600 12.5px var(--font-body);
  color: var(--color-accent);
  background: var(--color-accent-soft);
  border: none;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  padding: 4px 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.pending {
  color: var(--color-text-faint);
  font-size: 13px;
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/Preview.tsx`:

```tsx
import GithubSlugger from 'github-slugger';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { pageRead } from '../../ipc';
import { parsePage } from '../../markdown/parse';
import { toSafeHast } from '../../markdown/sanitize';
import { sectionOf } from '../../markdown/section';
import { openPage } from '../../stores/navigate';
import { usePageStore } from '../../stores/pageStore';
import { nameOf } from '../../stores/tree';
import styles from './Preview.module.css';
import { renderChildren, type RenderCtx } from './renderHast';
import { useDebounced } from './useDebounced';
import { useResolved } from './useResolved';

/** Re-render delay after typing (M2a §4). */
export const RENDER_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

export interface PreviewProps {
  text: string;
  /** Vault path of the page (links and assets resolve from it). */
  path: string;
  /** Render only this heading's section (note embeds). */
  heading?: string | null;
  depth?: number;
  chain?: string[];
}

/** The rendered pane (M2a §4): sanitized Markdown with resolved links and embeds. */
export function Preview({ text, path, heading = null, depth = 0, chain = [] }: PreviewProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const shown = useDebounced(text, RENDER_DEBOUNCE_MS);
  const tree = useMemo(() => {
    const page = parsePage(shown);
    if (heading) page.tree = sectionOf(page.tree, heading) ?? { type: 'root', children: [] };
    return toSafeHast(page, { sourcePath: path });
  }, [shown, path, heading]);
  const resolve = useResolved(path, tree);
  const anchor = usePageStore((s) => (depth === 0 ? s.anchor : null));
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Scroll to the heading a link asked for (by slug or by exact id).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!anchor || !box.current) return;
    const slug = new GithubSlugger().slug(anchor.heading);
    const target = [...box.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[id]')].find(
      (el) => el.id === slug || el.id === anchor.heading,
    );
    target?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'start' });
  }, [anchor, tree]);

  const ctx: RenderCtx = {
    path,
    depth,
    chain: [...chain, path],
    resolve,
    t,
    renderNote: (note, h) => <NoteEmbed key={`${note}#${h}`} path={note} heading={h} ctx={ctx} />,
  };
  return (
    <div ref={box} className={depth ? styles.nested : styles.content}>
      {renderChildren(tree, ctx)}
    </div>
  );
}

/** True once the element has been scrolled into view (always true without
 *  IntersectionObserver). */
function useInView(ref: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>): boolean {
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState(typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (seen || !ref.current) return;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      if (entries.some((e) => e.isIntersecting)) setSeen(true);
    });
    io.observe(ref.current);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [seen, ref]);
  return seen;
}

/** `![[Note]]` / `![[Note#Heading]]`: the note (or section) in a bordered
 *  block with its title and "Open"; loads when scrolled into view. */
function NoteEmbed({
  path,
  heading,
  ctx,
}: {
  path: string;
  heading: string | null;
  ctx: RenderCtx;
}) {
  const { t } = ctx;
  const ref = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const visible = useInView(ref);
  const [text, setText] = useState<string | null | undefined>(undefined);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return;
    let live = true;
    void pageRead(path).then((res) => {
      if (live) setText(res.ok ? res.value.text : null);
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [visible, path]);
  const name = nameOf(path).replace(/\.md$/i, '');
  const title = heading ? `${name} › ${heading}` : name;
  const missing = text === null || (text && heading && !sectionOf(parsePage(text).tree, heading));
  return (
    <section ref={ref} className={styles.noteEmbed} aria-label={title}>
      <header className={styles.noteHeader}>
        <span className={styles.noteTitle}>{title}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.cardButton}
          onClick={() => void openPage(path, heading)}
        >
          {t('preview.open')}
        </button>
      </header>
      {text === undefined ? (
        <p className={styles.pending}>{t('preview.loading')}</p>
      ) : missing ? (
        <p className={styles.card}>{t('preview.notFound', { target: title })}</p>
      ) : (
        <Preview
          text={text ?? ''}
          path={path}
          heading={heading}
          depth={ctx.depth + 1}
          chain={ctx.chain}
        />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/renderHast.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Element, ElementContent, Properties, Root } from 'hast';
import type { TFunction } from 'i18next';
import { createElement, type MouseEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { assetUrl } from '../../ipc';
import { followLink } from '../../stores/links';
import { Callout } from './Callout';
import { Embed } from './Embed';
import styles from './Preview.module.css';
import type { Resolve } from './useResolved';

export interface RenderCtx {
  /** Page being rendered. */
  path: string;
  /** 0 = the page itself, 1+ = inside a note embed. */
  depth: number;
  /** Pages on the embed path, outermost first (cycle detection). */
  chain: string[];
  resolve: Resolve;
  t: TFunction;
  /** Transcludes a note (provided by Preview, which renders it recursively). */
  renderNote: (path: string, heading: string | null) => ReactNode;
  /** Accessible name for a task checkbox (the list item's text). */
  taskLabel?: string;
}

const VOID = new Set(['br', 'hr', 'img', 'input', 'source']);

const kebab = (s: string) =>
  s.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (c, i: number) => (i ? '-' : '') + c.toLowerCase());

/** hast properties → React props (`className` arrays, `dataFoo` → `data-foo`). */
export function reactProps(p: Properties): Record<string, unknown> {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p)) {
    if (v === undefined || v === null) continue;
    if (k === 'className') out.className = Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(' ') : String(v);
    else if (/^data[A-Z]/.test(k)) out[`data-${kebab(k.slice(4))}`] = String(v);
    else out[k] = v;
  }
  return out;
}

export function textOf(n: ElementContent | Root): string {
  if (n.type === 'text') return n.value;
  if (n.type === 'element' || n.type === 'root')
    return n.children.map((c) => textOf(c as ElementContent)).join('');
  return '';
}

const click = (run: () => void) => (e: MouseEvent) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  run();
};

/** A paragraph holding nothing but one embed renders as a block embed. */
function loneEmbed(e: Element): Element | null {
  const els = e.children.filter((c) => c.type === 'element');
  const rest = e.children.filter((c) => c.type === 'text' && c.value.trim());
  return els.length === 1 && !rest.length && els[0].properties.dataLiber === 'embed' ? els[0] : null;
}

function pageLink(e: Element, key: number, ctx: RenderCtx): ReactNode {
  const p = e.properties;
  const kind = p.dataLiber === 'wikilink' ? 'wiki' : 'markdown';
  const target = typeof p.dataTarget === 'string' ? p.dataTarget : '';
  const heading = typeof p.dataHeading === 'string' ? p.dataHeading : null;
  const resolved = target ? ctx.resolve(kind, target) : null;
  const state = resolved === undefined ? 'pending' : resolved === null ? 'unresolved' : 'resolved';
  return (
    <a
      key={key}
      href="#"
      className={state === 'unresolved' ? styles.unresolved : styles.link}
      data-liber={p.dataLiber}
      data-state={state}
      onClick={click(() => {
        if (resolved === undefined) return;
        if (!target && heading) followLink({ type: 'anchor', heading });
        else followLink({ type: 'page', kind, target, heading, resolved, source: ctx.path });
      })}
    >
      {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
    </a>
  );
}

function renderElement(e: Element, key: number, ctx: RenderCtx): ReactNode {
  const p = e.properties;
  const { t } = ctx;
  switch (p.dataLiber) {
    case 'wikilink':
    case 'link':
      return pageLink(e, key, ctx);
    case 'external': {
      const url = String(p.href ?? '');
      return (
        <a
          key={key}
          href="#"
          className={styles.link}
          data-liber="external"
          title={url}
          onClick={click(() => followLink({ type: 'external', url }))}
        >
          {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
          <span aria-hidden="true" className={styles.externalIcon}>
            ↗
          </span>
        </a>
      );
    }
    case 'anchor': {
      const heading = decodeURIComponent(String(p.href ?? '').slice(1));
      return (
        <a
          key={key}
          href="#"
          className={styles.link}
          data-liber="anchor"
          onClick={click(() => followLink({ type: 'anchor', heading }))}
        >
          {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
        </a>
      );
    }
    case 'embed':
      return <Embed key={key} e={e} ctx={ctx} block={false} />;
    case 'remote-blocked':
      return (
        <span key={key} className={styles.remote} title={String(p.dataUrl ?? '')}>
          {t('preview.remoteBlocked')}
        </span>
      );
    case 'missing':
      return (
        <span key={key} className={styles.card}>
          {t('preview.notFound', { target: String(p.alt ?? p.src ?? '') })}
        </span>
      );
    case 'callout':
      return (
        <Callout key={key} e={e} props={reactProps(p)}>
          {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
        </Callout>
      );
    case 'columns': {
      const count = e.children.filter((c) => c.type === 'element').length;
      return (
        <div key={key} className={styles.columns} {...reactProps(p)}>
          <div className={styles.columnsGrid} style={{ ['--columns' as string]: count }}>
            {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    case 'column':
      return (
        <div key={key} className={styles.column} {...reactProps(p)}>
          {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
        </div>
      );
    case 'tag':
      return (
        <span key={key} className={styles.tag} {...reactProps(p)}>
          {renderChildren(e, ctx)}
        </span>
      );
  }
  if (e.tagName === 'p') {
    const embed = loneEmbed(e);
    if (embed) {
      return (
        <div key={key} className={styles.block} {...reactProps(p)}>
          <Embed e={embed} ctx={ctx} block />
        </div>
      );
    }
  }
  const asset = typeof p.dataAssetPath === 'string' ? p.dataAssetPath : null;
  if (e.tagName === 'img') {
    if (!asset && typeof p.src !== 'string') return null;
    return (
      <img
        key={key}
        {...reactProps(p)}
        src={asset ? assetUrl(asset) : String(p.src)}
        alt={String(p.alt ?? '')}
        loading="lazy"
      />
    );
  }
  if (asset && (e.tagName === 'audio' || e.tagName === 'video' || e.tagName === 'source')) {
    return createElement(e.tagName, {
      key,
      ...reactProps(p),
      src: assetUrl(asset),
      controls: e.tagName === 'source' ? undefined : true,
    });
  }
  if (e.tagName === 'input') {
    return (
      <input
        key={key}
        type="checkbox"
        checked={Boolean(p.checked)}
        disabled
        readOnly
        aria-label={ctx.taskLabel}
      />
    );
  }
  if (e.tagName === 'li' && String(p.className ?? '').includes('task-list-item')) {
    const label = textOf(e).trim();
    return createElement(
      'li',
      { key, ...reactProps(p) },
      renderChildren(e, { ...ctx, taskLabel: label }),
    );
  }
  return createElement(
    e.tagName,
    { key, ...reactProps(p) },
    VOID.has(e.tagName) ? undefined : renderChildren(e, ctx),
  );
}

export function renderChildren(parent: Root | Element, ctx: RenderCtx): ReactNode[] {
  return parent.children.map((c, i) => {
    if (c.type === 'text') return c.value;
    if (c.type === 'element') return renderElement(c, i, ctx);
    return null;
  });
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/useDebounced.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/** `value`, updated `ms` after it stops changing (the first value is immediate). */
export function useDebounced<T>(value: T, ms: number): T {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setShown(value), ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, ms]);
  return shown;
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/useResolved.ts`:

```ts
import type { Element, Root } from 'hast';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { linksResolve, type LinkKind, type LinkTarget } from '../../ipc';
import { useVaultStore } from '../../stores/vaultStore';

/** `kind` + `target` → vault path, `null` (doesn't exist) or `undefined` (not known yet). */
export type Resolve = (kind: LinkKind, target: string) => string | null | undefined;

const keyOf = (t: LinkTarget) => `${t.kind}\u0000${t.target}`;

/** Link targets in a render tree, as `links_resolve` expects them. Markdown
 *  link targets are already vault paths; a leading `/` makes them root-based. */
export function linkTargets(tree: Root): LinkTarget[] {
  const out = new Map<string, LinkTarget>();
  const walk = (n: Root | Element) => {
    for (const c of n.children) {
      if (c.type !== 'element') continue;
      const { dataLiber, dataTarget } = c.properties;
      if (typeof dataTarget === 'string') {
        const t: LinkTarget | null =
          dataLiber === 'wikilink'
            ? { kind: 'wiki', target: dataTarget }
            : dataLiber === 'embed'
              ? { kind: 'embed', target: dataTarget }
              : dataLiber === 'link'
                ? { kind: 'markdown', target: `/${dataTarget}` }
                : null;
        if (t) out.set(keyOf(t), t);
      }
      walk(c);
    }
  };
  walk(tree);
  return [...out.values()];
}

/** Resolves the tree's links against the live file set (PG-05); re-resolves
 *  when the vault's files change. */
export function useResolved(source: string, tree: Root): Resolve {
  const targets = useMemo(() => linkTargets(tree), [tree]);
  const key = targets.map(keyOf).join('\u0001');
  const nodes = useVaultStore((s) => s.nodes);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ key: string; map: Map<string, string | null> }>({
    key: '',
    map: new Map(),
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    if (!targets.length) return;
    void linksResolve(source, targets).then((res) => {
      if (!live || !res.ok) return;
      setResult({ key, map: new Map(targets.map((t, i) => [keyOf(t), res.value[i] ?? null])) });
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
    // `key` stands for `targets`; `nodes` re-resolves after file changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [source, key, nodes]);
  return (kind, target) => {
    const k = keyOf({ kind, target: kind === 'markdown' ? `/${target}` : target });
    return result.map.has(k) ? result.map.get(k) : undefined;
  };
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/section.ts`:

```ts
import type { Heading, Root, RootContent } from 'mdast';

function text(node: RootContent): string {
  if ('value' in node && typeof node.value === 'string') return node.value;
  if ('children' in node) return (node.children as RootContent[]).map(text).join('');
  return '';
}

const norm = (s: string) => s.trim().toLowerCase();

/** The section under `heading` (case-insensitive): the heading itself and
 *  everything until the next heading of the same or a higher level. */
export function sectionOf(tree: Root, heading: string): Root | null {
  const start = tree.children.findIndex(
    (n) => n.type === 'heading' && norm(text(n)) === norm(heading),
  );
  if (start < 0) return null;
  const depth = (tree.children[start] as Heading).depth;
  let end = start + 1;
  while (end < tree.children.length) {
    const n = tree.children[end];
    if (n.type === 'heading' && n.depth <= depth) break;
    end++;
  }
  return { type: 'root', children: tree.children.slice(start, end) };
}
```

```ts
import type { LinkKind } from '../ipc';
import { openPage } from './navigate';
import { usePageStore } from './pageStore';
import { useUiStore } from './uiStore';

/** What a click on a link or embed card leads to (M2a §3.4). */
export type LinkAction =
  | {
      type: 'page';
      kind: LinkKind;
      /** As written in the source (wiki target or vault path). */
      target: string;
      heading: string | null;
      /** Vault path the target resolves to; `null` = doesn't exist. */
      resolved: string | null;
      /** Page the link is on. */
      source: string;
    }
  | { type: 'external'; url: string }
  | { type: 'anchor'; heading: string }
  | { type: 'file'; path: string };

const isPage = (path: string) => /\.md$/i.test(path);

export function followLink(action: LinkAction): void {
  const ui = useUiStore.getState();
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'external':
      ui.openDialog({ type: 'openExternal', url: action.url });
      return;
    case 'file':
      ui.openDialog({ type: 'openFile', path: action.path });
      return;
    case 'anchor':
      usePageStore.getState().reveal(action.heading);
      return;
    case 'page':
      if (action.resolved === null) {
        ui.openDialog({ type: 'createPage', source: action.source, target: action.target });
      } else if (isPage(action.resolved)) {
        void openPage(action.resolved, action.heading);
      } else {
        ui.openDialog({ type: 'openFile', path: action.resolved });
      }
  }
}
```

### Task 7: CodeMirror — Live Preview, Source, Split, incremental re-parse, scroll sync

Spec §3.2, §3.4, §7. A full remark parse of a 5,000-line note takes ~110 ms, so the spec's "< 50 ms" needs `reparse` (only the top-level blocks around the edit, ± one neighbour, when blank-line separated; anything non-local falls back to a full parse; it takes ownership of the previous model). Live Preview is a `StateField` (block widgets must come from state), re-parsed by a debounced `ViewPlugin`; external text is applied as a minimal change outside the undo history.

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/Editor.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { undo } from '@codemirror/commands';
import { EditorView } from '@codemirror/view';
import { act, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { clearMocks } from '../../ipc/testing';
import { fakeDisk, type FakeDisk } from '../../test/fakeDisk';
import { Editor } from './Editor';
import { AUTOSAVE_MS, PageDoc } from './pageDoc';

const hooks = { onChange: () => undefined, onConflictCopy: () => undefined };

async function load(path: string) {
  const res = await PageDoc.load(path, hooks);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(res.error.message);
  return res.value;
}

const viewOf = (): EditorView =>
  EditorView.findFromDOM(document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.cm-editor')!)!;

/** Types `text` at `at` (default: the cursor), as a user would. */
const type = (text: string, at?: number) =>
  act(() => {
    const v = viewOf();
    const from = at ?? v.state.selection.main.head;
    v.dispatch({
      changes: { from, insert: text },
      selection: { anchor: from + text.length },
      userEvent: 'input.type',
    });
  });

let disk: FakeDisk;

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  clearMocks();
});

describe('Editor (M2a §3.2–3.3)', () => {
  it('ED-07: switching modes keeps the text, the selection and the undo history', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': '# A\n\nbody\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    const { rerender } = render(<Editor doc={doc} mode="livePreview" label="A" />);
    const view = viewOf();
    await type('!', 3);
    rerender(<Editor doc={doc} mode="source" label="A" />);
    expect(viewOf()).toBe(view);
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('# A!\n\nbody\n');
    expect(view.state.selection.main.head).toBe(4);
    expect(view.dom).toHaveClass('cm-liber-source');
    rerender(<Editor doc={doc} mode="split" label="A" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Preview' })).toHaveTextContent('A!');
    act(() => void undo(view));
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('# A\n\nbody\n');
  });

  it('typing reaches the page document, which autosaves', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(<Editor doc={doc} mode="source" label="A" />);
    await type('two ', 0);
    expect(doc.text).toBe('two one\n');
    await act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(AUTOSAVE_MS));
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('two one\n');
  });

  it('leaving the editor (blur) saves at once, without waiting for the debounce', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(<Editor doc={doc} mode="source" label="A" />);
    await type('two ', 0);
    await act(async () => {
      viewOf().contentDOM.dispatchEvent(new FocusEvent('blur'));
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    });
    expect(disk.files.get('A.md')).toBe('two one\n');
  });

  it('review focus: undo after an external merge undoes only my edit', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\nthree\nfour\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    render(<Editor doc={doc} mode="source" label="A" />);
    await type('!', 3);
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'one\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
    await act(() => doc.sync());
    const view = viewOf();
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('one!\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
    act(() => void undo(view));
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('one\ntwo\nthree\nFOUR\n');
  });

  it('an external reload keeps the cursor on the same text', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'one\ntwo\n' });
    const doc = await load('A.md');
    render(<Editor doc={doc} mode="source" label="A" />);
    act(() => viewOf().dispatch({ selection: { anchor: 6 } })); // in "two"
    disk.files.set('A.md', 'zero\none\ntwo\n');
    await act(() => doc.sync());
    expect(viewOf().state.selection.main.head).toBe(11);
  });

  it('each page keeps its own undo history; a background change shows on return', async () => {
    disk = fakeDisk({ 'A.md': 'aaa\n', 'B.md': 'bbb\n' });
    const a = await load('A.md');
    const b = await load('B.md');
    const { rerender } = render(<Editor doc={a} mode="source" label="A" />);
    await type('1', 0);
    rerender(<Editor doc={b} mode="source" label="B" />);
    expect(viewOf().state.doc.toString()).toBe('bbb\n');
    await type('2', 0);
    await act(() => a.flush());
    disk.files.set('A.md', '1aaa\nmore\n');
    await act(() => a.sync()); // A is not shown: no editor attached
    rerender(<Editor doc={a} mode="source" label="A" />);
    const view = viewOf();
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('1aaa\nmore\n');
    act(() => void undo(view));
    expect(view.state.doc.toString()).toBe('aaa\nmore\n');
    expect(b.text).toBe('2bbb\n');
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/livePreview.test.ts`:

```ts
import { EditorSelection, EditorState } from '@codemirror/state';
import { EditorView } from '@codemirror/view';
import { act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { clearMocks, mockIPC } from '../../ipc/testing';
import { usePageStore } from '../../stores/pageStore';
import {
  followLinkAtCursor,
  LARGE_NOTE_CHARS,
  livePreview,
  pagePath,
  REPARSE_MS,
} from './livePreview';

let view: EditorView | null = null;

/** Creates the editor inside act(): block widgets render with React. */
function editor(doc: string, cursor = 0): EditorView {
  act(() => {
    view = new EditorView({
      parent: document.body.appendChild(document.createElement('div')),
      state: EditorState.create({
        doc,
        selection: EditorSelection.cursor(cursor),
        extensions: [livePreview(), pagePath.of(() => 'Notes/Page.md')],
      }),
    });
  });
  return view!;
}

/** Visible text of line `n` (1-based), as rendered. */
const line = (v: EditorView, n: number) =>
  (v.contentDOM.querySelectorAll('.cm-line')[n - 1] as HTMLElement | undefined)?.textContent;

const moveTo = (v: EditorView, pos: number) =>
  act(() => v.dispatch({ selection: { anchor: pos } }));

afterEach(() => {
  view?.destroy();
  view = null;
  vi.useRealTimers();
  clearMocks();
  document.body.innerHTML = '';
});

describe('Live Preview (M2a §3.2)', () => {
  it('hides markup except on lines touched by the selection', async () => {
    const v = editor('# Title\n\n**bold** and [[Sparda|alias]] and [md](x.md) `code`\n');
    expect(line(v, 1)).toBe('# Title');
    expect(line(v, 3)).toBe('bold and alias and md code');
    await moveTo(v, v.state.doc.line(3).from);
    expect(line(v, 1)).toBe('Title');
    expect(line(v, 3)).toBe('**bold** and [[Sparda|alias]] and [md](x.md) `code`');
  });

  it('styles headings by level and inline formatting by kind', () => {
    const v = editor('x\n\n## Two\n\n*em* ==hi== ~~no~~ #tag\n');
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-h2')).toHaveTextContent('Two');
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-em')).toHaveTextContent('em');
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-highlight')).toHaveTextContent('hi');
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-strike')).toHaveTextContent('no');
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-tag')).toHaveTextContent('#tag');
  });

  it('review focus: positions are UTF-16 offsets, so astral characters keep markup aligned', () => {
    const v = editor('x\n\n😀 **b** [[X|y]] 中文 ==m== 👩‍👩‍👧 *e*\n');
    expect(line(v, 3)).toBe('😀 b y 中文 m 👩‍👩‍👧 e');
  });

  it('task checkboxes are clickable and toggle the source', async () => {
    const v = editor('- [ ] todo\n\nother\n', 13);
    const box = v.contentDOM.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('input[type="checkbox"]')!;
    expect(box.checked).toBe(false);
    await act(() => box.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown', { bubbles: true })));
    expect(v.state.doc.line(1).text).toBe('- [x] todo');
  });

  it('tables, callouts and rules become rendered widgets until the cursor enters them', async () => {
    const doc = 'x\n\n| a | b |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |\n\n> [!tip] Hint\n> body\n\n---\n';
    const v = editor(doc);
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelectorAll('.cm-liber-widget')).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-widget table')).not.toBeNull();
    await moveTo(v, doc.indexOf('| 1'));
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelectorAll('.cm-liber-widget')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(v.contentDOM.textContent).toContain('| 1 | 2 |');
  });

  it('re-parses after typing (debounced) and hides the new markup', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const v = editor('x\n\nend\n', 0);
    act(() => v.dispatch({ changes: { from: 1, insert: ' **new**' } }));
    await moveTo(v, v.state.doc.length);
    await act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(REPARSE_MS));
    expect(line(v, 1)).toBe('x new');
  });

  // Parses a real 200 KB note: seconds when the whole suite runs in parallel.
  it('large notes get the reduced set: headings, emphasis and links only', () => {
    // Realistic: short paragraphs (one giant paragraph is a much slower, separate case).
    const para = 'plain text line one\nplain text line two\n\n';
    const filler = para.repeat(Math.ceil(LARGE_NOTE_CHARS / para.length) + 10);
    const v = editor(`x\n\n# Big\n\n- [ ] task **b**\n\n| a |\n|---|\n| 1 |\n\n${filler}`);
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-h1')).not.toBeNull();
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('.cm-liber-widget')).toBeNull();
    expect(v.contentDOM.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(line(v, 5)).toBe('- [ ] task b');
  }, 30_000);

  it('Mod-Enter follows the link under the cursor (keyboard ⌘-click)', async () => {
    mockIPC((cmd) => {
      if (cmd === 'links_resolve') return ['Notes/Sparda.md'];
      if (cmd === 'page_get') throw { kind: 'notFound', message: 'x' };
      if (cmd === 'page_read') return { text: '', hash: 'h' };
      return null;
    });
    const v = editor('See [[Sparda#Weapons]] now\n', 8);
    expect(followLinkAtCursor(v)).toBe(true);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(usePageStore.getState().path).toBe('Notes/Sparda.md'));
    expect(usePageStore.getState().anchor?.heading).toBe('Weapons');
    await moveTo(v, 1);
    expect(followLinkAtCursor(v)).toBe(false);
  });
});

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/scrollSync.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { blockForOffset, offsetAtTop } from './scrollSync';

/** A pane with blocks at fixed vertical positions (jsdom has no layout). */
function pane(blocks: [start: number, end: number, top: number, height: number, depth?: number][]) {
  const root = document.createElement('div');
  Object.defineProperty(root, 'getBoundingClientRect', { value: () => ({ top: 0, height: 500 }) });
  let parent = root;
  for (const [start, end, top, height, depth] of blocks) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.dataset.sourceStart = String(start);
    el.dataset.sourceEnd = String(end);
    Object.defineProperty(el, 'getBoundingClientRect', {
      value: () => ({ top: top - root.scrollTop, height }),
    });
    (depth ? parent : root).appendChild(el);
    parent = el;
  }
  return root;
}

describe('Split scroll sync (M2a §3.2)', () => {
  const p = pane([
    [0, 10, 0, 40],
    [12, 200, 50, 400], // a list…
    [14, 60, 60, 100, 1], // …and its first item
    [202, 220, 460, 30],
  ]);

  it('maps a source offset to the deepest block containing it', () => {
    expect(blockForOffset(p, 5)?.dataset.sourceStart).toBe('0');
    expect(blockForOffset(p, 20)?.dataset.sourceStart).toBe('14');
    expect(blockForOffset(p, 100)?.dataset.sourceStart).toBe('12');
    expect(blockForOffset(p, 11)?.dataset.sourceStart).toBe('12'); // between blocks: the next
  });

  it('maps the top of the pane back to a source offset', () => {
    p.scrollTop = 0;
    expect(offsetAtTop(p)).toBe(0);
    p.scrollTop = 70;
    expect(offsetAtTop(p)).toBe(14);
    p.scrollTop = 300;
    expect(offsetAtTop(p)).toBe(12);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/incremental.test.ts`:

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { reparse } from './incremental';
import { parsePage } from './parse';

/** Deterministic PRNG (mulberry32) so failures reproduce. */
function rng(seed: number) {
  return () => {
    seed |= 0;
    seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

const block = (i: number) =>
  `## Section ${i}\n\nSome *text* with **bold**, a [[Link ${i}|alias]] and #tag${i % 7} plus ==mark==.\n\n` +
  `- [ ] task ${i}\n- item with [md](page${i}.md) and 😀 emoji\n\n> [!note] Callout ${i}\n> body\n\n` +
  `| a | b |\n|---|---|\n| ${i} | x |\n\n1. one\n2. two\n\n`;

function note(lines: number): string {
  let src = '# Big note\n\n';
  for (let i = 0; src.split('\n').length < lines; i++) src += block(i);
  return src;
}

/** Typing-like edits; `NONLOCAL_PIECES` add constructs that force a full parse. */
const LOCAL_PIECES = [
  'a',
  ' ',
  '\n',
  '\n\n',
  '#',
  '# ',
  '*',
  '**',
  '- ',
  '1. ',
  '[[',
  ']]',
  '==',
  '>',
  '|',
  '---',
  '😀',
  '\t',
  '  - ',
  '[!tip]',
];
const NONLOCAL_PIECES = ['`', '```', '%%', '<b>', '[x]: y', ':::'];

/** Applies `steps` seeded random edits; after each, the incremental model must
 *  equal a full parse. Returns how often untouched blocks were reused. */
function fuzz(seed: number, pieces: string[], steps: number): number {
  const next = rng(seed);
  let text = note(60);
  let page = parsePage(text);
  let reused = 0;
  for (let step = 0; step < steps; step++) {
    const firstBlock = page.tree.children[0];
    const at = Math.floor(next() * (text.length + 1));
    const edited =
      next() < 0.6
        ? text.slice(0, at) + pieces[Math.floor(next() * pieces.length)] + text.slice(at)
        : text.slice(0, at) + text.slice(Math.min(text.length, at + 1 + Math.floor(next() * 6)));
    page = reparse(page, text, edited);
    text = edited;
    expect(page, `seed ${seed} step ${step}`).toEqual(parsePage(text));
    if (page.tree.children[0] === firstBlock) reused++;
  }
  return reused;
}

describe('incremental re-parse (M2a §7: typing in a 5,000-line note)', () => {
  it('typing-like edits: equals the full parse, and mostly re-parses locally', () => {
    expect(fuzz(42, LOCAL_PIECES, 200)).toBeGreaterThan(130);
  }, 60_000);

  it('edits adding fences, HTML, comments or definitions still equal the full parse', () => {
    fuzz(7, [...LOCAL_PIECES, ...NONLOCAL_PIECES], 200);
  }, 60_000);

  it('re-parses one typed character in a 5,000-line note in under 50 ms', () => {
    const text = note(5000);
    let page = parsePage(text);
    let current = text;
    const times: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const at = current.indexOf('Some *text*', Math.floor(current.length / 2) + i * 997);
      const typed = current.slice(0, at) + 'x' + current.slice(at);
      const t0 = performance.now();
      page = reparse(page, current, typed);
      times.push(performance.now() - t0);
      current = typed;
    }
    expect(page).toEqual(parsePage(current));
    expect(Math.min(...times)).toBeLessThan(50);
  }, 30_000); // two full parses + a deep compare of a 5,000-line tree

  it('returns the previous model for identical text', () => {
    const page = parsePage('# A\n');
    expect(reparse(page, '# A\n', '# A\n')).toBe(page);
  });
});
````

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/Editor.tsx`:

```tsx
import { defaultKeymap, history, historyKeymap } from '@codemirror/commands';
import { markdown } from '@codemirror/lang-markdown';
import { HighlightStyle, syntaxHighlighting } from '@codemirror/language';
import { Compartment, EditorState, Transaction, type Extension } from '@codemirror/state';
import { drawSelection, EditorView, keymap } from '@codemirror/view';
import { tags } from '@lezer/highlight';
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { EditorMode } from '../../ipc';
import { Preview } from '../preview/Preview';
import styles from './Editor.module.css';
import { followLinkAtCursor, livePreview, pagePath } from './livePreview';
import type { PageDoc } from './pageDoc';
import { blockForOffset, offsetAtTop, topIn } from './scrollSync';

/** Each page keeps its CodeMirror state (text, selection, undo) while the app
 *  runs, so switching pages or modes never loses history (ED-07). */
const states = new WeakMap<PageDoc, EditorState>();
const modeSlot = new Compartment();
const labelSlot = new Compartment();

const sourceHighlight = HighlightStyle.define([
  { tag: tags.heading, fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--color-text-strong)' },
  { tag: tags.emphasis, fontStyle: 'italic' },
  { tag: tags.strong, fontWeight: '700' },
  { tag: tags.strikethrough, textDecoration: 'line-through' },
  { tag: tags.link, color: 'var(--color-accent)' },
  { tag: tags.url, color: 'var(--color-text-muted)' },
  { tag: tags.quote, color: 'var(--color-text-muted)' },
  {
    tag: [tags.processingInstruction, tags.meta, tags.contentSeparator],
    color: 'var(--color-text-faint)',
  },
]);

const theme = EditorView.theme({
  '&': { height: '100%', color: 'var(--color-text)', backgroundColor: 'transparent' },
  '&.cm-focused': { outline: 'none' },
  '.cm-scroller': { fontFamily: 'var(--font-body)', fontSize: '15.5px', lineHeight: '1.7' },
  '&.cm-liber-source .cm-scroller': {
    fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)',
    fontSize: '13.5px',
    lineHeight: '1.6',
  },
  '.cm-content': { padding: '20px 0 40vh', caretColor: 'var(--color-text)' },
  '.cm-cursor': { borderLeftColor: 'var(--color-text)' },
  '.cm-selectionBackground, &.cm-focused .cm-selectionBackground': {
    backgroundColor: 'var(--color-accent-soft)',
  },
});

function modeExtensions(mode: EditorMode): Extension {
  return mode === 'livePreview'
    ? [livePreview(), EditorView.editorAttributes.of({ class: 'cm-liber-live' })]
    : [
        syntaxHighlighting(sourceHighlight),
        EditorView.editorAttributes.of({ class: 'cm-liber-source' }),
      ];
}

const labelled = (label: string) => EditorView.contentAttributes.of({ 'aria-label': label });

/** The smallest change turning `from` into `to`. */
export function diffText(from: string, to: string) {
  if (from === to) return null;
  let start = 0;
  const max = Math.min(from.length, to.length);
  while (start < max && from.charCodeAt(start) === to.charCodeAt(start)) start++;
  let a = from.length;
  let b = to.length;
  while (a > start && b > start && from.charCodeAt(a - 1) === to.charCodeAt(b - 1)) {
    a--;
    b--;
  }
  return { from: start, to: a, insert: to.slice(start, b) };
}

/** Text that changed outside the editor (reload, merge): applied as a minimal
 *  change outside the undo history, so the selection maps through it and undo
 *  only ever reverts the user's own edits. */
function applyExternal(view: EditorView, text: string) {
  const changes = diffText(view.state.doc.toString(), text);
  if (!changes) return;
  view.dispatch({
    changes,
    annotations: [Transaction.addToHistory.of(false), Transaction.remote.of(true)],
  });
}

export interface EditorProps {
  doc: PageDoc;
  mode: EditorMode;
  /** Accessible name of the text area (the page title). */
  label: string;
}

/** The page editor: Live Preview, Source, or Split (Source + rendered pane). */
export function Editor({ doc, mode, label }: EditorProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const pane = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const view = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
  const current = useRef(doc);
  const split = mode === 'split';
  // The text for the Split pane (cheap to track: the pane renders only in Split).
  const [text, setText] = useState(doc.text);

  const stateFor = (d: PageDoc): EditorState =>
    states.get(d) ??
    EditorState.create({
      doc: d.text,
      extensions: [
        history(),
        drawSelection(),
        EditorView.lineWrapping,
        markdown(),
        keymap.of([
          { key: 'Mod-Enter', run: followLinkAtCursor },
          ...defaultKeymap,
          ...historyKeymap,
        ]),
        pagePath.of(() => current.current.path),
        theme,
        labelSlot.of(labelled(label)),
        modeSlot.of(modeExtensions(mode)),
        // Autosave also when the editor loses focus (M2a §3.3).
        EditorView.domEventHandlers({
          blur: () => {
            void current.current.flush();
          },
        }),
        EditorView.updateListener.of((u) => {
          if (!u.docChanged) return;
          const next = u.state.doc.toString();
          current.current.edit(next);
          setText(next);
        }),
      ],
    });

  // One view for the component's lifetime; page states are swapped into it.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const v = new EditorView({ parent: host.current!, state: stateFor(current.current) });
    view.current = v;
    return () => {
      states.set(current.current, v.state);
      current.current.sink = null;
      v.destroy();
      view.current = null;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const v = view.current!;
    if (current.current !== doc) {
      states.set(current.current, v.state);
      current.current.sink = null;
      current.current = doc;
      v.setState(stateFor(doc));
    }
    doc.sink = (next) => applyExternal(v, next);
    // Changed while it was in the background (reload or merge without a view).
    applyExternal(v, doc.text);
    v.dispatch({
      effects: [modeSlot.reconfigure(modeExtensions(mode)), labelSlot.reconfigure(labelled(label))],
    });
    setText(doc.text);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [doc]);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    view.current?.dispatch({
      effects: [modeSlot.reconfigure(modeExtensions(mode)), labelSlot.reconfigure(labelled(label))],
    });
  }, [mode, label]);

  // Split: the editor and the rendered pane follow each other's scrolling.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!split || !view.current || !pane.current) return;
    const v = view.current;
    const p = pane.current;
    let lock: 'editor' | 'pane' | null = null;
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    const hold = (who: 'editor' | 'pane') => {
      lock = who;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(() => (lock = null), 120);
    };
    const fromEditor = () => {
      if (lock === 'pane') return;
      hold('editor');
      const el = blockForOffset(p, v.lineBlockAtHeight(v.scrollDOM.scrollTop).from);
      if (el) p.scrollTop = topIn(p, el);
    };
    const fromPane = () => {
      if (lock === 'editor') return;
      hold('pane');
      const offset = offsetAtTop(p);
      if (offset !== null) {
        v.dispatch({ effects: EditorView.scrollIntoView(offset, { y: 'start' }) });
      }
    };
    v.scrollDOM.addEventListener('scroll', fromEditor);
    p.addEventListener('scroll', fromPane);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      v.scrollDOM.removeEventListener('scroll', fromEditor);
      p.removeEventListener('scroll', fromPane);
    };
  }, [split]);

  return (
    <div className={split ? styles.split : styles.single}>
      <div ref={host} className={mode === 'livePreview' ? styles.live : styles.source} />
      {split && (
        <div ref={pane} className={styles.pane} role="region" aria-label={t('editor.preview')}>
          <Preview text={text} path={doc.path} />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/livePreview.ts`:

```ts
// Live Preview (M2a §3.2): the Markdown model drives CodeMirror decorations.
// Markup is hidden except on lines the selection touches; blocks render as
// preview widgets; large notes get a reduced set.
import {
  Facet,
  StateEffect,
  StateField,
  type EditorState,
  type Extension,
  type Range,
} from '@codemirror/state';
import {
  Decoration,
  EditorView,
  ViewPlugin,
  type DecorationSet,
  type ViewUpdate,
} from '@codemirror/view';
import type { Heading, Image, Link, ListItem, Node, Parent, RootContent } from 'mdast';
import { linksResolve, type LinkKind } from '../../ipc';
import { reparse } from '../../markdown/incremental';
import { parsePage } from '../../markdown/parse';
import { vaultJoin } from '../../markdown/sanitize';
import type { Embed, ParsedPage, WikiLink } from '../../markdown/types';
import { rangeOf } from '../../markdown/walk';
import { followLink } from '../../stores/links';
import { CheckboxWidget, PreviewWidget } from './widgets';

/** Notes above this size get the reduced decoration set (M2a §3.2). */
export const LARGE_NOTE_CHARS = 200 * 1024;
/** Re-parse delay after typing; positions are mapped until then. */
export const REPARSE_MS = 150;

/** The open page's vault path (links and embeds resolve from it). */
export const pagePath = Facet.define<() => string, () => string>({
  combine: (values) => values[0] ?? (() => ''),
});

interface Model {
  page: ParsedPage;
  /** The text `page` was parsed from. */
  text: string;
  /** Doc changed since `page` was parsed (decorations are mapped). */
  stale: boolean;
  /** Lines the selection touched when `decos` were built. */
  active: string;
  decos: DecorationSet;
}

const setModel = StateEffect.define<{ page: ParsedPage; text: string }>();

const hide = Decoration.replace({});
const mark = (cls: string) => Decoration.mark({ class: cls });
const MARKS = {
  emphasis: mark('cm-liber-em'),
  strong: mark('cm-liber-strong'),
  delete: mark('cm-liber-strike'),
  highlight: mark('cm-liber-highlight'),
  inlineCode: mark('cm-liber-code'),
  link: mark('cm-liber-link'),
  tag: mark('cm-liber-tag'),
};
const lineClass = (cls: string) => Decoration.line({ class: cls });

const BLOCK_WIDGET = new Set(['table', 'callout', 'columns', 'thematicBreak']);
const TASK = /^(\s*(?:[-*+]|\d+[.)])[ \t]+)\[([ xX])\]/;

function activeLines(state: EditorState): Set<number> {
  const lines = new Set<number>();
  for (const r of state.selection.ranges) {
    const last = state.doc.lineAt(r.to).number;
    for (let n = state.doc.lineAt(r.from).number; n <= last; n++) lines.add(n);
  }
  return lines;
}

const childRange = (node: Parent): [number, number] | null => {
  const kids = node.children as Node[];
  if (!kids.length || !kids[0].position || !kids.at(-1)!.position) return null;
  return [rangeOf(kids[0])[0], rangeOf(kids.at(-1)!)[1]];
};

function build(state: EditorState, page: ParsedPage, active: Set<number>): DecorationSet {
  const doc = state.doc;
  const text = (from: number, to: number) => doc.sliceString(from, to);
  const reduced = doc.length > LARGE_NOTE_CHARS;
  const path = state.facet(pagePath)();
  const out: Range<Decoration>[] = [];
  const touched = (from: number, to: number) => {
    const last = doc.lineAt(Math.min(to, doc.length)).number;
    for (let n = doc.lineAt(from).number; n <= last; n++) if (active.has(n)) return true;
    return false;
  };
  const conceal = (from: number, to: number) => {
    if (from < to) out.push(hide.range(from, to));
  };
  const lines = (from: number, to: number, deco: Decoration) => {
    const last = doc.lineAt(Math.min(to, doc.length)).number;
    for (let n = doc.lineAt(from).number; n <= last; n++) out.push(deco.range(doc.line(n).from));
  };
  /** Wrapping markup around children: show content, hide the rest. */
  const wrapped = (node: Parent, deco: Decoration) => {
    const [from, to] = rangeOf(node);
    const inner = childRange(node);
    if (!inner || inner[0] >= inner[1]) return;
    out.push(deco.range(inner[0], inner[1]));
    if (!touched(from, to)) {
      conceal(from, inner[0]);
      conceal(inner[1], to);
    }
  };
  const blockWidget = (node: Node) => {
    const [from, to] = rangeOf(node);
    const start = doc.lineAt(from).from;
    const end = doc.lineAt(Math.min(to, doc.length)).to;
    out.push(
      Decoration.replace({
        widget: new PreviewWidget(text(start, end), path),
        block: true,
      }).range(start, end),
    );
  };
  const isLoneEmbed = (node: Parent) => {
    const kids = (node.children as RootContent[]).filter(
      (c) => !(c.type === 'text' && !c.value.trim()),
    );
    return kids.length === 1 && (kids[0].type === ('embed' as string) || kids[0].type === 'image');
  };

  const visit = (node: Node) => {
    if (!node.position) return;
    const [from, to] = rangeOf(node);
    const type = node.type as string;
    if (!reduced) {
      if (
        (BLOCK_WIDGET.has(type) || (type === 'paragraph' && isLoneEmbed(node as Parent))) &&
        !touched(from, to)
      ) {
        blockWidget(node);
        return;
      }
    }
    switch (type) {
      case 'heading': {
        const line = doc.lineAt(from);
        out.push(lineClass(`cm-liber-h${(node as Heading).depth}`).range(line.from));
        const marks = /^ {0,3}#{1,6}(?:[ \t]+|$)/.exec(line.text);
        if (marks && !touched(from, to)) conceal(line.from, line.from + marks[0].length);
        break;
      }
      case 'emphasis':
      case 'strong':
      case 'delete':
      case 'highlight':
        wrapped(node as Parent, MARKS[type]);
        break;
      case 'link':
        wrapped(node as Parent, MARKS.link);
        break;
      case 'wikiLink': {
        const inner = text(from + 2, to - 2);
        const bar = inner.search(/(?<!\\)\|/);
        const show: [number, number] = bar >= 0 ? [from + 2 + bar + 1, to - 2] : [from + 2, to - 2];
        if (show[0] < show[1]) out.push(MARKS.link.range(show[0], show[1]));
        if (!touched(from, to)) {
          conceal(from, show[0]);
          conceal(show[1], to);
        }
        return;
      }
      case 'inlineCode': {
        if (reduced) return;
        const ticks = /^`+/.exec(text(from, to))?.[0].length ?? 1;
        if (from + ticks < to - ticks) out.push(MARKS.inlineCode.range(from + ticks, to - ticks));
        if (!touched(from, to)) {
          conceal(from, from + ticks);
          conceal(to - ticks, to);
        }
        return;
      }
      case 'tag':
        if (!reduced && from < to) out.push(MARKS.tag.range(from, to));
        return;
      case 'code':
        if (!reduced) lines(from, to, lineClass('cm-liber-codeblock'));
        return;
      case 'yaml':
        lines(from, to, lineClass('cm-liber-frontmatter'));
        return;
      case 'blockquote':
        if (!reduced) lines(from, to, lineClass('cm-liber-quote'));
        break;
      case 'listItem': {
        const item = node as ListItem;
        if (reduced || typeof item.checked !== 'boolean') break;
        const line = doc.lineAt(from);
        const m = TASK.exec(line.text);
        if (m && !touched(from, from)) {
          const at = line.from + m[1].length;
          const label = line.text.slice(m[0].length).trim();
          out.push(
            Decoration.replace({ widget: new CheckboxWidget(item.checked, label) }).range(
              at,
              at + 3,
            ),
          );
        }
        break;
      }
    }
    const kids = (node as Parent).children as Node[] | undefined;
    if (kids) kids.forEach(visit);
  };
  page.tree.children.forEach(visit);
  return Decoration.set(out, true);
}

const keyOf = (lines: Set<number>) => [...lines].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(',');

const model = StateField.define<Model>({
  create(state) {
    const text = state.doc.toString();
    const page = parsePage(text);
    const active = activeLines(state);
    return { page, text, stale: false, active: keyOf(active), decos: build(state, page, active) };
  },
  update(m, tr) {
    for (const e of tr.effects) {
      if (e.is(setModel)) {
        const active = activeLines(tr.state);
        return {
          ...e.value,
          stale: false,
          active: keyOf(active),
          decos: build(tr.state, e.value.page, active),
        };
      }
    }
    if (tr.docChanged) return { ...m, stale: true, decos: m.decos.map(tr.changes) };
    if (tr.selection && !m.stale) {
      const active = activeLines(tr.state);
      const key = keyOf(active);
      if (key !== m.active) return { ...m, active: key, decos: build(tr.state, m.page, active) };
    }
    return m;
  },
  provide: (f) => EditorView.decorations.from(f, (m) => m.decos),
});

/** Brings the model up to date now (instead of after the debounce). */
function refresh(view: EditorView): Model {
  const m = view.state.field(model);
  if (!m.stale) return m;
  const text = view.state.doc.toString();
  view.dispatch({ effects: setModel.of({ page: reparse(m.page, m.text, text), text }) });
  return view.state.field(model);
}

const reparser = ViewPlugin.fromClass(
  class {
    private timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    constructor(private readonly view: EditorView) {}
    update(u: ViewUpdate) {
      if (!u.docChanged) return;
      if (this.timer) clearTimeout(this.timer);
      this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.timer = null;
        refresh(this.view);
      }, REPARSE_MS);
    }
    destroy() {
      if (this.timer) clearTimeout(this.timer);
    }
  },
);

/** The deepest link-like node containing `pos`. */
function linkAt(page: ParsedPage, pos: number): Node | null {
  let found: Node | null = null;
  const walk = (node: Node) => {
    const [from, to] = rangeOf(node);
    if (!node.position || pos < from || pos > to) return;
    if (['wikiLink', 'embed', 'link', 'image'].includes(node.type)) found = node;
    const kids = (node as Parent).children as Node[] | undefined;
    if (kids) kids.forEach(walk);
  };
  page.tree.children.forEach(walk);
  return found;
}

async function resolveAndFollow(
  kind: LinkKind,
  query: string,
  target: string,
  heading: string | null,
  source: string,
) {
  const res = await linksResolve(source, [{ kind, target: query }]);
  if (res.ok)
    followLink({ type: 'page', kind, target, heading, resolved: res.value[0] ?? null, source });
}

/** Follows the link at `pos` (⌘-click, Mod-Enter); `false` if there is none. */
export function followLinkAt(view: EditorView, pos: number): boolean {
  const node = linkAt(refresh(view).page, pos);
  if (!node) return false;
  const source = view.state.facet(pagePath)();
  if (node.type === 'wikiLink' || node.type === 'embed') {
    const n = node as WikiLink | Embed;
    const kind: LinkKind = node.type === 'embed' ? 'embed' : 'wiki';
    void resolveAndFollow(kind, n.target, n.target, n.heading, source);
    return true;
  }
  const url = (node as Link | Image).url;
  if (/^(https?|mailto):/i.test(url)) {
    followLink({ type: 'external', url });
    return true;
  }
  if (url.startsWith('#')) {
    followLink({ type: 'anchor', heading: decodeURIComponent(url.slice(1)) });
    return true;
  }
  if (/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(url)) return false;
  const hash = url.indexOf('#');
  const joined = vaultJoin(source, hash >= 0 ? url.slice(0, hash) : url);
  if (!joined) return false;
  const heading = hash >= 0 ? decodeURIComponent(url.slice(hash + 1)) || null : null;
  void resolveAndFollow('markdown', `/${joined}`, joined, heading, source);
  return true;
}

export const followLinkAtCursor = (view: EditorView): boolean =>
  followLinkAt(view, view.state.selection.main.head);

/** ⌘/Ctrl-click follows links (plain clicks place the cursor). */
const modClick = EditorView.domEventHandlers({
  mousedown(e, view) {
    if (!(e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) || e.button !== 0) return false;
    const pos = view.posAtCoords({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
    if (pos === null || !followLinkAt(view, pos)) return false;
    e.preventDefault();
    return true;
  },
});

const theme = EditorView.baseTheme({
  '.cm-liber-h1, .cm-liber-h2, .cm-liber-h3, .cm-liber-h4, .cm-liber-h5, .cm-liber-h6': {
    fontFamily: 'var(--font-display)',
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: 'var(--color-text-strong)',
  },
  '.cm-liber-h1': { fontSize: '1.9em' },
  '.cm-liber-h2': { fontSize: '1.5em' },
  '.cm-liber-h3': { fontSize: '1.25em' },
  '.cm-liber-h4': { fontSize: '1.1em' },
  '.cm-liber-em': { fontStyle: 'italic' },
  '.cm-liber-strong': { fontWeight: '700' },
  '.cm-liber-strike': { textDecoration: 'line-through' },
  '.cm-liber-highlight': { background: 'var(--palette-yellow-bg)', borderRadius: '3px' },
  '.cm-liber-code': {
    fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)',
    fontSize: '0.9em',
    background: 'var(--color-surface-subtle)',
    borderRadius: '4px',
  },
  '.cm-liber-link': { color: 'var(--color-accent)', textDecoration: 'underline' },
  '.cm-liber-tag': { color: 'var(--color-accent)', fontWeight: '600' },
  '.cm-liber-codeblock': { fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)', fontSize: '0.9em' },
  '.cm-liber-quote': { borderLeft: '3px solid var(--color-border-strong)', paddingLeft: '12px' },
  '.cm-liber-frontmatter': { color: 'var(--color-text-muted)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' },
  '.cm-liber-widget': { padding: '4px 0', cursor: 'default' },
  '.cm-liber-checkbox': { margin: '0 4px 0 0', verticalAlign: 'middle', cursor: 'pointer' },
});

export function livePreview(): Extension {
  return [model, reparser, modClick, theme];
}

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/scrollSync.ts`:

```ts
// Split scroll sync (M2a §3.2): rendered blocks carry their source range
// (`data-source-start/end`), so the pane and the editor map onto each other.

const blocks = (pane: HTMLElement) => [
  ...pane.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-source-start][data-source-end]'),
];
const startOf = (el: HTMLElement) => Number(el.dataset.sourceStart);
const endOf = (el: HTMLElement) => Number(el.dataset.sourceEnd);

/** Top of `el` within the pane's scrollable content. */
export const topIn = (pane: HTMLElement, el: HTMLElement): number =>
  el.getBoundingClientRect().top - pane.getBoundingClientRect().top + pane.scrollTop;

/** The deepest rendered block containing source `offset`, else the next one. */
export function blockForOffset(pane: HTMLElement, offset: number): HTMLElement | null {
  let best: HTMLElement | null = null;
  for (const el of blocks(pane)) {
    // Document order: a later match inside an earlier one is deeper.
    if (startOf(el) <= offset && offset < endOf(el)) best = el;
    else if (!best && startOf(el) > offset) return el;
  }
  return best;
}

/** Source offset of the deepest block at the top edge of the pane. */
export function offsetAtTop(pane: HTMLElement): number | null {
  const y = pane.scrollTop;
  let best: HTMLElement | null = null;
  for (const el of blocks(pane)) {
    const top = topIn(pane, el);
    if (top <= y && y < top + el.getBoundingClientRect().height) best = el;
    else if (!best && top > y) return startOf(el);
  }
  return best ? startOf(best) : null;
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/editor/widgets.tsx`:

```tsx
import { WidgetType, type EditorView } from '@codemirror/view';
import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client';
import { Preview } from '../preview/Preview';

/** A block (table, callout, columns, embed, rule) rendered with the preview
 *  components (M2a §3.2). A click outside its links moves the cursor into
 *  the block, which shows its source. */
export class PreviewWidget extends WidgetType {
  private root: Root | null = null;

  constructor(
    readonly text: string,
    readonly path: string,
  ) {
    super();
  }

  eq(other: PreviewWidget): boolean {
    return other.text === this.text && other.path === this.path;
  }

  toDOM(view: EditorView): HTMLElement {
    const dom = document.createElement('div');
    dom.className = 'cm-liber-widget';
    dom.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest('a, button, input, audio, video, summary')) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      view.dispatch({ selection: { anchor: view.posAtDOM(dom) } });
      view.focus();
    });
    this.root = createRoot(dom);
    this.root.render(<Preview text={this.text} path={this.path} embedded />);
    return dom;
  }

  destroy(): void {
    const root = this.root;
    this.root = null;
    // Not while React may be rendering (CodeMirror updates run inside effects).
    if (root) queueMicrotask(() => root.unmount());
  }

  ignoreEvent(): boolean {
    return true;
  }
}

/** `[ ]` / `[x]` of a task item: a checkbox that edits the source. */
export class CheckboxWidget extends WidgetType {
  constructor(
    readonly checked: boolean,
    readonly label: string,
  ) {
    super();
  }

  eq(other: CheckboxWidget): boolean {
    return other.checked === this.checked && other.label === this.label;
  }

  toDOM(view: EditorView): HTMLElement {
    const box = document.createElement('input');
    box.type = 'checkbox';
    box.checked = this.checked;
    box.tabIndex = -1;
    box.className = 'cm-liber-checkbox';
    box.setAttribute('aria-label', this.label);
    box.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const at = view.posAtDOM(box) + 1; // the character inside the brackets
      view.dispatch({
        changes: { from: at, to: at + 1, insert: this.checked ? ' ' : 'x' },
        userEvent: 'input.toggle',
      });
    });
    return box;
  }

  ignoreEvent(): boolean {
    return true;
  }
}

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/preview/useParsed.ts`:

```ts
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { reparse } from '../../markdown/incremental';
import { parsePage } from '../../markdown/parse';
import type { ParsedPage } from '../../markdown/types';

/** The model of `text`, re-parsed incrementally from the previous render's. */
export function useParsed(text: string): ParsedPage {
  const last = useRef<{ text: string; page: ParsedPage } | null>(null);
  return useMemo(() => {
    const prev = last.current;
    const page = prev ? reparse(prev.page, prev.text, text) : parsePage(text);
    last.current = { text, page };
    return page;
  }, [text]);
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/markdown/incremental.ts`:

```ts
// Incremental re-parse for typing (M2a §7: a keystroke in a 5,000-line note
// re-parses in < 50 ms; a full remark parse of such a note takes ~110 ms).
// Only the top-level blocks around the edit are parsed again; everything with
// a reach beyond its own block falls back to a full parse.
import type { Node, Parent, Root, RootContent } from 'mdast';
import { finishPage, parsePage, parseTree } from './parse';
import type { ParsedPage } from './types';

/** Constructs whose meaning can depend on, or change, text outside their own
 *  block: fences, HTML, comments, definitions/footnotes, directives, math. */
const NONLOCAL = /`{3}|~{3}|%%|\]:|<|^[ \t]*:{3}|\$\$/m;

const begin = (n: Node) => n.position!.start.offset!;
const end = (n: Node) => n.position!.end.offset!;

/** Line-start offset at or before `pos`. */
const lineStart = (text: string, pos: number) => text.lastIndexOf('\n', pos - 1) + 1;

/** `pos` is a line start preceded by a blank line (or the start of the text). */
function blankBefore(text: string, pos: number): boolean {
  if (pos === 0) return true;
  const prev = text.lastIndexOf('\n', pos - 2);
  return text.slice(prev + 1, pos - 1).trim() === '';
}

/** After `pos` comes the end of its line, a blank line, then a line that
 *  isn't indented (so it can't continue a list or a code block) — or nothing. */
function blankAfter(text: string, pos: number): boolean {
  const m = /^[ \t]*(?:\n|$)/.exec(text.slice(pos, pos + 200));
  if (!m) return false;
  const rest = text.slice(pos + m[0].length);
  if (!rest) return true;
  const blank = /^[ \t]*\n/.exec(rest);
  if (!blank) return false;
  const after = rest.slice(blank[0].length).replace(/^([ \t]*\n)+/, '');
  return after === '' || !/^[ \t]/.test(after);
}

/** Moves every position in `nodes` by `offset` characters and `lines` lines. */
function shift(nodes: Node[], offset: number, lines: number) {
  const walk = (n: Node) => {
    const p = n.position;
    if (p) {
      p.start = { ...p.start, offset: p.start.offset! + offset, line: p.start.line + lines };
      p.end = { ...p.end, offset: p.end.offset! + offset, line: p.end.line + lines };
    }
    const children = (n as Parent).children as Node[] | undefined;
    if (children) children.forEach(walk);
  };
  nodes.forEach(walk);
}

const countLines = (text: string, from: number, to: number) => {
  let n = 0;
  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) if (text.charCodeAt(i) === 10) n++;
  return n;
};

/** The model of `text`, reusing `prev` (the model of `prevText`). Takes
 *  ownership of `prev`: its nodes are reused and moved, so don't use it again. */
export function reparse(prev: ParsedPage, prevText: string, text: string): ParsedPage {
  if (text === prevText) return prev;
  const blocks = prev.tree.children;
  if (prev.frontmatter || prev.comments.length || blocks.length < 3 || text.startsWith('---')) {
    return parsePage(text);
  }
  // The changed region: [from, oldTo) in prevText became [from, newTo) in text.
  let from = 0;
  const max = Math.min(prevText.length, text.length);
  while (from < max && prevText.charCodeAt(from) === text.charCodeAt(from)) from++;
  let oldTo = prevText.length;
  let newTo = text.length;
  while (
    oldTo > from &&
    newTo > from &&
    prevText.charCodeAt(oldTo - 1) === text.charCodeAt(newTo - 1)
  ) {
    oldTo--;
    newTo--;
  }
  // Blocks touching the change, widened by one neighbour on each side.
  let first = blocks.findIndex((b) => end(b) >= from);
  let last = blocks.length - 1;
  while (last > 0 && begin(blocks[last]) > oldTo) last--;
  if (first < 0 || last < first) return parsePage(text);
  first = Math.max(0, first - 1);
  last = Math.min(blocks.length - 1, last + 1);
  const sliceStart = lineStart(prevText, begin(blocks[first]));
  const oldEnd = end(blocks[last]);
  if (from < sliceStart || oldTo > oldEnd) return parsePage(text);
  const delta = text.length - prevText.length;
  const newEnd = oldEnd + delta;
  const slice = text.slice(sliceStart, newEnd);
  if (
    !blankBefore(text, sliceStart) ||
    !blankAfter(text, newEnd) ||
    NONLOCAL.test(slice) ||
    NONLOCAL.test(prevText.slice(sliceStart, oldEnd))
  ) {
    return parsePage(text);
  }
  const part = parseTree(slice).children;
  const before = blocks.slice(0, first);
  const after = blocks.slice(last + 1);
  // Lists separated only by blank lines merge: let the full parse decide.
  const edgeList = (a?: RootContent, b?: RootContent) => a?.type === 'list' && b?.type === 'list';
  if (
    !part.length ||
    part.some((n) => n.type === 'yaml') ||
    edgeList(before.at(-1), part[0]) ||
    edgeList(part.at(-1), after[0])
  ) {
    return parsePage(text);
  }
  shift(part, sliceStart, countLines(text, 0, sliceStart));
  shift(
    after,
    delta,
    countLines(text, sliceStart, newEnd) - countLines(prevText, sliceStart, oldEnd),
  );
  const lines = countLines(text, 0, text.length);
  const tree: Root = {
    ...prev.tree,
    children: [...before, ...part, ...after],
    position: {
      start: { line: 1, column: 1, offset: 0 },
      end: {
        line: lines + 1,
        column: text.length - (text.lastIndexOf('\n') + 1) + 1,
        offset: text.length,
      },
    },
  };
  return finishPage(tree, []);
}
```

@@ -141,3 +140,13 @@ export function parsePage(text: string): ParsedPage {
