```diff
+
+/** Parses one page. Pure and synchronous; positions are UTF-16 offsets into `text`. */
+export function parsePage(text: string): ParsedPage {
+  const loc = locator(text);
+  // Comments are blanked (same length) and the page re-parsed, so positions
+  // still index `text` but nothing inside a comment renders or links.
+  const comments = findComments(text, processor.parse(text) as Root);
+  const source = maskComments(text, comments);
+  return finishPage(parseTree(source, loc), comments);
+}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/page/Breadcrumb.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { TreeNode } from '../../ipc';
import { childrenByParent } from '../../stores/tree';
import { resetVaultStore, useVaultStore } from '../../stores/vaultStore';
import { ExplorerTree } from '../explorer/ExplorerTree';
import { Breadcrumb } from './Breadcrumb';

const nodes: TreeNode[] = [
  { path: 'Art', kind: 'folder' },
  { path: 'Art/Sketches', kind: 'folder' },
  { path: 'Art/Sketches/rex.md', kind: 'page' },
  { path: 'b.md', kind: 'page' },
];

beforeEach(() =>
  act(() =>
    useVaultStore.setState({
      status: 'open',
      info: { name: 'My Vault', root: '/v', report: {} as never },
      nodes,
      children: childrenByParent(nodes),
      expanded: new Set(),
    }),
  ),
);
afterEach(() => resetVaultStore());

describe('Breadcrumb (M2a §5)', () => {
  it('lists the vault, each folder and the current page', () => {
    render(<Breadcrumb path="Art/Sketches/rex.md" />);
    const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Breadcrumb' });
    const items = [...nav.querySelectorAll('li')].map((li) => li.textContent);
    expect(items).toEqual(['My Vault', 'Art', 'Sketches', 'rex']);
    expect(screen.getByText('rex')).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
  });

  it('a folder segment expands its chain in the explorer and focuses it', async () => {
    render(
      <>
        <Breadcrumb path="Art/Sketches/rex.md" />
        <ExplorerTree />
      </>,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sketches' }));
    expect(useVaultStore.getState().expanded).toEqual(new Set(['Art', 'Art/Sketches']));
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('treeitem', { name: 'Sketches' }));
  });

  it('the vault segment collapses the tree to the top and focuses it', async () => {
    act(() => useVaultStore.setState({ expanded: new Set(['Art', 'Art/Sketches']) }));
    render(
      <>
        <Breadcrumb path="Art/Sketches/rex.md" />
        <ExplorerTree />
      </>,
    );
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'My Vault' }));
    expect(useVaultStore.getState().expanded.size).toBe(0);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(screen.getByRole('treeitem', { name: 'Art' }));
  });
});
```

Create `apps/desktop/tests/syntax-target.test.ts`:

```ts
// @vitest-environment node
import { ESLint } from 'eslint';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const cwd = fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url));
const eslint = new ESLint({ cwd });

async function ruleIds(code: string) {
  const [result] = await eslint.lintText(code, { filePath: 'src/features/example.ts' });
  return result.messages.map((m) => m.ruleId);
}

describe('build target (Safari 15 / WKWebView on macOS 12)', () => {
  it.each(['export const a = /(?<=x)y/;', 'export const b = /(?<!\\\\)\\|/;'])(
    'rejects regex lookbehind (Safari 16.4+): %s',
    async (code) => {
      expect(await ruleIds(code)).toContain('no-restricted-syntax');
    },
  );

  it('allows lookahead and named groups', async () => {
    expect(await ruleIds('export const c = /x(?=y)(?<name>z)/;')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```diff
+++ b/apps/desktop/src/features/editor/merge.ts
@@ -2,7 +2,17 @@ import { diff3Merge } from 'node-diff3';
 
 export type MergeOutcome = { clean: true; text: string } | { clean: false };
 
-const lines = (text: string): string[] => text.split(/(?<=\n)/);
+/** Lines with their `\n` kept (no lookbehind split: Safari 15 target). */
+function lines(text: string): string[] {
+  const out: string[] = [];
+  let start = 0;
+  for (let i = text.indexOf('\n'); i >= 0; i = text.indexOf('\n', start)) {
+    out.push(text.slice(start, i + 1));
+    start = i + 1;
+  }
+  if (start < text.length || !out.length) out.push(text.slice(start));
+  return out;
+}
 
 /** Line-based 3-way merge (M2a §3.3): `base` is the last text both sides
  *  agreed on, `mine` the editor, `theirs` the file on disk. */
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/page/Breadcrumb.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { nameOf, parentOf } from '../../stores/tree';
import { useVaultStore } from '../../stores/vaultStore';
import styles from './PageView.module.css';

/** Vault › folders › page (M2a §5). Folder segments reveal and focus the folder
 *  in the explorer; the vault segment collapses the tree to the top. Shared
 *  with the Global View (GV-14). */
export function Breadcrumb({ path }: { path: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const vault = useVaultStore((s) => s.info?.name ?? '');
  const reveal = useVaultStore((s) => s.revealFolder);
  const folders: string[] = [];
  for (let p = parentOf(path); p; p = parentOf(p)) folders.unshift(p);
  return (
    <nav aria-label={t('page.breadcrumb')} className={styles.breadcrumb}>
      <ol>
        <li>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => reveal('')}>
            {vault}
          </button>
        </li>
        {folders.map((folder) => (
          <li key={folder}>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => reveal(folder)}>
              {nameOf(folder)}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
        <li>
          <span aria-current="page">{nameOf(path).replace(/\.md$/i, '')}</span>
        </li>
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/page/ModeToggle.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { EditorMode } from '../../ipc';
import { useSettingsStore } from '../../stores/settingsStore';
import styles from './PageView.module.css';

const MODES: EditorMode[] = ['livePreview', 'source', 'split'];

/** Live Preview · Source · Split (segmented control of native radios); the
 *  choice is saved as the `editorMode` setting. */
export function ModeToggle({ mode }: { mode: EditorMode }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <fieldset className={styles.modes}>
      <legend className={styles.srOnly}>{t('editor.mode')}</legend>
      {MODES.map((m) => (
        <label key={m} className={m === mode ? styles.modeOn : styles.mode}>
          <input
            type="radio"
            name="editor-mode"
            value={m}
            checked={m === mode}
            onChange={() => void useSettingsStore.getState().update({ editorMode: m })}
          />
          {t(`editor.modes.${m}`)}
        </label>
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/page/PageHeader.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { assetUrl, linksResolve, type EditorMode, type Page } from '../../ipc';
import { useEditorStore } from '../../stores/editorStore';
import { Breadcrumb } from './Breadcrumb';
import { ModeToggle } from './ModeToggle';
import styles from './PageView.module.css';

/** The cover's vault path (`null`: none, remote or missing). */
function useCover(page: Page): string | null {
  const [cover, setCover] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setCover(null);
    if (!page.cover || /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/i.test(page.cover)) return;
    let live = true;
    void linksResolve(page.path, [{ kind: 'cover', target: page.cover }]).then((res) => {
      if (live && res.ok) setCover(res.value[0] ?? null);
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, [page.path, page.cover]);
  return cover;
}

/** Breadcrumb, cover (read-only), title, subtitle, tags, link counts, the mode
 *  toggle and the save status (M2a §5). */
export function PageHeader({ page, mode }: { page: Page; mode: EditorMode }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const status = useEditorStore((s) => s.status);
  const hasDoc = useEditorStore((s) => s.doc !== null);
  const large = useEditorStore((s) => s.large);
  const cover = useCover(page);
  return (
    <header className={styles.header}>
      <div className={styles.bar}>
        <Breadcrumb path={page.path} />
        <div className={styles.controls}>
          {hasDoc && (
            <span role="status" aria-label={t('editor.saveStatus')} className={styles.status}>
              {t(`editor.${status}`)}
            </span>
          )}
          <ModeToggle mode={mode} />
        </div>
      </div>
      {cover && (
        <img data-cover className={styles.cover} src={assetUrl(cover)} alt="" loading="lazy" />
      )}
      <h1 className={styles.title}>{page.title}</h1>
      {page.subtitle && <p className={styles.subtitle}>{page.subtitle}</p>}
      {page.tags.length > 0 && (
        <ul className={styles.tags} aria-label={t('page.tags')}>
          {page.tags.map((tag) => (
            <li key={tag} className={styles.tag}>
              #{tag}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <p className={styles.meta}>
        <span>{t('page.meta', { links: page.links, backlinks: page.backlinks })}</span>
        {large && mode === 'livePreview' && <span>{t('editor.largeNote')}</span>}
      </p>
    </header>
  );
}
```

### Task 9: M1b polish a–f, j

Spec §6 (M1b hand-off §10). a: a failed switch keeps the current vault (and switching first saves open pages; a page that can't be saved refuses the switch so text is never dropped). b: Welcome `inert` while opening; a second open is refused. c: delete dialog focuses its confirm button; Enter confirms. d: "…" row button opens the context menu. e: rename error id via `useId()`. f: recent list reloads after a failed open. j: `vault_open` survives a read-only config dir (index warning instead).

```diff
+/// M1b hand-off §10 j: a read-only config dir must not stop a vault from
+/// opening; the failed recent-vaults update becomes an index warning.
+#[cfg(unix)]
+#[test]
+fn vlt_01_open_survives_an_unwritable_config_dir() {
+    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
+    let h = harness();
+    let config = h._config.path().to_path_buf();
+    std::fs::set_permissions(&config, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o555)).unwrap();
+    let vault = copy_fixture("obsidian-basic");
+    let res = call(&h.main, "vault_open", json!({ "path": vault.path() }));
+    std::fs::set_permissions(&config, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
+    let info = res.expect("vault opens");
+    let warnings: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(info["report"]["warnings"].clone()).unwrap();
+    assert!(
+        warnings.iter().any(|w| w.contains("recent vaults")),
+        "{warnings:?}"
+    );
+    call(&h.main, "vault_tree", json!({})).expect("the vault is open");
+}
```

Create `apps/desktop/src/features/shell/Polish.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// M1b hand-off §10 items a–f (M2a spec §6).
import { act, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { App } from '../../app/App';
import type { TreeNode } from '../../ipc';
import { clearMocks, mockIPC } from '../../ipc/testing';
import { resetEditorStore, useEditorStore } from '../../stores/editorStore';
import { usePageStore } from '../../stores/pageStore';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, useSettingsStore } from '../../stores/settingsStore';
import { useToastStore } from '../../stores/toastStore';
import { childrenByParent } from '../../stores/tree';
import { useUiStore } from '../../stores/uiStore';
import { resetVaultStore, useVaultStore } from '../../stores/vaultStore';
import { AppShell } from './AppShell';

const nodes: TreeNode[] = [
  { path: 'Art', kind: 'folder' },
  { path: 'Art/rex.md', kind: 'page' },
  { path: 'My Notes.md', kind: 'page' },
  { path: 'Welcome.md', kind: 'page' },
];
const info = (name: string) => ({
  name,
  root: `/${name}`,
  report: {
    files: 1,
    pages: 1,
    parsed: 1,
    skippedUnchanged: 0,
    removed: 0,
    warnings: [],
    durationMs: 1,
  },
});

function backend(handlers: Record<string, (a: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown>) {
  const calls: [string, Record<string, unknown>][] = [];
  mockIPC(
    (cmd, args) => {
      const a = (args ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
      calls.push([cmd, a]);
      return handlers[cmd]?.(a);
    },
    { shouldMockEvents: true },
  );
  return calls;
}

function seed() {
  act(() =>
    useVaultStore.setState({
      status: 'open',
      info: info('A'),
      nodes,
      children: childrenByParent(nodes),
    }),
  );
}

function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (v: T) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((r) => (resolve = r));
  return { promise, resolve };
}

afterEach(async () => {
  resetEditorStore();
  usePageStore.getState().clear();
  await resetVaultStore();
  useUiStore.getState().closeDialog();
  useToastStore.getState().clear();
  useSettingsStore.setState({ settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS });
  clearMocks();
});

describe('M1b polish (M2a §6)', () => {
  it('a: a failed switch keeps the current vault open', async () => {
    backend({
      vault_open: () => {
        throw { kind: 'notFound', message: '/B' };
      },
      vault_tree: () => nodes,
    });
    seed();
    const res = await act(() => useVaultStore.getState().open('/B'));
    expect(res.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(useVaultStore.getState().status).toBe('open');
    expect(useVaultStore.getState().info?.name).toBe('A');
    expect(useToastStore.getState().toasts[0].message).toBe('Not found.');
  });

  it('a: switching vaults saves open pages first; an unsaveable page blocks the switch', async () => {
    const files: Record<string, string> = { 'Welcome.md': 'hi\n' };
    let failWrites = false;
    const calls = backend({
      vault_tree: () => nodes,
      page_get: (a) => ({ path: a.path, title: 'Welcome', tags: [], links: 0, backlinks: 0 }),
      page_read: (a) => ({ text: files[a.path as string], hash: 'h1' }),
      page_write: (a) => {
        if (failWrites) throw { kind: 'io', message: 'disk full' };
        files[a.path as string] = a.text as string;
        return { hash: 'h2' };
      },
      vault_open: () => info('B'),
    });
    seed();
    await act(() => usePageStore.getState().load('Welcome.md'));
    useEditorStore.getState().doc!.edit('hi there\n');
    await act(() => useVaultStore.getState().open('/B'));
    const order = calls.map(([c]) => c);
    expect(order.indexOf('page_write')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('vault_open'));
    expect(files['Welcome.md']).toBe('hi there\n');
    expect(useEditorStore.getState().doc).toBeNull();

    // Now a page that can't be saved: the switch is refused, the text stays.
    await act(() => usePageStore.getState().load('Welcome.md'));
    failWrites = true;
    useEditorStore.getState().doc!.edit('unsaved\n');
    const opens = order.filter((c) => c === 'vault_open').length;
    const res = await act(() => useVaultStore.getState().open('/C'));
    expect(res.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(calls.filter(([c]) => c === 'vault_open')).toHaveLength(opens);
    expect(useEditorStore.getState().doc?.text).toBe('unsaved\n');
    expect(useToastStore.getState().toasts.at(-1)?.message).toBe(
      'Unsaved changes in “Welcome” couldn’t be written, so the vault stays open.',
    );
  });

  it('b: Welcome is inert while opening and a second open is refused', async () => {
    const pending = deferred<unknown>();
    const calls = backend({ vault_open: () => pending.promise, vaults_recent: () => [] });
    render(<App />);
    let first!: Promise<unknown>;
    act(() => {
      first = useVaultStore.getState().open('/A');
    });
    expect(screen.getByRole('main', { hidden: true })).toHaveAttribute('inert');
    const second = await act(() => useVaultStore.getState().open('/B'));
    expect(second.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(calls.filter(([c]) => c === 'vault_open')).toHaveLength(1);
    pending.resolve(info('A'));
    await act(() => first);
  });

  it('c: the delete dialog focuses its confirm button and Enter confirms', async () => {
    const calls = backend({ backlink_count: () => 0, vault_tree: () => nodes });
    seed();
    render(<AppShell />);
    screen.getByRole('treeitem', { name: 'Welcome' }).focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Delete}');
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Move to Trash' })).toHaveFocus();
    within(dialog).getByRole('checkbox').focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Enter}');
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.map(([c]) => c)).toContain('entry_trash'));
  });

  it('d: a "…" button on each row opens its context menu', async () => {
    backend({});
    seed();
    render(<AppShell />);
    const row = screen.getByRole('treeitem', { name: 'Welcome' });
    await userEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Welcome' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('menu', { name: 'Actions for Welcome' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(useVaultStore.getState().selected).not.toBe('Welcome.md'); // the row wasn't activated
  });

  it('e: the inline-rename error id is a safe generated id', async () => {
    backend({});
    seed();
    render(<AppShell />);
    act(() => useVaultStore.getState().startRename('My Notes.md'));
    const input = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Rename' });
    await userEvent.clear(input);
    await userEvent.type(input, '.hidden');
    const id = input.getAttribute('aria-describedby')!;
    expect(id).not.toMatch(/\s|Notes/);
    expect(document.getElementById(id)).toHaveTextContent("Names can't start with a dot.");
  });

  it('f: the recent list reloads after a failed open (missing flag)', async () => {
    let missing = false;
    backend({
      vaults_recent: () => [{ path: '/gone', name: 'gone', openedAt: '', missing }],
      vault_open: () => {
        missing = true;
        throw { kind: 'notFound', message: '/gone' };
      },
    });
    render(<App />);
    const open = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /^gone/ });
    await userEvent.click(open);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^gone/ })).toBeDisabled());
    expect(screen.getByText('Folder not found')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

### Task 10: Zero-network end-to-end test (Playwright)

Spec §7 (NFR-04, arch §6.1 layer 3). The built UI is served with the exact production CSP (read from `tauri.conf.json`), Tauri's `freezePrototype` is replicated, the IPC is mocked, and a page full of remote references is opened in all three modes. The test asserts zero non-local requests **and** zero CSP violations (the sanitizer, not the CSP, must stop every reference; CSP-blocked loads never become requests, so violations are collected from `securitypolicyviolation` events).

**Files:**
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Modify: `.gitignore`
- Test (create): `apps/desktop/e2e/ipcMock.ts`
- Test (create): `apps/desktop/e2e/remote.md`
- Test (create): `apps/desktop/e2e/serve.ts`
- Test (create): `apps/desktop/e2e/zero-network.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/package.json`
- Create: `apps/desktop/playwright.config.ts`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/tsconfig.json`
- Modify: `package.json`
- Generated (commit as produced): `pnpm-lock.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole UI.
- Produces: `pnpm test:e2e` (root and `@liber/desktop`: `vite build && playwright test`); CI job `e2e`; `.gitignore` entries `test-results/`, `playwright-report/`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install Playwright and Chromium**

```bash
pnpm --filter @liber/desktop add -D @playwright/test@^1.64.0
pnpm --filter @liber/desktop exec playwright install chromium
```

Add the scripts — in `apps/desktop/package.json` `"scripts"`: `"test:e2e": "vite build && playwright test"`; in the root `package.json` `"scripts"`: `"test:e2e": "pnpm --filter @liber/desktop test:e2e"`, and in the root `"check"` insert ` && pnpm test:e2e` right after `pnpm build:ui`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the test, its fixture page, the IPC mock and the CSP server**

Create `apps/desktop/e2e/ipcMock.ts`:

```ts
// Installed before the app's scripts (page.addInitScript): a fake Tauri IPC
// with one vault holding `remote.md`, then Tauri's freezePrototype.
// Runs in the page, so it must be self-contained.
export function installIpcMock({
  pages,
  editorMode,
}: {
  pages: Record<string, string>;
  editorMode: string;
}) {
  type Args = Record<string, unknown>;
  const w = window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  let nextId = 1;
  const page = (path: string) => ({
    path,
    title: path.replace(/\.md$/, ''),
    subtitle: null,
    cover: 'https://remote.example/cover.png',
    tags: [],
    links: 0,
    backlinks: 0,
    text: pages[path],
    truncated: false,
    binary: false,
  });
  const handlers: Record<string, (a: Args) => unknown> = {
    app_info: () => ({ name: 'Liber Linteum', version: '0.0.0-e2e' }),
    settings_get: () => ({
      settings: {
        version: 1,
        language: 'en',
        theme: 'light',
        rewriteLinks: true,
        confirmDelete: true,
        editorMode,
      },
    }),
    settings_set: (a) => ({
      version: 1,
      language: 'en',
      theme: 'light',
      editorMode,
      ...(a.patch as object),
    }),
    vaults_recent: () => [{ path: '/vault', name: 'vault', openedAt: '', missing: false }],
    vault_open: () => ({
      name: 'vault',
      root: '/vault',
      report: {
        files: 2,
        pages: 2,
        parsed: 2,
        skippedUnchanged: 0,
        removed: 0,
        warnings: [],
        durationMs: 1,
      },
    }),
    vault_tree: () => Object.keys(pages).map((path) => ({ path, kind: 'page' })),
    page_get: (a) => page(a.path as string),
    page_read: (a) => ({ text: pages[a.path as string], hash: 'h' }),
    page_write: () => ({ hash: 'h2' }),
    backlink_count: () => 0,
    links_resolve: (a) =>
      (a.targets as { kind: string; target: string }[]).map(({ target }) => {
        const name = `${target.replace(/^\//, '').replace(/#.*$/, '')}.md`;
        return name in pages ? name : null;
      }),
    'plugin:event|listen': () => nextId++,
    'plugin:event|unlisten': () => null,
  };
  w.__TAURI_INTERNALS__ = {
    metadata: {
      currentWindow: { label: 'main' },
      currentWebview: { windowLabel: 'main', label: 'main' },
    },
    transformCallback: (cb: unknown) => {
      const id = nextId++;
      w[`_${id}`] = cb;
      return id;
    },
    unregisterCallback: (id: number) => delete w[`_${id}`],
    convertFileSrc: (p: string) => p,
    invoke: async (cmd: string, args: Args) => {
      const handler = handlers[cmd];
      if (!handler) throw { kind: 'io', message: `not mocked: ${cmd}` };
      return handler(args ?? {});
    },
  };
  w.__TAURI_EVENT_PLUGIN_INTERNALS__ = { unregisterListener: () => undefined };
  w.isTauri = true;
  // CSP-blocked loads never become requests; record them as violations.
  const violations: string[] = [];
  w.__liberCspViolations = violations;
  document.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', (e) =>
    violations.push(`${e.violatedDirective} ${e.blockedURI}`),
  );
  // tauri.conf.json: "freezePrototype": true
  Object.freeze(Object.prototype);
}
```

Create `apps/desktop/e2e/serve.ts`:

```ts
// Serves the built UI (dist/) with the production CSP from tauri.conf.json,
// for the zero-network test (M2a §7, NFR-04). Usage: node e2e/serve.ts [port]
import { readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { extname, join, normalize } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url));
const dist = join(root, 'dist');
const conf = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'src-tauri/tauri.conf.json'), 'utf8'));
const csp = Object.entries(conf.app.security.csp as Record<string, string>)
  .map(([directive, value]) => `${directive} ${value}`)
  .join('; ');
const port = Number(process.argv[2] ?? 4317);

const TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
  '.js': 'text/javascript',
  '.css': 'text/css',
  '.woff2': 'font/woff2',
  '.woff': 'font/woff',
  '.svg': 'image/svg+xml',
  '.png': 'image/png',
};

createServer((req, res) => {
  const path = normalize(decodeURIComponent((req.url ?? '/').split('?')[0]));
  let file = join(dist, path);
  if (!file.startsWith(dist)) {
    res.writeHead(403).end();
    return;
  }
  try {
    if (statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = join(file, 'index.html');
    const body = readFileSync(file);
    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': TYPES[extname(file)] ?? 'application/octet-stream',
      'Content-Security-Policy': csp,
    });
    res.end(body);
  } catch {
    res.writeHead(404).end();
  }
}).listen(port, '127.0.0.1', () => console.log(`serving ${dist} on ${port}`));
```

Create `apps/desktop/e2e/zero-network.spec.ts`:

```ts
// NFR-04 / arch §6.1 layer 3 (M2a §7): the built UI, under the production CSP
// and Tauri's frozen prototypes, renders a page full of remote references in
// every editor mode without a single non-local request — and without the CSP
// having to block anything (the sanitizer already removed every reference;
// the CSP is the second layer, so a violation here is a sanitizer bug).
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { installIpcMock } from './ipcMock';

const REMOTE = readFileSync(new URL('./remote.md', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const PAGES = {
  'remote.md': REMOTE,
  'Other.md': '# Other\n\n## Section\n\n![nested](https://remote.example/v.png)\n',
};

/** Records every request that isn't to the local test server, and stops it. */
async function watchNetwork(page: Page) {
  const outside: string[] = [];
  const problems: string[] = [];
  page.on('request', (r) => {
    const url = new URL(r.url());
    const local = url.protocol === 'http:' && url.hostname === '127.0.0.1';
    if (!local && url.protocol !== 'data:') outside.push(r.url());
  });
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => problems.push(`error: ${e.message}`));
  await page.route(/^(?!http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1)/, (route) => route.abort());
  return { outside, problems };
}

/** Scrolls `selector` through its whole height (CodeMirror and lazy embeds
 *  render only what is in view). */
async function scrollThrough(page: Page, selector: string) {
  const el = page.locator(selector).first();
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
    const done = await el.evaluate((e) => {
      e.scrollTop += e.clientHeight * 0.8;
      return e.scrollTop + e.clientHeight >= e.scrollHeight - 1;
    });
    await page.waitForTimeout(60);
    if (done) break;
  }
}

async function openRemotePage(page: Page, mode: string) {
  const seen = await watchNetwork(page);
  await page.addInitScript(installIpcMock, { pages: PAGES, editorMode: mode });
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^vault/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole('treeitem', { name: 'remote' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'remote', exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  return seen;
}

test('the interceptor records a request that tries to leave', async ({ page }) => {
  const { outside } = await watchNetwork(page);
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = 'https://remote.example/probe';
    a.click();
  });
  await expect.poll(() => outside).toEqual(['https://remote.example/probe']);
});

for (const mode of ['livePreview', 'source', 'split']) {
  test(`zero network requests: ${mode}`, async ({ page }) => {
    const { outside, problems } = await openRemotePage(page, mode);
    if (mode === 'split') {
      const pane = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Preview' });
      await expect(pane.getByText('Remote image blocked (privacy)').first()).toBeVisible();
      await scrollThrough(page, '[role="region"][aria-label="Preview"]');
      await expect(pane.getByRole('region', { name: 'Other › Section' })).toBeVisible();
    }
    if (mode === 'livePreview') {
      await expect(page.locator('.cm-liber-widget').first()).toBeVisible();
    }
    await scrollThrough(page, '.cm-scroller');
    await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
    await page.waitForTimeout(500); // debounced renders, lazy embeds
    const csp = await page.evaluate(
      () => (window as unknown as { __liberCspViolations: string[] }).__liberCspViolations,
    );
    expect(problems).toEqual([]);
    expect(csp).toEqual([]); // nothing even reached the CSP
    expect(outside).toEqual([]);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the hand-off**

Create `docs/handoff/2026-10-12-m2a-editor-handoff.md`:

```markdown
# M2a — Editor: Hand-off

Written for whoever continues by hand. It says what exists, where it lives, how
to run and check it, what was decided on your behalf, and what still needs a
person in front of the real window.

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-m2a-editor-design.md` ·
Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-m2a-editor.md`

---

## 1. Status at a glance

| Area | State |
|---|---|
| Markdown model (`src/markdown/`): parse, extensions, frontmatter, sanitizer, incremental re-parse | done, tested (unit + TS/Rust parity fixtures + seeded fuzz vs full parse) |
| Backend: `page_read`, `page_write` (CAS + conflict payload), `page_create_conflict_copy`, `links_resolve`, `url_open`, `file_open_external` | done, async, ACL-tested |
| `liber-asset:` protocol (vault files for `<img>/<audio>`) | done, tested (types, traversal, hidden files, ranges, no vault) |
| Page documents: autosave 500 ms, retry 1 s→30 s, 3-way merge, conflict copies, rename follow, deleted-while-dirty, quit flush | done, tested |
| Editor: Live Preview / Source / Split, scroll sync, ⌘-click / Mod-Enter links | done, tested in jsdom |
| Rendered pane: all node types, embeds, callouts, columns, privacy placeholders | done, tested (+ axe) |
| Page header: breadcrumb, cover (read-only), mode toggle, save status | done, tested (+ axe) |
| M1b polish a–f, j | done, tested |
| Zero-network e2e (Playwright, production CSP, frozen prototypes) | done, mutation-checked |
| Real-window checks (§9) | **need you** |

Totals at the end: 253 vitest tests (38 files), 4 Playwright tests, 176 cargo
tests; eslint, prettier, tsc, i18n parity, clippy `-D warnings`, rustfmt and
`cargo deny` all clean.

## 2. How to run

| What | Command (repo root) |
|---|---|
| Everything (the CI gate) | `pnpm check` |
| UI unit tests | `pnpm test` |
| Zero-network test | `pnpm --filter @liber/desktop exec playwright install chromium` once, then `pnpm test:e2e` |
| Rust | `pnpm build:ui && cargo test --workspace` |
| App (dev) | `pnpm dev` |

`cargo test -p liber-desktop` needs `apps/desktop/dist` (run `pnpm build:ui`
first). `LIBER_TRASH_TESTS=1` stays for CI only (it uses the real OS Trash).

## 3. Backend map (`apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/`)

| File | What it does |
|---|---|
| `editor_commands.rs` | The six M2a commands. Each is `*_impl(&AppState, …)` plus an `async` wrapper through `vault_commands::blocking` (never on the UI thread). |
| `asset.rs` | `liber-asset:` — `serve(&AppState, uri_path, range)` resolves through the current session's `VaultPath` (no `..`, no absolute paths, no symlink escapes), refuses hidden files and the root (403), 404 without a vault, `Range` → 206/416. |
| `lib.rs` | Registers the opener plugin (Rust API only), the protocol (on a thread) and the commands. |
| `error.rs` | `ErrorKind::{Binary, InvalidUrl}`; `CommandError.conflict` flattens `{currentText, currentHash}` into a `conflict` error. |
| `settings.rs` | `editorMode: livePreview \| source \| split` (unknown value → default + warning). |
| `vault_commands.rs` | `vault_open` no longer fails when recents can't be saved: the failure becomes an index warning. |
| `crates/liber-session/src/editing.rs` | `write_page` (CAS + reindex + resolve), `create_conflict_copy` (`<stem> (conflict).md`, `(conflict 2)`…), `resolve_links`. |

Permissions (`capabilities/main.json`): `allow-page-read`, `allow-page-write`,
`allow-page-create-conflict-copy`, `allow-links-resolve`, `allow-url-open`,
`allow-file-open-external`, `core:window:allow-destroy` (the UI closes the
window itself after saving). No `opener:*` permission: the webview cannot call
the opener plugin; `plugin:opener|open_url` is "not allowed" (tested).

CSP: `img-src`/`media-src` allow `liber-asset: http://liber-asset.localhost`
(instead of Tauri's `asset:`); nothing else changed.

## 4. Frontend map (`apps/desktop/src/`)

| Path | What it does |
|---|---|
| `markdown/parse.ts` | `parsePage(text)` → `ParsedPage {tree, frontmatter, links, tags, comments}`; `parseTree`, `finishPage` for the incremental path. |
| `markdown/incremental.ts` | `reparse(prev, prevText, text)`: re-parses only the touched top-level blocks (± one neighbour) when they are blank-line separated; anything non-local (fences, HTML, comments, definitions, directives, frontmatter, adjoining lists) → full parse. Takes ownership of `prev`. |
| `markdown/blocks.ts`, `inline.ts`, `wikilink.ts`, `frontmatter.ts` | Extensions: callouts, columns, `%%` comments; wikilinks, embeds, highlights, tags. |
| `markdown/sanitize.ts` | `toSafeHast(page, {sourcePath})`: mdast → hast → raw HTML parsed → `hast-util-sanitize` allowlist → URL policy (remote media → `remote-blocked`, vault paths → `dataAssetPath`, escaping paths → `missing`, links classified) → heading ids. |
| `markdown/section.ts` | `sectionOf(tree, heading)` for `![[Note#Heading]]`. |
| `features/editor/pageDoc.ts` | `PageDoc`: one open page — text, merge base, hash, save loop, merge, conflict copy, orphaning, Save as new. |
| `features/editor/merge.ts`, `eol.ts` | `merge3` (node-diff3, line based); CRLF kept on disk, LF in the editor. |
| `features/editor/Editor.tsx` | The CodeMirror view; per-page `EditorState` (undo survives page and mode switches); external text applied outside history; Split pane + scroll sync. |
| `features/editor/livePreview.ts`, `widgets.tsx` | Decorations from the model; block widgets render `<Preview embedded>`; checkboxes toggle the source; reduced set above 200 KB; ⌘-click / Mod-Enter follow links. |
| `features/preview/` | `Preview` (debounced 150 ms, incremental parse, memoized body), `renderHast`, `Embed`, `Callout`, `useResolved`. |
| `features/page/` | `PageView`, `PageHeader`, `Breadcrumb`, `ModeToggle`. |
| `features/dialogs/LinkDialogs.tsx` | Create page / open in browser / open in default app confirmations. |
| `stores/editorStore.ts` | Current + background `PageDoc`s, conflict toast with "Open", `saveAll`/`reset`, quit guard. |
| `stores/navigate.ts`, `stores/links.ts` | `openPage(path, heading)`, `openNewPage`; `followLink(action)`. |

## 5. Flows

- **Typing:** CodeMirror update → `PageDoc.edit` → 500 ms (at once when the editor loses focus) → `page_write(path, text, expectedHash)` → new hash → `pageStore.refresh()` (header) because the watcher does not report own writes.
- **External change:** watcher → `vault:changed` → `vaultStore` (100 ms coalesce) → `pageStore.onExternalChange` → `editorStore.onExternalChange` → `PageDoc.sync()` → reload (clean) / merge / conflict copy → `doc.sink` applies the text to CodeMirror synchronously, outside the undo history.
- **Write conflict:** `page_write` → `conflict {currentText, currentHash}` → same merge path, no extra read.
- **Vault switch / close:** `editorStore.saveAll()` first; if a page still can't be written, the switch is refused with a toast (text never dropped).
- **Quit:** `tauri://close-requested` → `flushAll(2000)` → `window.destroy()`.
- **Links:** pane/widget click or ⌘-click/Mod-Enter → `followLink` → open page (+ heading) · create-page dialog · external-link dialog → `url_open` · file dialog → `file_open_external`.

## 6. Recipes

- **New Markdown extension:** add the node type to `markdown/types.ts`, produce it in `blocks.ts`/`inline.ts`, render it in `sanitize.ts` handlers + `features/preview/renderHast.tsx`, decorate it in `livePreview.ts`; if it has non-local reach, add its marker to `NONLOCAL` in `incremental.ts`; add a parity fixture if Rust indexes it.
- **New backend command:** follow M1b hand-off §6 (build.rs list, capability, `*_impl` + async wrapper, ACL test in `tests/commands.rs`).

## 7. Tests inventory (new in M2a)

| File | Covers |
|---|---|
| `src/markdown/*.test.ts` | parse, blocks, sanitizer, section, incremental (fuzz + 5,000-line < 50 ms), parity with Rust |
| `src/features/editor/*.test.ts(x)` | PageDoc, merge/EOL, Live Preview, Editor, scroll sync |
| `src/features/preview/Preview.test.tsx` | node types, links, embeds, remote placeholders, axe |
| `src/features/page/*.test.tsx` | header, breadcrumb, modes, save status, orphan, axe |
| `src/stores/editorStore.test.tsx` | page switch mid-save, background retry, rename follow, delete, conflict toast, quit flush |
| `src/features/shell/Polish.test.tsx` | M1b polish a–f |
| `src/theme/palette.test.ts` | palette contrast ≥ 4.5:1 in light and dark |
| `tests/syntax-target.test.ts` | lint rule: no regex lookbehind |
| `e2e/zero-network.spec.ts` | zero requests + zero CSP violations, three modes |
| `src-tauri/tests/{commands,asset,settings}.rs`, `crates/liber-session/tests/editing.rs`, `crates/liber-markdown/tests/parity.rs` | backend |

## 8. Rulings made on your behalf (deviations from the spec)

1. **Own `liber-asset:` protocol instead of Tauri's asset protocol + scope.** Tauri's scope can only add directories (no revoke), so a previous vault would stay readable. The protocol resolves every request through the current session instead. Spec §2.5/§7 "scope set on open and revoked on close" is met by construction and tested ("no vault → 404").
2. **Opening URLs/files through our own commands** (`url_open`, `file_open_external`, scheme-checked in Rust) instead of granting `opener:allow-open-url` to the webview. Smaller attack surface; same behaviour.
3. **`hast-util-sanitize` instead of DOMPurify:** it works on the tree, so source positions survive (Split scroll sync, M4 dragging). `dompurify` is not a dependency.
4. **Container directives only** (`:::name`), not the text form, which would turn `3:2` into a directive.
5. **Incremental re-parse** (`markdown/incremental.ts`): a full remark parse of a 5,000-line note takes ~110 ms, so the spec's "< 50 ms" needs it. Equivalence with the full parse is fuzz-tested.
6. **Switching or closing the vault with a page that can't be saved is refused** (toast), so text is never dropped.
7. **`core:window:allow-destroy`** added for the save-before-quit flow (Tauri's `onCloseRequested` destroys the window itself).
8. **No regex lookbehind** (lint rule): the build targets Safari 15 (WKWebView, macOS 12).
9. Versions: `@codemirror/language` 6.13.1, `@codemirror/view` 6.43.14 (pnpm's release-age gate resolved them); `@playwright/test` 1.64.0.

## 9. Needs the real window (you)

Agent screenshots must be launch-only (no synthetic keystrokes). Please check:

1. Typing feel in Live Preview (cursor never jumps; markup appears on the cursor line only).
2. IME input (e.g. Japanese/accents with dead keys) in Live Preview and Source.
3. Mode toggle; Split scroll sync both ways.
4. Edit the open page in another editor while typing (merge; overlapping edit → conflict copy toast → "Open").
5. Breadcrumb: folder segment reveals and focuses the folder; vault segment collapses the tree.
6. ⌘-click a link; Mod-Enter; unresolved link → Create page; external link → browser after confirmation.
7. A page with remote images: "Remote image blocked (privacy)" placeholder.
8. Images and audio from the vault (`liber-asset:`) play/show; audio seeking (ranges).
9. Quit with unsaved text (⌘Q right after typing): the text is on disk.

## 10. Known gaps / deferred

| # | Gap | Target |
|---|---|---|
| a | Raw HTML blocks show as source in Live Preview (rendered in Split) | M2b |
| b | Large notes (> 200 KB) also re-parse fully if they contain fences/HTML/comments | later perf |
| c | Mixed line endings in one file are normalised to the first one on save | later |
| d | Cover editing, attachments, `[[` autocomplete | M2b |
| e | Header title updates after each save, not per keystroke | later |
| f | One giant paragraph (no blank lines) is slow to parse: a 200 KB single paragraph takes ~1.4 s to parse and ~2.1 s to open in Live Preview (jsdom), against ~0.35 s for 200 KB of normal paragraphs | later perf |
| g | On a busy machine the default vitest worker count can hit worker-start timeouts (CPU time stays ~40 s while wall time grew to 270 s); `pnpm exec vitest run --maxWorkers=2` is a workaround | watch in CI |

## 11. Troubleshooting

| Symptom | Fix |
|---|---|
| Images don't show | `tauri.conf.json` CSP must list `liber-asset: http://liber-asset.localhost` in `img-src`/`media-src` |
| "not allowed" on a new command | `build.rs` list + `capabilities/main.json` + ACL test |
| `pnpm test:e2e` can't find a browser | `pnpm --filter @liber/desktop exec playwright install chromium` |
| e2e fails with `csp:`/violations | something reached the DOM with a remote URL: the sanitizer, not the CSP, must stop it |

## 12. Next steps

1. Your real-window pass (§9); note anything odd in a new hand-off section.
2. **M2b:** covers/subtitle editing, paste/drop attachments, `[[` autocomplete.
3. M3 Global View (reuse `Breadcrumb`).
```
